# Provenance: how each crest is constructed

Every drawing is made for this package and is MIT. The geometric crests are struck from their construction with compass and ruler.
The organic crests whose source is given below are vectorised from a public-domain period crest book: the page was read at the
National Diet Library, the crest's outline drawing was filled, traced to curves, cleaned of print noise, and made exactly symmetric
by keeping one half (or one leaf) and mirroring or turning it; no scan, crop or picture of the book is in the package or the
repository. This is the one-line rule each crest is set out by, in English and in Japanese, and the page it was drawn from.
Written by `pnpm data`; do not edit it by hand.

## Rings

The weight of a ring and the white inside it are measured from fifteen ringed crests drawn in the usual way (fourteen in a sheet of
ringed crests and one ringed chrysanthemum, the reference pictures the package was checked against): the ring is 0.079 to 0.095 of
the crest's diameter, 0.087 at the median; the white between ring and motif is 0.011 to 0.029 of the diameter, 0.018 at the median;
the motif reaches 0.77 to 0.80 of the outer radius. So a medium ring is 16.8 on the 192 of the layout circle, the white inside it 3.5,
and a ringed motif is drawn to the ring's inside less that white. A thin ring is a thirtieth of the diameter.

## Three hollyhock leaves · 三つ葉葵 (`mitsuba-aoi`)

Three heart-shaped leaves a third of a turn apart, each with its point at the centre and its two round lobes outside, white stems between them. Every edge is an arc of a compass circle or a ruled line: each lobe is one circle, its straight side a line through two grid points, and its veins are white arcs 1.7 wide, every one bowed. In each half of a leaf seven veins fan out from the curl beside the notch to the edge (the last leaves the midrib), each bowing towards the notch; from each of the lower six a branch bows the other way and ends where the vein before it ends. So the black cell between two veins is a lens, pointed at the curl and at the edge, with a smaller pointed cell between each pair of tips. Between the two lobes a black spindle runs up the leaf's axis from near the centre and ends in a point at the notch; beside it the outline rolls into a curl with a hook.

心形の葉を三枚、120度ずつ離し、葉先を中心に、二つの丸い葉の耳を外側に向けて置き、葉の間を白い茎で分けます。すべての縁はコンパスの円の弧か、定規の直線です。葉の耳は一つの円、まっすぐな辺は二つの格子点を通る直線、葉脈は幅1.7の白い弧で、まっすぐな線は一本もありません。葉の半分ごとに、切れ込みの横の渦から縁へ、七本の白い葉脈が扇のように広がります（最後の一本は中央の葉脈から出ます）。どれも切れ込みの方へふくらみます。下の六本からは枝が一本ずつ出て、反対の方へふくらみ、一つ前の葉脈が縁に届くところで終わります。そのため、葉脈の間の黒い部分は、渦の側と縁の側がとがり、真ん中が太い、木の葉の形になります。となり合う先と先の間には、小さなとがった形が入ります。二つの耳の間には、中心近くから葉の軸に沿って黒い紡錘形が伸び、切れ込みで尖って終わります。その横で、葉の輪郭が鉤のついた渦に巻き込みます。

Measured: The leaf's veins only, from the 三ツあふひ of the 1892 crest book (frame 37, right page, row 1, first column from the right), checked against the single large leaf of its 一ツつるあふひ (frame 36, left page, row 2, third column from the right); counts and angles only. In each half of a leaf the book draws seven pointed cells that fan from the curl beside the notch to the edge, every line bowed, their tips about 2, 16, 36, 52, 68, 86 and 104 degrees round from the leaf's axis as seen from the curl; in the large leaf each tip is where a line from the curl meets a shorter line that branches off the next. Ours has seven veins that end 2, 16, 36, 52, 69, 88 and 109 degrees round, and six branches. The book draws black lines on white, ours white veins on black, so the book's short cells between the long ones are the pointed cells between a branch and its vein here. The book's three leaves have their notches at a centre circle and their tips outwards; neither it nor the 1891 book (NDL 780551) has a cell with the tips at the centre. The arrangement, the leaf's outline, its spindle and its curls are not from the book: they were measured from a picture of the ringed crest. Nothing is taken from Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 37, right page, first row, first column from the right: 三ツあふひ (the leaf's veins only: measured, and drawn with compass and ruler; the book draws its three leaves with their tips outwards) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「三ツあふひ」（葉脈だけ。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの。本の図は、三枚の葉先が外を向きます）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

63 guides, 9 shapes, 120 steps.

## Three hollyhock leaves in a circle · 丸に三つ葉葵 (`maru-ni-mitsuba-aoi`)

The three hollyhock leaves inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick (100 and 81 about the centre). Each leaf's black spindle widens above the notch into a wedge that joins the ring, with a white horn either side of it between the lobe and the ring.

三つ葉葵を、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪（中心から100と81の円）の中に収めます。各葉の黒い紡錘形は、切れ込みの上でくさび形に広がって輪につながり、その両側、葉の耳と輪の間に白い角の形が残ります。

Measured: The leaf's veins only, from the 三ツあふひ of the 1892 crest book (frame 37, right page, row 1, first column from the right), checked against the single large leaf of its 一ツつるあふひ (frame 36, left page, row 2, third column from the right); counts and angles only. In each half of a leaf the book draws seven pointed cells that fan from the curl beside the notch to the edge, every line bowed, their tips about 2, 16, 36, 52, 68, 86 and 104 degrees round from the leaf's axis as seen from the curl; in the large leaf each tip is where a line from the curl meets a shorter line that branches off the next. Ours has seven veins that end 2, 16, 36, 52, 69, 88 and 109 degrees round, and six branches. The book draws black lines on white, ours white veins on black, so the book's short cells between the long ones are the pointed cells between a branch and its vein here. The book's three leaves have their notches at a centre circle and their tips outwards; neither it nor the 1891 book (NDL 780551) has a cell with the tips at the centre. The arrangement, the leaf's outline, its spindle and its curls are not from the book: they were measured from a picture of the ringed crest. Nothing is taken from Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 37, right page, first row, first column from the right: 三ツあふひ (the leaf's veins only: measured, and drawn with compass and ruler; the book draws its three leaves with their tips outwards) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「三ツあふひ」（葉脈だけ。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの。本の図は、三枚の葉先が外を向きます）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

67 guides, 10 shapes, 128 steps.

## Standing hollyhock · 立ち葵 (`tachi-aoi`)

Three hollyhock leaves on stems that rise from one foot: one leaf upright at the top and one a quarter turn to each side, their tips on the layout circle and their notches towards the centre. The leaf is the three-leaf hollyhock's, with its white veins and its curls. A stem 6 wide comes in between a leaf's two lobes and runs on as the black spindle. The top leaf's stem goes straight down to the base. The left branch leaves its leaf between two arcs and joins the upright stem; the right branch passes behind it, so a white line 3 wide parts the two all the way down. Below the branches the trunk flares into a foot that stands on a level line, each side an arc of a circle 100 wide. The top leaf is built first, then the right leaf and the left leaf where they stand, clockwise, and the stems last.

葵の葉を三枚、一つの根元から立ち上がる茎につけます。一枚は上にまっすぐ立て、残りの二枚は左右に90度ずつ回して置きます。葉先は三枚とも外の円の上にあり、切れ込みは中心を向きます。葉は三つ葉葵と同じで、白い葉脈と渦があります。幅6の茎が二つの耳の間から入り、そのまま黒い紡錘形につながります。上の葉の茎は、根元までまっすぐ下ります。左の枝は、二つの弧にはさまれて葉から出て、まっすぐな茎に合わさります。右の枝は、まっすぐな茎の後ろを通るので、二つの間に幅3の白い線が根元まで残ります。枝の下で幹は左右に広がり、水平な線の上に立つ根元になります。広がりの両側は、幅100の円の弧です。上の葉を作図し、右の葉、左の葉の順に、時計回りにその場で作図して、最後に茎を作図します。

Measured: The 立あふひ of the 1892 crest book (frame 36, left page, row 3, column 3 from the right) and a Wikimedia Commons picture of 立ち葵 (Japanese crest Tachi Aoi.svg, public domain), measured as numbers only, in the unit of the hollyhock leaf (the leaf is 99.4 wide and 68.4 long). In the Commons picture the top leaf's tip stands 117 from the point the three leaves turn about and the side leaves' tips 114; the stems are 6 wide; a white line 3 wide parts the upright stem from the right branch; the trunk is 26 wide at its narrowest, and the foot is 92 wide on a base 96 below that point. The book draws the same arrangement by hand and in outline, with the side leaves tilted upwards, rounder leaves with a small curl each side of the stem, and the same white line to the right of the upright stem. Ours keeps the package's hollyhock leaf (straight sides, and large round shoulders at the notch where both pictures have small curls), sets the side leaves level as the Commons picture does, and makes them the top leaf a quarter turn about the centre, 115 out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, left page, third row, third column counted from the right: 立あふひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「立あふひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

76 guides, 11 shapes, 142 steps.

## Standing hollyhock in a circle · 丸に立ち葵 (`maru-ni-tachi-aoi`)

The standing hollyhock inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick. The leaves stand closer to the centre than in the crest on its own and the branches are shorter, so that the three leaves fill the ring. The white line between the upright stem and the right branch is 4 wide. The left branch's upper edge touches the upright stem, so the white between the two closes to a point.

立ち葵を、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に収めます。輪のない立ち葵より葉を中心に寄せ、枝を短くして、三枚の葉で輪の中を埋めます。まっすぐな茎と右の枝の間の白い線は、幅4です。左の枝の上の縁は、まっすぐな茎に接します。そのため、二つの間の白は一点で閉じます。

Measured: The 丸に左りはなれ葵 of the 1892 crest book (frame 36, right page, row 4, column 4) and a Wikimedia Commons picture of the Honda house's crest (Japanese crest Honda Tachi Aoi.svg, public domain), measured as numbers only, in the unit of the hollyhock leaf. In the Commons picture the ring is 0.086 of the diameter thick; the tips stand 104 from the centre, 0.75 of the radius; the foot's base is 92 below the centre; the stems are 7 wide and the white line beside the upright stem 4; the left branch's upper edge runs into the upright stem without a corner. Both pictures draw the leaves larger for the ring and closer to the centre than in the crest on its own. The book names its cell a hollyhock in a circle with the left one apart and draws a thin double ring; the Commons picture is the same drawing in a thick ring, named the Honda standing hollyhock. Ours has the ring of the package's ringed crests, and its leaves reach 0.79 of the radius.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, right page, fourth row, fourth column: 丸に左りはなれ葵 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目の四列目「丸に左りはなれ葵」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

77 guides, 12 shapes, 145 steps.

## Hemp leaf · 麻の葉 (`asa-no-ha`)

A six-pointed star, with a thin white line from the centre to each point and to each notch, so that it is twelve kites.

六つの先を持つ星形に、中心から各先と各切れ込みへ細い白線を引き、十二の凧形に分けます。

32 guides, 1 shapes, 39 steps.

## Hemp leaf in a circle · 丸に麻の葉 (`maru-ni-asa-no-ha`)

The six-pointed hemp-leaf star inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its points a narrow white line from it.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、六つの先を持つ麻の葉を収め、先を輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 35, right page, third row, first column counted from the right: 丸に麻の葉 (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「丸に麻の葉」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

34 guides, 2 shapes, 45 steps.

## Three ginkgo leaves · 三つ銀杏 (`mitsu-icho`)

Three fan-shaped leaves a third of a turn apart round a hub, filling a circle. Thirty equal small circles on one circle make the wavy edge. Between the leaves, three white lenses run from the hub to the edge, each two equal arcs. Each leaf has a ruled middle vein, an arc vein each side of it, and two small curls where its edge rolls into a loop.

扇形の葉を三枚、芯の円のまわりに120度ずつ離して置き、全体を円に収めます。一つの円周上に同じ大きさの小円を三十並べて、波打つ縁を作ります。葉の間には、同じ半径の二つの弧でできた白いレンズ形を、芯から縁まで三つ通します。各葉には、定規で引いた中央の葉脈、その左右に弧の葉脈、縁が小さな輪に巻き込む渦を二つ入れます。

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 5, left page, second row, fourth column counted from the right: 三ついてふ (measured, and drawn afresh with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「三ついてふ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描き直したもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

40 guides, 4 shapes, 57 steps.

## Hexagonal ginkgo · 六角銀杏 (`rokkaku-icho`)

Three ginkgo fans and a round heart form a gently scalloped hexagon.

三枚の銀杏と丸い中心で、縁に小さなふくらみのある六角を作ります。

Measured: Three pale curved divisions meet alternating corners of the six-sided perimeter.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 5, left page, fourth row, fourth column from the right: 六角いてふ (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「六角いてふ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

91 guides, 2 shapes, 107 steps.

## Five ginkgo leaves · 五つ銀杏 (`itsutsu-icho`)

Five scalloped ginkgo leaves look inward along pale curves to a round heart.

ふくらみのある五枚の銀杏を、白い弧に沿って丸い中心へ向けます。

Measured: Five open lenses, small cut curls and paired veins are spaced equally about the heart.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 5, left page, second row, second column from the right: 五ッいてふ (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「五ッいてふ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

119 guides, 2 shapes, 135 steps.

## Three ginkgo leaves in a circle · 丸に三つ銀杏 (`maru-ni-mitsu-icho`)

The three ginkgo leaves inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white line between the ring and their wavy edge.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に三つ銀杏を収め、輪と波打つ縁の間に細い白線を残します。

42 guides, 5 shapes, 63 steps.

## Plum bowl · 梅鉢 (`ume-bachi`)

Five equal discs on a circle round the centre, each a little apart from its neighbours, and in the middle a plain disc three quarters of one of them across.

同じ大きさの円を五つ、中心のまわりの円の上に、隣どうしが少し離れるように並べます。中央には、そのうちの一つの4分の3ほどの大きさの無地の円を置きます。

Measured: The plain 梅鉢 of the 1892 crest book (frame 24, row 3, column 3 from the left of the left page), its six circles fitted by least squares: the five discs of radius 50.4 on a circle of radius 98.1 and a middle disc of radius 38.3, so in a reach of 100 the discs are 34 on 66 and the middle disc 26.

10 guides, 6 shapes, 22 steps.

## Outlined twisting plum blossom · 陰捻じ梅 (`kage-neji-ume`)

Five open petals turn in one direction around a round heart. Two arcs and a short hooked stamen mark each petal.

丸い中心のまわりで五枚の花びらを同じ向きに回します。各花びらに二本の円弧と先の曲がった短い雄しべを置きます。

Measured: The outer petal rims and inner echoes share their compass circles. The five hooked stamens follow the same turn.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 25, right page, first row, fourth column from the right: かげ捻ぢむめ (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「かげ捻ぢむめ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

32 guides, 11 shapes, 62 steps.

## Twisted front-facing plum blossom · 向こう捻じ梅 (`muko-neji-ume`)

Five rounded petals turn about a small disc. Curved white lines part them, and twenty fine stamens curve in the same direction at even intervals around the disc.

小さな丸い中心のまわりに、五枚の丸い花びらを同じ向きにひねって並べます。曲がった白い線で花びらを分け、中心のまわりに、同じ向きに曲がる細いしべを二十本、等間隔に入れます。

Measured: Frame 25: the outline has five rounded lobes with a turn in each of the five seams. Twenty curved stamens with slightly enlarged tips are spaced evenly around the disc. The heart is a little under a quarter of the outer radius across.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 25, right page, fourth row, third column from the right: むかふねぢ梅 (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「むかふねぢ梅」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

78 guides, 2 shapes, 93 steps.

## Calyx plum bowl · うてな梅鉢 (`utena-umebachi`)

Five separate oval petals lie around the star of the calyx. Four tangent arcs make each oval, with a shorter oval in the lower pair. A short curved stalk leaves the calyx below.

星形のがくのまわりに、離れた楕円形の花びらを五枚並べます。花びらは接する四つの円弧で作図し、下の二枚を短くします。がくの下から短い曲がった柄を伸ばします。

Measured: Frame 24: the lower two petals are rounder and reach a little less far from the calyx. Each has most of its width halfway to the tip. The short stalk curves down below the irregular five-point calyx.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, left page, fourth row, second column from the right: うてなむめばち (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「うてなむめばち」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

22 guides, 6 shapes, 37 steps.

## Three back-view plums drawn together · 三つ寄せ裏梅 (`mitsu-yose-ura-ume`)

Three back-view plums turn towards one another. Five rounded petals, a white calyx and a short curved stalk make each flower.

裏から見た梅を三つ、互いに向けて置きます。丸い花びら五枚、白いがく、短く曲がった柄で、一つの梅を作ります。

Measured: Frame 25: the three short stalks point towards the centre of the group. The five petals of each flower retain thin white internal outlines, and the narrow white cuts in the crown are spaced evenly.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 25, right page, fourth row, second column from the right: 三ッよせうら梅 (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「三ッよせうら梅」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

61 guides, 3 shapes, 80 steps.

## Three clustered plum blossoms · 三つ盛り梅 (`mitsumori-ume`)

Three upright plums stand on a triangle. Each has a crown of three rounded lobes, two smaller petals below, and nine white rays that open like a fan.

三つの梅を、どれも上向きのまま三角形の頂点に置きます。梅は一つずつ、上に三つの丸いふくらみ、下に二枚の小さな花びらを置き、細い白いしべを扇状に九本広げます。

Measured: Frame 24: the three flowers stay upright, even at the lower corners. The lower paired petals are less than half as broad as the three upper lobes together. The rays spread out over the lower part of the crown.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, left page, fourth row, fourth column from the right: 三ッ盛むめ (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「三ッ盛むめ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

66 guides, 3 shapes, 79 steps.

## Peeping plum bowl · 覗き梅鉢 (`nozoki-umebachi`)

Three petals peek up above the lower rim. The middle petal is a disc and the next two are clipped by the circle inside the ring. Only the top of the heart and a few short rays show.

輪の下の縁から花びらを三枚のぞかせます。中央の花びらは円で、両脇の花びらは輪の内側の円で切ります。丸い中心の上端と短いしべだけを見せます。

Measured: Frame 24: the middle disc is about one third of the inside diameter and its centre lies just under two fifths of the radius below the middle of the ring. The two neighbouring discs rise to near the middle disc’s centre line and stand a fine gap away. Short rays and the upper edge of the heart are visible.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, left page, second row, third column from the right: のぞきむめばち (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「のぞきむめばち」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 4 shapes, 35 steps.

## Double front-facing plum blossom · 八重向こう梅 (`yae-muko-ume`)

Five rounded lobes form the face of the plum. The rounded tips of a second row show between them. Thirty fine white stamens, each tipped with a dot, radiate from the small round heart.

五つの丸いふくらみで梅の花の正面を形作り、その間から二重目の丸い花びらの先をのぞかせます。小さな丸い中心から、先に点のある細い白いしべを三十本放射状に伸ばします。

Measured: Frame 24: the rear lobes show only at the notches between the large front lobes. The fine rays are regularised to thirty equal intervals, and their round tips stand on a circle just over half the main reach.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, left page, first row, fourth column from the right: 八重むかふ梅 (左頁、一段目、右から四列目「八重むかふ梅」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

80 guides, 3 shapes, 97 steps.

## Double plum blossom · 八重梅 (`yae-ume`)

Five broad petals crowd around a round heart. A short white stamen rises inside each one. Between the outer shoulders, the tops of five round petals show from behind.

丸い中心を幅広い花びら五枚で囲み、それぞれに短い白いしべを入れます。花びらの肩と肩の間から、奥の丸い花びら五枚の先を見せます。

Measured: Frame 24: the five outer petals rise to about the same height. Their crowns lie on circles of radius 43, struck 57 from the centre in a reach of 100. The rounded rear petals alternate with the front petals and show just below their tips.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, left page, second row, fourth column from the right: 八重むめ (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「八重むめ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

41 guides, 11 shapes, 64 steps.

## Plum bowl in a circle · 丸に梅鉢 (`maru-ni-ume-bachi`)

The plum bowl inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に梅鉢を収めます。

11 guides, 7 shapes, 26 steps.

## Quiver plum blossom in middle shadow · 中陰箙梅 (`chu-kage-ebira-ume`)

Three double-edged lobes sit under a shallow fan of stamens. The calyx is open and the stalk turns down to one side.

二重の輪郭の花びら三枚の上に、浅い扇形に雄しべを広げます。開いたがくから茎を下へ、片側へ曲げます。

Measured: The side lobes are even compass circles by reflection. A smooth upper arc surrounds the fan of round-tipped stamens.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 25, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 中かげゑびら梅 (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「中かげゑびら梅」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

97 guides, 3 shapes, 124 steps.

## Kaga plum bowl · 加賀梅鉢 (`kaga-umebachi`)

Five equal discs on a circle round the centre, and in the middle a disc with ten short blocks round it that widen outwards, the five that point between the petals the longer.

同じ大きさの円を五つ、中心のまわりの円の上に並べます。中央に円を置き、そのまわりに、外へ向かって広がる短い台形を十個並べます。そのうち、花びらの間に向く五つは、ほかの五つより長くします。

Measured: The 加賀梅鉢 of the 1892 crest book (frame 24, row 2, column 3 from the left of the left page), its circles fitted by least squares: the five discs of radius 50.8 on a circle of radius 102.6, a middle disc of radius 20.5, so in a reach of 100 the discs are 33 on 67 and the middle disc 13; the longer blocks of the sunburst reach 38 and the shorter 32, from 16 out. The book draws the blocks as pointed fans in outline, drawn here as flat-ended blocks.

57 guides, 16 shapes, 79 steps.

## Standing water plantain · 立ち沢瀉 (`omodaka`)

A leaf of three pointed lobes of one size about a small ring at the centre: one upright, and one down to each side, 45 degrees from straight down. Each lobe is the lens between two arcs of one radius, with a white vein from the ring towards its tip. The upright lobe is built in full, and the other two are built the same way where they stand. A spray of five three-petalled flowers stands each side of the leaf, and three stems hang under it: the middle one straight and thickest, the outer two thin and bowed outward.

真ん中の小さな輪から、先のとがった同じ大きさの裂片を三つ出します。一つはまっすぐ上、あとの二つは左右の下向きで、真下から45度ずつ開きます。裂片はどれも、同じ半径の二つの弧にはさまれた形で、輪から裂片の先へ白い葉脈を一本通します。上の裂片を先に最後まで作図し、あとの二つは、同じ作図をそれぞれ立つ場所でそのまま行います。葉の左右に、花びら三枚の花を五つ付けた枝を一本ずつ立てます。葉の下には茎を三本垂らします。真ん中の茎はまっすぐで一番太く、外の二本は細く、外へ弓なりに曲がります。

Measured: The standing water plantain of the 1892 crest book (frame 14, 立ちおもだか), with two present-day drawings of it and of the ringed crest for comparison, in units of a lobe's length from the centre ring to its tip. The three lobes are one length in all three. The upright lobe is 0.55 to 0.58 as wide as it is long, widest a little over a third of the way up, and each edge is close to one arc of 0.87. The lower lobes stand 49 to 53 degrees from straight down in the book and 41 to 45 in the present-day drawings; here they are 45. The ring at the centre is 0.07 across, the white round it and the white veins about 0.02 wide. The middle stem is 0.06 wide, the outer two 0.04 and bowed outward. In the book and in the present-day drawings the lower lobes bend outward a little and are fuller above the vein than below it; here each is the upright lobe turned.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, right page, second row, third column counted from the right: 立ちおもだか (measured, and drawn afresh with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「立ちおもだか」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描き直したもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

43 guides, 9 shapes, 65 steps.

## Water plantain in a thin ring · 糸輪に沢瀉 (`itowa-ni-omodaka`)

The standing water plantain inside a thin ring, a narrow white line between them.

細い輪の内側に、立ち沢瀉を収め、輪とのあいだに細い白線を残します。

Measured: The 糸ワニおもだか of the 1892 crest book (frame 14, right page, row 1, column 3 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the ring's outer radius (96): the standing water plantain, drawn as 立ち沢瀉 is, inside one thin ring; the leaf's tip and the foot of its stems reach 83 (read 82.9 and 81.6) and the two sprays stand 74 to 80 out. The ring is the package's thin ring, 6.4 wide, where the book's is a single thin line; the book's leaf is slightly narrower across its lower lobes than ours.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, right page, first row, third column counted from the right: 糸ワニおもだか (the standing water plantain in a thin ring; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「糸ワニおもだか」（細い輪に入った立ち沢瀉。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

45 guides, 10 shapes, 71 steps.

## Standing water plantain in a circle · 丸に立ち沢瀉 (`maru-ni-omodaka`)

The standing water plantain inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に立ち沢瀉を収めます。

Measured: The ringed water plantain of the 1892 crest book (frame 14, 丸ニふと沢瀉) and a present-day drawing of 丸に立ち沢瀉 for comparison, measured as the standing water plantain with no ring is. The ring and the white inside it are the package's measured ones; the leaf reaches the same 0.79 of the radius as every ringed crest.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, right page, third row, third column counted from the right: 丸ニふと沢瀉 (measured, and drawn afresh with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「丸ニふと沢瀉」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描き直したもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

45 guides, 10 shapes, 71 steps.

## Pair of embracing arrowheads · 抱き合わせ沢瀉 (`dakiawase-omodaka`)

Two curved arrowhead leaves cup one flowering spray. Their stalks cross below.

曲がった二枚の沢瀉で花の枝を抱き、下で茎を交差させます。

Measured: The arch and two side notches follow the mirror axis of the upright flowering spray.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 抱合せおもだか (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「抱合せおもだか」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

74 guides, 2 shapes, 110 steps.

## Nagato arrowhead · 長門沢瀉 (`nagato-omodaka`)

A straight central spray rises between two open folded lobes.

まっすぐな花の茎を、折り返した二枚の沢瀉の間に立てます。

Measured: The long leaf openings mirror each other and close near the foot of the central stalk.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, right page, third row, second column from the right: 長門澤瀉 (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「長門澤瀉」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

66 guides, 2 shapes, 99 steps.

## Three water plantains · 三つ沢瀉 (`mitsu-omodaka`)

Three heads of three slender pointed lobes each: a central head, larger and lower, whose lobes point up, down to the left and down to the right, over a head each side that stands higher, with a spray of five round florets on twigs on each side of each of the side heads. Each lobe is the lens between two arcs, with a white vein, and a small ring stands where a head's lobes meet. A bar for the stalk runs down from the central ring and a thin curved band lies behind the heads. The band and the stalk are built first, then the sprays, then the central head, then the side heads cut a white line short of it.

細くとがった三つの裂片をもつ葉を三枚置きます。真ん中の葉は大きくて低く、裂片を上、左下、右下に向け、高いところに立つ左右の葉の上に重なります。左右の葉の両側には、五つの丸い花をつけた枝を一本ずつ添えます。裂片はどれも二つの弧にはさまれた形で、白い葉脈を一本通し、葉の裂片が出会うところに小さな輪を置きます。茎は中央の輪から下へ伸びる棒で、葉の後ろに細く曲がった帯を置きます。帯と茎、枝、真ん中の葉、左右の葉（真ん中の葉から白い線一本分だけ離して切ります）の順に作図します。

Measured: The 三ッ沢瀉 of the 1892 crest book (frame 14, right page, row 4, column 4 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius of the circle round it. The central head's ring stands at (-3, -2) with its lobe tips at (-1, 64), (-51, -50) and (50, -50); the left head's ring at (-52, 10) with tips at (-52, 78), (-79, -35) and (-46, -17); the right head's at (52, -3) with tips at (51, 70), (85, -44) and (40, -17); each lobe a lens whose half-width is 0.18 of its length. The stalk is 8 wide at x = -3 from -2 down to -76; the band an arc of 46 about (0, -8), 3.5 wide. Each spray is a stem and five florets of 4.5 placed as the cell shows them. The book draws the heads and florets in outline, the florets as small rings and the band as a double line; ours is solid, with plain lens lobes and round florets.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, right page, fourth row, fourth column counted from the right: 三ッ沢瀉 (three heads with sprays; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から四列目「三ッ沢瀉」（花の枝を添えた三枚の葉。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

115 guides, 18 shapes, 172 steps.

## Three standing water plantains · 三つ立ち沢瀉 (`mitsu-tachi-omodaka`)

Three heads of three pointed lobes each, on one stalk: a central head with its lobes up, down to the left and down to the right, and a head each side lower down, turned a little differently. Each lobe is the lens between two arcs, with a white vein up it, and a small ring stands where a head's three lobes meet. A bar for the stalk rises up the middle to the central ring and a curved band lies behind the heads, both cut a white line short of the heads in front. The band is built first, then the stalk, then the central head, then the left and the right one where they stand.

一本の茎に、とがった三つの裂片をもつ葉を三枚置きます。真ん中の葉は裂片を上、左下、右下に向け、左右の葉はそれより低く、向きを少し変えます。裂片はどれも二つの弧にはさまれた形で、白い葉脈を一本通し、葉の三つの裂片が出会うところに小さな輪を置きます。茎は真ん中を上へ通る棒で、先は中央の輪に届き、葉の後ろに曲がった帯を置きます。どちらも前にある葉からは白い線一本分だけ離します。帯、茎、真ん中の葉、左の葉、右の葉の順に、その場で作図します。

Measured: The 三ッ立ちおもだか of the 1892 crest book (frame 14, left page, row 1, column 1 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius of the circle round it. The central head's ring stands at (-5, 27) with its lobe tips at (-1, 99), (-44, -6) and (38, -5); the left head's ring at (-53, -9) with tips at (-59, 51), (-81, -49) and (-21, -36); the right head's at (45, -12) with tips at (49, 44), (79, -54) and (10, -43). Each lobe is a lens whose half-width is 0.26 of its length; the stalk is a bar 9 wide at x = -4, from 24 down to -83; the band is an arc of 49 about (0, -20), 7 wide. The book draws the heads in outline, its lower lobes fuller and drooping, the band as a double line and the stalk as a box; ours is solid, with plain lens lobes, and the band is a single curved bar.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, first row, first column counted from the right: 三ッ立ちおもだか (three heads on one stalk; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から一列目「三ッ立ちおもだか」（一本の茎に三枚の葉。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

65 guides, 14 shapes, 114 steps.

## Maple leaf · 紅葉 (`momiji`)

Seven pointed lobes about a small ring low on the leaf, each the lens between two arcs of one radius: the top one the longest, and each pair shorter down to the lowest two, with deep, narrow gaps between them. A straight white vein runs from the ring towards every tip. A short stem hangs under the ring, a little wider at its flat end.

葉の下の方にある小さな輪のまわりに、先のとがった七つの裂片を広げます。裂片はどれも、同じ半径の二つの弧にはさまれた形です。一番上が一番長く、左右の組は下へいくほど短くなり、裂片の間は深く細く切れこみます。輪からそれぞれの裂片の先へ、まっすぐな白い葉脈を一本ずつ通します。輪の下に短い茎を垂らし、平らな切り口の方を少し太くします。

Measured: The leaf's proportions are measured, as numbers only, from a present-day drawing of 一つ楓; no drawing or outline from it is included. In units of the top lobe's length from the small ring where the veins meet: the lobes stand 0, 48, 93 and 132 degrees from upright and are 1, 0.85, 0.61 and 0.45 long, each about a third as wide as it is long and fuller on its upper side; the gaps between them reach in to 0.44, 0.35 and 0.33 from the ring, and the lowest two lobes meet 0.16 under it. The ring is 0.07 across with a white line round it, and the stem hangs to 0.4 under it, widening to its flat end. Ours is within 0.02 of each of these, every lobe the lens between two circles of 87; its veins run straight from the ring to every tip, where the drawing's bow upward, by up to 0.09 in the upper lobes. The crest's period source is the 1892 crest book (frame 44, 紅葉), measured the same way for comparison: its lobes stand 0, 41, 74 and 123 degrees from upright and are 1, 0.89, 0.75 and 0.55 long, and it has a white arc across the foot, veins that start from that arc, and a broad stem.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, third row, fourth column counted from the right: 紅葉 (measured, and drawn afresh with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から四列目「紅葉」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描き直したもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

33 guides, 5 shapes, 48 steps.

## Tatsuta River maple · 立田川 (`tatsutagawa`)

An angled maple leaf lies across three parallel bands of a stream. A shallow arc marks the leaf's foot, and the veins spread from the arc to its lobes. The stream turns through two round bends and opens into three ruled tails.

斜めを向いた紅葉を、水の曲がりの上に重ねます。葉の付け根には浅い弧を引き、そこから各裂片に葉脈を伸ばします。水は二か所で丸く折り返し、定規で引いた三本の流れになります。

Measured: The print spans about 310 pixels and rises 250. The tallest maple lobe points up and right, and the leaf covers parts of all three stream bands. The water turns at the upper right and again at the lower left. The drawing regularises the bands and makes their overlap with the leaf clear.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, right page, second row, first column from the right: 立田川 (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「立田川」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

100 guides, 2 shapes, 162 steps.

## Paper-mulberry leaf · 梶の葉 (`kaji-no-ha`)

A leaf of five round lobes on a short stem: one at the top, one to each side and two below, standing on a round middle that the bays between them reach in to. A lobe's edge is nine scallops, circles two fifths of the lobe wide on the grid points nearest a ring about its middle. A white rib runs along each lobe from the point on the axis where the ribs meet, with white veins leaving it on both sides: four a side in the top lobe and three a side in the others. Every rib and vein ends on a circle a little inside its lobe's edge. The stem lies over the leaf with a white line round it. The right half is built in full, then the stem, then the left half where it stands.

短い柄のついた、丸い裂片が五つある葉です。裂片は上に一つ、左右に一つずつ、下に二つあり、まん中の丸い部分につながります。裂片の間の切れ込みは、その円までです。裂片の縁は、小さな円九つでできた波の形です。中心線の上の一点から、それぞれの裂片へ白い葉脈を一本引き、その両側に白い葉脈を出します。上の裂片は四本ずつ、ほかの裂片は三本ずつです。白い葉脈はどれも、裂片の縁より少し内側の円で終わります。柄は葉の上に重ね、まわりに白い線を残します。右半分、柄の順に作図し、左半分はその場で作図します。

Measured: The 梶の葉 of the 1892 crest book (frame 17, left page, row 2, column 2 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of half the leaf's height with its stem. Five round lobes: the top one 0.8 across and 0.8 tall, its top at 0.97; one each side 0.7 across that reaches 0.97 from the axis, its middle a little over the centre; and two below, 0.67 across, down to 0.88 under the centre. The bays between the lobes reach in to about 0.5 from a point 0.12 under the centre. The veins meet on the axis 0.5 under the centre; a midrib runs up the top lobe with five or six veins a side, four or five veins fan into each side lobe, and each low lobe has a level rib with four veins hanging from it. The stem is 0.14 wide and ends 0.98 under the centre. The book draws it in outline with wavy edges, curved veins and a small fold where two lobes meet; ours is solid, as a crest is worn, the left half the mirror of the right, each lobe's edge nine round scallops, with a straight white rib along every lobe from the point where they meet, four straight white veins a side in the top lobe and three a side in the others, as the usual drawing of this crest has them (a Wikimedia Commons picture, Kaji-no-Ha, was looked at for comparison only).

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 17, left page, second row, second column counted from the right: 梶の葉 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「梶の葉」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

69 guides, 3 shapes, 84 steps.

## Paper-mulberry leaf in a circle · 丸に梶の葉 (`maru-ni-kaji-no-ha`)

The paper-mulberry leaf inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white line between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、梶の葉を収め、輪とのあいだに細い白線を残します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 17, left page, third row, second column counted from the right: 丸に梶の葉 (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「丸に梶の葉」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

71 guides, 4 shapes, 90 steps.

## Three oak leaves · 三つ柏 (`mitsu-gashiwa`)

Three oak leaves a third of a turn apart, their stalks at the centre. A leaf's smooth edge is five circles: one at the top, a shoulder each side and a foot each side. A white midrib runs up it, and three white veins each side leave the midrib low, bow out beside the edge and run up towards the top, each an arc. A small disc sits in a white ring where the leaves meet.

柏の葉を三枚、葉柄を中心に集めて120度ずつ離して置きます。葉のなめらかな縁は五つの円（上に一つ、左右の肩に一つずつ、左右の下に一つずつ）でできます。白い中肋を一本通し、左右三本ずつの白い葉脈を、中肋の下の方から縁に沿ってふくらませ、上へ向かわせます。葉脈はどれも弧です。葉が集まるところに、白い輪の中の小さな円を置きます。

35 guides, 4 shapes, 57 steps.

## Three small-scaled oak leaves · いも柏 (`imo-kashiwa`)

Three narrow, rounded oak leaves meet in a small round joint.

ふくらみのある細い三枚の柏を、小さな丸い中心に合わせます。

Measured: The lobes stay close to the three stems, leaving wide open bays.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, left page, first row, third column from the right: いもかしワ (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「いもかしワ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

32 guides, 4 shapes, 54 steps.

## Three pointed oak leaves · かぶ柏 (`kabu-gashiwa`)

Three jagged oak leaves turn away from a round heart.

小さな丸い中心から、縁のとがった柏を三枚開きます。

Measured: The veins and unequal edge tips follow the same measured lobe in each third.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 17, right page, fourth row, second column from the right: かぶかしワ (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「かぶかしワ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

49 guides, 4 shapes, 61 steps.

## Trailing oak leaves · 蔓柏 (`tsuru-gashiwa`)

Three oak leaves surround a round heart. Three paired curls spring from the spaces between them.

丸い中心を三枚の柏で囲み、その間から二股に巻く蔓を三つ伸ばします。

Measured: Each veined leaf and paired curl repeats through thirds of the circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 17, right page, second row, second column from the right: 蔓かしは (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「蔓かしは」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

53 guides, 7 shapes, 72 steps.

## One oak leaf in a circle · 丸に一枚柏 (`maru-ni-ichimai-gashiwa`)

One oak leaf standing upright inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white line between them. The leaf's edge is seven arcs a side that meet in notches, from the tip down to the foot, the left edge the mirror of the right; a white midrib runs up it with four white veins each side, each an arc; its stalk is a short bar under it.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、柏の葉を一枚まっすぐに立てて収め、輪とのあいだに細い白線を残します。葉の縁は、先から足まで、くぼみで出会う片側七つの弧でできていて、左の縁は右の縁を左右反転したものです。白い中肋を通し、左右四本ずつの白い葉脈を弧で通します。葉柄は葉の下の短い棒です。

Measured: The 丸ニ一枚かしは of the 1892 crest book (frame 16, left page, row 1, column 4 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius the ringed motif reaches. The leaf stands tip to foot over the whole reach (the stalk's end 98 under the centre) and is about 55 across each side of the midrib at its widest. Each side's edge is seven arcs fitted to the book's outline, of circles of 54, 37, 29, 65, 24, 49 and 11; four veins a side, arcs of 60 to 146, leave the midrib one above the next. The book draws the leaf in outline, with a notched edge, more veins and a stalk drawn as a box; ours is solid, the ring is the package's medium ring, and the white midrib stops short of the tip and the foot.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, left page, first row, fourth column counted from the right (the leftmost): 丸ニ一枚かしは (one leaf in a ring; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から四列目（いちばん左）「丸ニ一枚かしは」（輪に入った一枚の葉。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

57 guides, 3 shapes, 82 steps.

## Three oak leaves in a circle · 丸に三つ柏 (`maru-ni-mitsu-gashiwa`)

The three oak leaves inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に三つ柏を収めます。

37 guides, 5 shapes, 63 steps.

## Three small-scaled oak leaves in a fine ring · 細輪にいも柏 (`hosowa-ni-imo-kashiwa`)

Three narrow, rounded oak leaves stand in a fine enclosing ring.

ふくらみのある細い三枚の柏を、細輪の内側に置きます。

Measured: The same narrow lobes and long straight stems turn in thirds inside the ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, left page, second row, third column from the right: 細ワニいもかしワ (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「細ワニいもかしワ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

33 guides, 5 shapes, 58 steps.

## Crossed oak leaves · 違い柏 (`chigai-gashiwa`)

Two oak leaves crossed. One broad leaf lies across from the lower left to the upper right, over a second leaf, its mirror image drawn a twentieth smaller, of which only the pointed end at the upper left and a wedge at the lower right show; a narrow white line runs round the front leaf where it passes over. A leaf's edge is a scalloped run of small arcs about 26 long, 3 high, that follow five sides meeting at its stalk, at a corner of its upper edge, at its tip and at a notch below; a white midrib is ruled up it and five white arc veins leave it each side. The back leaf is built first and cut a white line short of the front one, then the front leaf, then a short bar for each stalk.

柏の葉を二枚、交差させます。幅の広い一枚が左下から右上へ横切って、もう一枚（それを左右反転して20分の1だけ小さくしたもの）の上に重なり、後ろの葉は、左上のとがった先と、右下のくさび形のところだけが見えます。前の葉のまわりは、重なるところに細い白線を一本通します。葉の縁は、長さ約26、ふくらみ約3の小さな弧が並んだ波形で、葉柄のところ、上の縁の角、先、下のくぼみで出会う五つの辺に沿います。白い中肋を直線で通し、その左右に五本ずつ、弧の白い葉脈を出します。後ろの葉を先に作図し、前の葉から白い線一本分だけ離して切ります。つぎに前の葉、最後に二本の短い葉柄を作図します。

Measured: The ちがひかしは of the 1892 crest book (frame 17, right page, row 2, column 3 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius of the circle round it. The front leaf runs from its stalk at (-57, -57) to its tip at (86, 41), about 36 degrees above level, 150 long and about 90 across at its broadest; its upper edge stands 53 to 57 up and its lower edge turns in at a notch at (52, -14). The back leaf is its mirror image drawn 0.95 the size about its stalk at (57, -57). The book draws the back leaf a little smaller and rounder where it shows below the front leaf, wavy edges and a small cut end on each stalk; ours has a regular run of scallops (about 26 long and 3 high, read from the top edge's bumps, 25 to 30 apart), a wedge at the lower right, and square-cut stalks.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 17, right page, second row, third column counted from the right: ちがひかしは (two leaves crossed; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「ちがひかしは」（交差した二枚の葉。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

99 guides, 4 shapes, 131 steps.

## Embracing oak leaves · 抱き柏 (`daki-gashiwa`)

Two oak leaves face to face, their tips together at the top with a white line between them. A leaf's outer edge is four round scallops and one wide arc across the top; its foot is cut on a slant, so that the two leaves part in a V. A white midrib curves up it, with five white veins outside the midrib and six between it and the middle, each an arc. The right leaf is built in full, then its stalk, then the left leaf and its stalk where they stand; the two stalks cross.

柏の葉を二枚、先を上で合わせて向かい合わせに置き、間に白い線を一本通します。葉の外側の縁は、四つの丸いふくらみと、上を渡る大きな一つの弧でできています。足は斜めに切るので、二枚の葉はV字に分かれます。白い中肋を弧で通し、その外側に五本、内側（中心側）に六本の白い葉脈を、どれも弧で通します。右の葉、その葉柄の順に作図し、左の葉と葉柄はその場で作図します。二本の葉柄は交差します。

Measured: The 抱き柏 of the 1892 crest book (frame 17, right page, row 1, column 1 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius of the circle round it. Each leaf's outside is four scallops (circles of 20, 32, 29 and 30) and one arc of 105 across the top that runs close to the layout circle, fitted to the book's outline; the midrib is an arc of 156; there are five veins outside it and six between it and the middle, arcs of 58 to 149. The book draws the leaves in outline with a ragged edge, a notched slit between them, more veins than ours, and stalks that cross low. Ours is solid, as a crest is worn, the left leaf the mirror of the right, the slit a straight white line, and the stalks two short bars that cross.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 17, right page, first row, first column counted from the right: 抱かしは (two leaves face to face; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「抱かしは」（向かい合う二枚の葉。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

65 guides, 4 shapes, 103 steps.

## Wood sorrel · 片喰 (`katabami`)

Three heart leaves, their points at the centre. A leaf's two round lobes are two circles; its sides are ruled from the centre to touch them. Under the lobes a small white sepal runs down to a plain disc in a white ring at the centre. The leaf on top is built in full, and the other two are built the same way where they stand, clockwise.

心形の葉を三枚、葉先を中心に向けて置きます。葉の丸い裂片は並んだ二つの円で、両脇は中心からその円に接する直線です。裂片の下に、小さな白いがくを、中心の白い輪まで入れます。輪の中は無地の円です。上の葉を先に作図し、残りの二枚は、同じ作り方でその場で時計回りに作図します。

Measured: The 片ばミ of the 1892 crest book (frame 15, left page, fourth row, second column), its lobes fitted by least squares: in a reach of 100, each lobe is a circle of 34 whose centre is 66 from the centre and 25 degrees from the leaf's axis, and the ring at the centre is 19 across to its outside. Ours has lobes of 29 about (±24, 52), the same 34, 66 and 25 in a reach of 100, and a disc of 14 in a white ring to 16, so 19. The book's three sepals stand on the leaves' axes, a short stalk widening to a point, as ours do.

25 guides, 4 shapes, 40 steps.

## Three split wood-sorrel leaves · 三つ割り片喰 (`mitsu-wari-katabami`)

Three heart-shaped leaves turn inward from round hearts along the enclosing circle. Cut-off petals show to either side of each leaf.

外周に沿う丸い中心から、ハート形の葉を三枚内側に向けます。各葉の両側に、円で切った花びらを見せます。

Measured: The three hearts and the clipped side leaves repeat by thirds around the same circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, second row, first column from the right: 三ッワリかたばみ (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「三ッワリかたばみ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

20 guides, 7 shapes, 36 steps.

## Wood sorrel in a circle · 丸に片喰 (`maru-ni-katabami`)

The wood sorrel inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に片喰を収めます。

## Wood sorrel in a hexagon · 亀甲に片喰 (`kikko-ni-katabami`)

A tortoise-shell hexagon, a thick line with a thin line inside it, one corner up, and inside it the wood sorrel.

角を上にした亀甲（太い線と、その内側の細い線）の中に、片喰を収めます。

50 guides, 6 shapes, 71 steps.

## Sword wood sorrel · 剣片喰 (`ken-katabami`)

Three heart-shaped leaves alternate with three blades around the round centre. The upright blade is wide at its ruled point and curves down to a narrow foot. Each leaf ends a white gap short of the blade beside it.

丸い中心のまわりに、ハート形の葉と剣を三つずつ交互に置きます。上を向く剣は定規で引いた先が幅広く、根元に向かって弧で細くなります。葉と剣の間には白いすき間を残します。

Measured: Frame 15: the upper blade reaches the outer circle. Its shoulder is about 0.54 of the blade reach across. A leaf stands between each pair of blades and reaches slightly less far. Opposite edges are made symmetric and the three places are set 120 degrees apart.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, left page, first row, fourth column from the right: 剣片ばミ (左頁、一段目、右から四列目「剣片ばミ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

14 guides, 7 shapes, 30 steps.

## Sword wood sorrel in a circle · 丸に剣片喰 (`maru-ni-ken-katabami`)

Three heart-shaped leaves and three blades in a circular band. The upright blade reaches almost to the inside of the band, and the other two do the same where they stand.

ハート形の葉と剣を三つずつ輪の中に収めます。上を向く剣は輪の内側の近くまで伸び、残りの二本もそれぞれの位置で同じところまで伸びます。

Measured: Frame 15: the blade tips approach the inner ring and the leaves reach almost as far. The printed ring is thin. The package uses a medium ring with the standard 3.5-unit gap at the three blade tips.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, left page, second row, fourth column from the right: 丸に剣片ばミ (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「丸に剣片ばミ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

16 guides, 8 shapes, 36 steps.

## Sakai wood sorrel · 酒井片喰 (`sakai-katabami`)

Three broad heart-shaped leaves reach close to the inner edge of the ring. A pointed white stamen sits under each leaf’s notch.

幅広いハート形の葉を輪の内側の近くに三枚並べ、各葉の切れ込みの下に先のとがった白いしべを置きます。

Measured: The three broad leaf outlines and pointed rays share one set of angles. The round band has an even width.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, third row, first column from the right: 酒井かたばみ (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「酒井かたばみ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 5 shapes, 31 steps.

## Arabesque flower · 唐花 (`karahana`)

Five petals round a small disc, each with three rounded lobes at its edge. Narrow ruled gaps run between the petals, and a short white stamen runs out through the middle of each.

小さな円のまわりに、先が三つの丸いふくらみに分かれた花びらを五枚並べます。花びらの間は細い直線で分け、各花びらの中央に短い白いしべを一本入れます。

Measured: Frame 18: the notches between petals stand about four fifths out from the centre. The middle tip is struck from 77 out with a radius of 23. The two shoulder lobes stand 31 either side and 68 out, with radii of 21. The central disc has a radius of about 12 and the stamens reach 43.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 唐花 (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「唐花」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

16 guides, 6 shapes, 28 steps.

## Stylized sword arabesque flower · 剣唐花崩し (`ken-karahana-kuzushi`)

Five separated, scalloped petals turn about a round heart, with one pointing down. A white sword is ruled inside each petal, narrow at the heart and flaring before its point.

丸い中心のまわりに、丸いふくらみのある花びらをすき間を空けて五枚並べ、その一枚を真下に向けます。各花びらの中に、中心側が細く、先端の手前で広がる白い剣を直線で抜きます。

Measured: Frame 18: the printed flower has five separate petals, the lowest pointing down. The sword cuts reach about four fifths of their petal and flare to about 30 across before the point, in a reach of 100.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, left page, third row, first column from the right: けんから花くづし (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「けんから花くづし」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

19 guides, 6 shapes, 32 steps.

## Six-petalled arabesque flower · 六つ唐花 (`mutsu-karahana`)

Six scalloped petals are struck at equal angles about one round heart, the first pointing up. The narrow ruled cuts split the petals from the heart to the outside.

一つの丸い中心のまわりに、先がふくらむ花びらを六枚、60度ずつ回して並べます。一枚目は真上に向け、中心から外縁まで細い直線で花びらを分けます。

Measured: Frame 18: the labelled print is one flower with six scalloped petals. The petals stand 60 degrees apart, with shallow notches between them and a small central disc.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, left page, third row, second column from the right: 六ッから花 (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「六ッから花」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

17 guides, 7 shapes, 29 steps.

## Three clustered arabesque flowers · 三つ盛り唐花 (`mitsumori-karahana`)

Three small scalloped arabesque flowers stand on the corners of a triangle, with a narrow gap between their petals. The upper flower is laid out in full and drawn upright again at the two places below.

先に丸いふくらみのある小さな唐花を三角形の頂点に三つ並べ、花びらどうしの間に細いすき間を空けます。上の一つを作図し、下の二か所にも同じ向きで作図します。

Measured: Frame 18: three flowers in a compact triangular group, one above and two below. Each reaches about 45 in an overall layout radius of 100, with the centres 55 out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, left page, first row, second column from the right: 三ッもりから花 (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「三ッもりから花」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

92 guides, 3 shapes, 106 steps.

## Back of an arabesque flower · 裏唐花 (`ura-karahana`)

The black, scalloped flower turns around a white five-pointed calyx. A short bent stalk reaches down from its centre.

黒いふくらみのある花の中央に白い五角の萼を置き、短く曲がった茎を下に伸ばします。

Measured: The outer lobes are set at equal angles. The calyx and stalk remain one paper opening.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. ## Arabesque flower with a floating figured border · 唐花浮線綾 (`karahana-fusen-ryo`)

A scalloped central flower stands inside five split flowers along the outer circle. Short white rays face the heart of each blossom.

ふくらみのある中央の唐花を、外周に沿う五つの割り花の内側に置きます。それぞれの中心から短い白線を伸ばします。

Measured: The five outer flowers meet the same circle and turn equally around the middle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. ## Arabesque flower in a medium ring (chūwa) · 中輪に唐花 (`chuwa-ni-karahana`)

The five-petalled arabesque flower sits in a thin ring with a narrow space between the petals and the ring.

五枚の花びらの唐花を、細輪の中に収めます。花びらと輪の間に細いすき間を空けます。

Measured: Frame 18: the cell below the sword flower shows one band. Its printed ring lines stand about 3–5 pixels apart across an outer diameter of 124 pixels, or 2.5–4 percent. The medium-ring three geese in the same frame and the medium-ring three arrow nocks in frame 29 are about 4–5 percent. The 丸に wild goose on frame 18 is about 6–7 percent. The flower uses the package’s thin ring, which lies nearer the printed width.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, fourth row, fourth column from the right: 中わニから花 (右頁、四段目、右から四列目「中わニから花」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

18 guides, 7 shapes, 34 steps.

## Sword arabesque flower · 剣唐花 (`ken-karahana`)

Five scalloped petals around a round heart, with a ruled sword point between each pair. The petal flanks curve towards the heart, and each point keeps a narrow paper line against them.

丸い中心のまわりに先が三つにふくらむ花びらを五枚並べ、花びらの間に直線で剣先を描きます。花びらの両脇は中心へ向かって弧を描き、剣先との間に細い白いすき間を空けます。

Measured: Frame 18: five sword points alternate with five narrower scalloped petals. The swords and the petals have about the same reach. The sword shoulders stand about 89 out and 48 across in a reach of 100.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, third row, fourth column from the right: 剣から花 (右頁、三段目、右から四列目「剣から花」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

22 guides, 11 shapes, 40 steps.

## Bellflower · 桔梗 (`kikyo`)

Five petals. On each side of a petal a round shoulder, the arc of a circle from the notch between petals, and touching it a flatter arc that runs up to the tip, where the two sides meet in a small sharp point; below the notches its sides are ruled to the centre. A white gap parts neighbours, a short white ray runs up the middle of each petal, and in the centre is a plain round disc with a white line round it.

花弁は五枚です。花弁の両側は、花弁の間の切れ込みから始まる円の弧（丸い肩）と、それに接して先まで伸びるゆるい弧でできていて、左右の弧が先で小さく尖って出会います。切れ込みから下の辺は、中心への直線です。隣どうしは白い隙間で分け、各花弁の中央に短い白い線を一本入れます。中心には、白い線で囲んだ丸を置きます。

19 guides, 6 shapes, 33 steps.

## Outlined reverse bellflower · 中陰裏桔梗 (`chukage-ura-kikyo`)

Two sets of five pointed lobes form a band around the reverse of the flower. Each edge is a compass arc. A small five-pointed calyx sits inside, with a curved stem leaning to the right.

五つのとがった裂片を内外に並べ、花の裏側を囲む帯にします。それぞれの縁はコンパスの弧です。内側には五つの先を持つ小さながくを置き、曲がった茎を右に傾けます。

Measured: The print's upper outer and inner tips are about 29 pixels apart. On the 200-unit page their distances from the centre are 96 and 77. The two sets of notches are 71 and 54 from the centre and the arcs have radii 74 and 61. The five lobes are made identical. The little calyx and visibly slanted stem keep the print's separate silhouette.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, right page, third row, fourth column from the right: 中かげうらききやう (右頁、三段目、右から四列目「中かげうらききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

34 guides, 2 shapes, 46 steps.

## Five interlocking bellflower petals · 五つ桔梗組み (`itsutsu-kikyo-gumi`)

Five four-sided petals meet around a point. Ruled lines from the centre to the notches separate the petals, with a narrow white gap along each line.

四つの辺を持つ花びら五枚を、一点のまわりに並べます。中心から切れ込みまで定規で引いた直線に沿って、花びらの間に細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The cell's five outside tips and five notches lie on alternating radii. The notches reach about 0.57 as far from the centre as the tips. The drawing uses 96 and 55 on a regular fivefold layout. The hand-drawn seams become straight gaps at the package's thread weight.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 五ッききやう組 (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「五ッききやう組」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

21 guides, 5 shapes, 34 steps.

## Knotted balloon flower · 結び桔梗 (`musubi-kikyo`)

Five pointed bands turn around the centre. Each band passes over the next and rolls in towards the heart, with white left between the two ends of its curl.

先のとがった帯を五本、中心のまわりに並べます。それぞれ次の帯の上を通り、花の中心に向かって巻きます。巻いた先と帯の間に白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The five outside points stand at roughly equal intervals. Each band is about a tenth of the tip reach in width, and curls around an opening a little under a quarter of that reach across. The compass curls have radii 12 and 20 about centres about 38 from the heart. The book's loops are narrower and tilted. The drawing uses the same circular loop at each turn and makes the brush-outlined bands solid.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, right page, fourth row, third column from the right: むすびききやう (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「むすびききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 5 shapes, 20 steps.

## Vine balloon flower · 蔓桔梗 (`tsuru-kikyo`)

Five curved petals with a white ray in each surround a round heart. A narrow stem between each pair of petals forks at the rim into two compass curls. White gaps separate the vines from the petals.

白いしべを入れた曲線の花びら五枚で、丸い中心を囲みます。となり合う花びらの間には細い蔓を通し、その先を二つに分けて円弧で巻きます。花びらと蔓は白いすき間で分けます。

Measured: The printed heart is about 43 pixels across against about 151 from its centre to the top point. Five stems fork between the pointed petals at about 0.76 of that reach. The curls pair to either side of each stem. The drawing uses a heart 26 across, branches around 0.8 of the reach and matching circular curls of radius 10, with white around the vine and each petal's ray.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, left page, fourth row, second column from the right: 蔓桔梗 (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「蔓桔梗」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

30 guides, 11 shapes, 51 steps.

## Thin balloon flower · 痩せ桔梗 (`yase-kikyo`)

Five slender pointed petals, each a lens between two matching circles. A short lens clears the stamen near the foot. The petals stand just clear of the round heart.

細長く先のとがった花びら五枚を置きます。各花びらは、同じ大きさの二つの円が重なった部分の形です。根元には短いレンズ形の白いしべを抜き、円い中心のまわりに細いすき間を残します。

Measured: Frame 40: the five narrow petals are about one fifth as wide as the crest radius. The stamen reaches a little over one third of the way out, and the heart radius is about 0.13 of the tip reach. The petals are regularised to 72-degree turns.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, left page, first row, second column from the right: やせききやう (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「やせききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

7 guides, 6 shapes, 19 steps.

## One balloon flower in a circle · 丸に一つ桔梗 (`maru-ni-hitotsu-kikyo`)

A single side-view flower stands inside a ring. Pointed rear petals rise around a short curl. The two front petals fan from a chalice-shaped base, which curves down to a short foot.

輪の中に、横から見た桔梗を一輪立てます。短い巻きのまわりに、先のとがった奥の花びらが立ちます。手前の二枚は杯のような形から広がり、下には短い曲がった足を置きます。

Measured: The ring spans approximately 306 pixels. Its two ruled boundaries are about 20 pixels apart where they meet the horizontal centre line, or about 0.065 of the span. The package ring is 16.8 on a span of 192. The flower extends about 177 pixels across, and from 44 to 301 vertically. Its swept foot and broad front cup are retained. The fan of dark lines becomes seven short ruled white rays.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, right page, first row, fourth column from the right: 丸ニ一ッききやう (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「丸ニ一ッききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

## Three balloon flowers joined together · 三つ組合せ桔梗 (`mitsu-kumiawase-kikyo`)

Three bellflower centres stand at the corners of an equilateral triangle. Three narrow petals open out from each centre. Each pair of flowers shares one lens petal between its centres. A ruled white vein runs along every petal.

三つの桔梗の芯を正三角形の頂点に置きます。それぞれの芯から外へ細い花びらを三枚ずつ伸ばし、となり合う二つの芯の間にレンズ形の花びらを一枚ずつ渡します。すべての花びらに、定規で引いた白い筋を入れます。

Measured: The printed flower centres are around 101 pixels apart. The upright petal end is about 98 pixels from its centre, and each shared petal spans about 73 pixels between the printed centres' edges. The drawing sets the centres 36 from the middle, the outer petal tips 59 from their centres and the starts of the lenses 9 from their centres. The outer lens arcs have radius 43 and the shared arcs 35.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, right page, third row, third column from the right: 三ッ組合ききやう (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「三ッ組合ききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

26 guides, 3 shapes, 35 steps.

## Six knotted balloon flowers · 六つ結び桔梗 (`mutsu-musubi-kikyo`)

Three pointed bands cross in turn, with six ends arranged around the centre. Each band is ruled between two pairs of parallel sides and two angled ends. At the crossings, narrow white gaps separate the band above from the band below. The open centre has six points.

先のとがった帯を三本交互にくぐらせ、六つの先を中心のまわりに並べます。各帯は、二組の平行な直線と両端の斜めの直線で囲みます。交わりでは、上と下の帯を細い白いすき間で分けます。中央の空きは六つの先を持ちます。

Measured: The cell spans about 271 by 310 pixels. A vertical band is about 21 pixels thick on each side and the ends are angled about 30 degrees from level. The layout uses 13 for the band width, a 96-unit radius to its outer point and a 60-degree turn between bands. The centre opening has outer and inner radii of 24 and 16. The upper loop passes in front on the left and under on the right as it does in the print.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, left page, fourth row, fourth column from the right: 六ッ結びききやう (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「六ッ結びききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

49 guides, 3 shapes, 58 steps.

## Flower balloon flower · 花桔梗 (`hana-kikyo`)

The curved front flower covers five angled petals behind it. Seven rays fan out from a broad white bow low across its face. The two sides reflect about the upright ray.

弧で囲んだ手前の花を、角のある奥の花びら五枚に重ねます。花の下に引いた白い弧の上から、しべ七本を扇状に広げます。中央のしべを軸に左右反転にします。

Measured: The print is approximately 310 pixels wide by 251 tall. The upright petal rises about 109 pixels above the large side points, and the broad ray foot is about 130 pixels across. In the drawing the points are 190 apart, the top tip rises 63 above them and the ray foot spans 80. The angular rear tips keep a white gap from the curved foreground. The fan uses seven white rays in place of the source's dark brush lines.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, left page, first row, third column from the right: 花ききやう (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「花ききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

71 guides, 2 shapes, 94 steps.

## Double balloon flower · 八重桔梗 (`yae-kikyo`)

Five rear petals show between the five broad petals of the front flower. The rear tips reach almost as far as the front ones. A white gap between the layers is struck from larger circles with the same centres as the front petals' shoulders and flanks.

手前の幅広い花びら五枚の間から、奥の花びら五枚が見えます。奥の先端は手前の先端の近くまで伸びます。重なりの白いすき間は、手前の花びらの肩や脇の円と同じ中心を持つ、一回り大きい円から作ります。

Measured: Frame 40: the rear petals stand a half sector between the front petals and reach about 0.96 of their radius. The exposed tips are angular and the front shoulders are round. A consistent gap is left where one layer covers the other.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 八重ききやう (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「八重ききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

26 guides, 11 shapes, 44 steps.

## Bellflower in a circle · 丸に桔梗 (`maru-ni-kikyo`)

The bellflower inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に桔梗を収めます。

## Balloon flower lozenge · 桔梗菱 (`kikyo-bishi`)

A wide lozenge encloses two curved petals facing point to point. White arcs separate them from the pointed sides. At the upper and lower tips, each curved cap has three short ruled rays.

横長の菱の中で、弧で囲んだ二枚の花びらを先が向かい合うように置きます。左右のとがった部分とは白い弧で分けます。上下の先では、弧で仕切った部分に定規で引いた短いしべを三本ずつ入れます。

Measured: The printed lozenge spans about 304 pixels side to side and 236 from top to bottom. The drawing uses 192 by 148. The curved petals meet at the centre, and their shoulders stand 26 to either side and 22 above or below the centre. The arc seams and short rays use the package's thread weight. The lines towards the side points stop just short so the whole outer point remains inked.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. 63 guides, 1 shapes, 76 steps.

## Sword balloon flower · 剣桔梗 (`ken-kikyo`)

Five broad balloon-flower petals with a short white ray in each. A ruled sword point fills each space between two petals. Circles struck beside each petal leave a narrow white gap along the two sides of a blade.

幅広い桔梗の花びら五枚に、短い白いしべを一本ずつ入れます。花びらの間に直線で剣先を描きます。花びらの輪郭に沿う円で、剣の両脇に細い白いすき間を空けます。

Measured: Frame 40: the five blades stand halfway between the five petals. The blade point reaches 98 of a petal reach of 100, and its ruled shoulders are about 48 across and 88 out. The paper line follows two adjacent petals.

23 guides, 11 shapes, 40 steps.

## Balloon flower butterfly · 桔梗蝶 (`kikyo-cho`)

Five pointed petals shape the wings and the upright crown. Seven curved and ruled rays leave the round heart. Above the wings, two thin antennae arc out and curl at their tips.

先のとがった花びら五枚で、蝶の羽と上の花びらを作ります。丸い中心から、弧や直線でできたしべ七本を伸ばします。羽の上には、細い触角二本を外へ伸ばし、先を巻きます。

Measured: The wings span about 300 pixels. The crown rises about 87 above the heart and the antennae about 120. The total printed height is about 197. The drawing spans 190 with a height of about 120, a crown rising 59 and antennae rising 81 above the heart. The wings and antennae are reflected across the centre. The rays become tapered-looking white gaps of two weights instead of small brush-outlined loops.

## Bellflower on a black disc · 石持ち桔梗 (`kokumochi-kikyo`)

Five pointed petals and a round centre are cut out of a black disc. Two tangent compass arcs form each petal shoulder and tip, and a rounded black ray runs outward from the centre into each petal.

黒い円から、先のとがった花びら五枚と丸い中心を白く抜きます。接する二つの弧をつないで花びらの肩と先を作り、中心から各花びらへ、先の丸い黒いしべを伸ばします。

Measured: The photographed disc is about 307 pixels across. Its five petal tips meet the rim, the round centre is about 43 pixels across and the longest ray ends about 75 pixels from the centre. The disc is 192 wide here. The petals come to within two units of the rim so the circular edge stays continuous. The centre is 26 wide and the solid rays reach 47, using the package's thin weight. The shoulders use radius 24 and the tangent arcs to the tips use radius 88. The black seams between petals are twice the package's thread weight.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, right page, second row, third column from the right: 石持ききやう (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「石持ききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

55 guides, 2 shapes, 69 steps.

## Toki balloon flower · 土岐桔梗 (`toki-kikyo`)

Five broad pointed petals around the Toki crest's large circular heart. Each side of a petal turns from a broad shoulder into a flatter arc up to the tip. A narrow white ray runs along the petal and a ruled gap separates it from each neighbour.

土岐桔梗の大きな円い中心を、先のとがった幅広い花びら五枚で囲みます。花びらの両側は丸い肩からゆるい弧に移り、先端で出会います。花びらには細い白いしべを入れ、隣との間を定規で引いたすき間で分けます。

Measured: Frame 40: the heart radius is about 0.15 of the tip reach. Petals are divided into five equal sectors of 72 degrees. The shoulder begins near three fifths of the tip reach, and the short stamen ends a little over one third of the way from the centre. The book's slight asymmetry is regularised.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 40, right page, first row, fourth column from the right: 土岐ききやう (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「土岐ききやう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

20 guides, 6 shapes, 33 steps.

## Sixteen-petal chrysanthemum · 十六八重表菊 (`juroku-yae-giku`)

The sixteen-division: sixteen petals packed edge to edge round a small centre disc, each petal's two sides parallel to spokes a sixteenth of a turn apart with a thin white line between neighbours, and each ending in a round tip, the circle that touches both sides; at the rim, between the front tips, show the round tips of sixteen petals behind, turned half a petal.

十六割で割り付けます。中心の小さな円のまわりに花弁を16枚すき間なく並べ、各花弁の両側を22.5度ずつ離した放射線に平行にとって隣との間に細い白線を残し、先は両側に接する円で丸く収めます。外周では表の花弁の先の間から、半枚ずらした後ろの16枚の丸い先をのぞかせます。

## Three split chrysanthemums · 三つ割り菊 (`mitsu-wari-giku`)

Three half flowers turn inwards from the rim. Each has seven rounded petals and a half-disc at the foot.

Measured: Three half flowers turn inwards from the rim. Each has seven rounded petals and a half-disc at the foot.

八つのふくらみのある小さながくのまわりに、先の丸い花びらを十六枚、どれも外円まで伸ばして並べます。

## Chrysanthemum lozenge · 菊菱 (`kiku-bishi`)

Sixteen rounded petals open from a round centre. The horizontal petals reach furthest, and the upper and lower tips stop sooner to make a broad lozenge.

丸い中心から先の丸い花びらを十六枚伸ばします。左右は長く、上下は短くして、横に広い菱形にします。

Measured: The sixteen tips mark a lozenge with a width close to four thirds of its height. Symmetry is restored across both axes while retaining the shorter upper and lower petals.

## Chrysanthemum wheel · 菊車 (`kiku-guruma`)

Sixteen pointed leaves with slightly tilted axes turn in the same direction about a round centre. A shallow arc on either side of each axis joins the base and tip. The inner halves overlap, with a short white seam between them.

丸い中心のまわりに、先のとがった花びらを十六枚、同じ向きに少し傾けて並べます。軸はほぼまっすぐな線で、その両側に、根元と先を結ぶ浅い弧を引きます。内側の半分は重なり合い、そのあいだに短い白い線が入ります。

## Chrysanthemum seen from behind · 裏菊 (`ura-giku`)

Sixteen petals have the same reach and rounded tips. A six-point calyx and a short stalk curved on two compass arcs show the flower from behind.

## Chrysanthemum on water · 菊水 (`kiku-sui`)

Half a chrysanthemum rises above the water. The rounded ends touch the ruled sides of each petal. Solid bands of water double back around half circles, with white gaps at the crossings.

水の上に菊の半分を見せます。花びらの先は、花びらの両側に定規で引いた直線に接する円弧で丸くします。水の帯は半円を描いて折り返し、重なるところには白いすき間を空けます。

Measured: Frame 39: an upper half-flower with nine visible petals (one at the crown, four to either side) and a small half-disc stands over a much wider panel of water. The water's ruled horizontal bands double back in alternating directions. The two outlines of each stream become the edges of one filled ribbon, including the two inner returns.

## Fourteen-petal chrysanthemum on a black disc · 石持ち十四菊 (`kokumochi-jushi-giku`)

Fourteen rounded petals are cut out of a black disc. A small dot sits within a round white centre.

黒い円から先の丸い花びらを十四枚、白く抜きます。丸い白地の中心に小さな点を置きます。
