## Paulownia, five-three-five · 五三桐 (`gosan-no-kiri`)

Three broad leaves below, the middle one hanging and the two beside it spread; above them three upright spikes, five florets on the middle one and three on each of the others. All three leaves hang from the small circle where they meet. The middle leaf is a rounded heart: its two upper edges are one compass width mirrored, and round its body are five scallops of one width. A side leaf is longer and narrower and made the same way; it is turned out until its midrib falls one for every two it runs, and it passes under the middle leaf. A white midrib runs straight from the small circle to the leaf's tip. On each side of it are four white veins. The first leaves the small circle and the other three leave the midrib lower down, one below the other. Each sweeps out and then down beside the leaf's edge, inside the vein above it, and stops on a circle about the small circle's centre, short of the edge. The upper veins are long and the lowest is short, and none reaches the tip. Each vein is the white between two circles two apart. A spike is a thin upright stalk that rises from behind the leaves, with one floret on its top and pairs of florets below it on short side stems, each clear of its neighbours and of the stalk. A floret is a bud of three discs: a round head and two round shoulders. A side stem is the band between two circles two apart: it leaves the stalk outwards and turns up into the bud. The left half is struck first and the right half is struck in place as its mirror.

下に広い葉を三枚、中央の葉は垂らし、左右の葉は広げます。その上に花穂を三本立て、中央に五つ、左右に三つずつ花をつけます。三枚の葉はどれも、葉が集まる小さな円から出ます。中央の葉は丸みのあるハート形です。上の左右の縁は同じ幅の円で、まわりに同じ幅の丸い裂片を五つ並べます。左右の葉は中央の葉より細長く、同じ作り方です。中肋が2進むごとに1下がる向きまで外へ回し、中央の葉の下に入れます。どの葉にも、小さな円から葉先へ、白い中肋を一本まっすぐ通します。その左右に、白い葉脈を四本ずつ入れます。一本目は小さな円から、残りの三本は中肋の途中から、上から順に出します。どれも外へふくらんでから下へ向かい、葉の縁に沿って、一つ上の葉脈の内側を通ります。どれも、小さな円と同じ中心の円の上、縁の手前で止めます。上の葉脈は長く、いちばん下の葉脈は短く、葉先には集まりません。白い葉脈はどれも、幅が2だけ違う二つの円の間のすき間です。花穂は、葉の後ろから立ち上がる細い茎です。茎の上に花を一つ、その下に左右一対ずつの花を、短い枝の先につけます。花と花の間、花と茎の間には、白いすき間をあけます。花は三つの円でできたつぼみの形で、丸い頭が一つ、丸い肩が二つです。枝は、幅が2だけ違う二つの円の間の帯で、茎から外へ出て、上へ曲がって花に入ります。左半分を先に作図し、右半分は左右反転させて、その場で作図します。

Measured: Read from two cells of the 1892 crest book (frame 38), as parts of the crest's half-width (88 of the layout's 100), upward from the joint, by eye off enlarged crops, so good to a few parts. Its 五七きり (right page, first row, the leftmost cell): the middle spike is a stalk 78 high with a three-lobed bud on its top (about 19 high and 26 wide, drawn in outline) and three florets down each side of it, about 55, 40 and 24 up, each on a short curved stem and reaching about 30 from the stalk at the foot; the side spikes stand 53 to 55 out and 55 to 63 high, with two florets a side reaching about 17. Its 丸に五三きり (left page, third row, the leftmost cell), squeezed into its ring: the middle spike 90 high with two florets a side, the side spikes 59 out and 65 high with one a side. The veins, from the same two cells, as parts of the same half-width: in the middle leaf (89 long) a midrib from the joint to the tip and four lines each side. The first leaves the joint and the others leave the midrib about 24, 37 and 51 down it; each runs out and down beside the leaf's edge, bowing outward a little, and stops short of the lower edge about 51, 60, 68 and 75 down. None reaches the tip. A side leaf starts at the joint itself and is about 95 long; its rib leaves the joint about 27 degrees below level, with four long lines above it that leave it at the joint and about 17, 30 and 40 along it and sweep out beside the upper edge, and shorter lines below it. Ours: the middle spike 75 high in 五三桐 and 78 in 五七桐, its lowest buds reaching 27 and 20 from the stalk; the side spikes 56 out, 52 high in 五三桐 and 58 in 五七桐. The middle leaf is 86 long, with a midrib and four veins a side that leave it at the joint and 24, 37 and 51 down and stop 61, 69, 75 and 76 from the joint. A side leaf starts at the joint, is 93 long and 62 wide, and its midrib falls one for every two it runs (about 27 degrees below level); its four veins a side leave the midrib at the joint and 17, 29 and 40 along it. The book's florets are scalloped lumps that touch one another; ours are buds of three discs with white between them, as the crest is drawn solid. The book's rib sits low in its side leaf, with long lines above it and short ones below; ours is on the leaf's own axis with the same veins on both sides. The book's veins bow a little less than ours, and its middle leaf is narrower at the shoulders, so more of its side leaves shows beside the joint. Nothing is taken from Wikimedia Commons or from any modern book.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 38, left page, third row, the leftmost cell: 丸に五三きり, its ring left out (左頁、三段目、右から四列目（いちばん左）「丸に五三きり」（輪は除く）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

87 guides, 8 shapes, 130 steps.

## Paulownia, seven-five-seven · 五七桐 (`goshichi-no-kiri`)

The same plan as the five-three paulownia with more and smaller florets: seven on the middle spike and five on each of the others. Below the top floret the middle spike has three pairs and each side spike two. The spikes stand a little taller than the five-three paulownia's. The left half is struck first and the right half is struck in place as its mirror.

五三桐と同じ構図で、花を小さくして数を増やし、中央の花穂に七つ、左右の花穂に五つずつつけます。いちばん上の花の下に、中央は三対、左右は二対の花が並びます。花穂は五三桐より少し高くなります。左半分を先に作図し、右半分は左右反転させて、その場で作図します。

## Paulownia in a circle · 丸に五三桐 (`maru-ni-gosan-no-kiri`)

The five-three paulownia scaled to fill a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow even gap inside it.

五三桐を縮めて、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、狭く均一な隙間を残して収めます。

Measured: Read from two cells of the 1892 crest book (frame 38), as parts of the crest's half-width (88 of the layout's 100), upward from the joint, by eye off enlarged crops, so good to a few parts. Its 五七きり (right page, first row, the leftmost cell): the middle spike is a stalk 78 high with a three-lobed bud on its top (about 19 high and 26 wide, drawn in outline) and three florets down each side of it, about 55, 40 and 24 up, each on a short curved stem and reaching about 30 from the stalk at the foot; the side spikes stand 53 to 55 out and 55 to 63 high, with two florets a side reaching about 17. Its 丸に五三きり (left page, third row, the leftmost cell), squeezed into its ring: the middle spike 90 high with two florets a side, the side spikes 59 out and 65 high with one a side. The veins, from the same two cells, as parts of the same half-width: in the middle leaf (89 long) a midrib from the joint to the tip and four lines each side. The first leaves the joint and the others leave the midrib about 24, 37 and 51 down it; each runs out and down beside the leaf's edge, bowing outward a little, and stops short of the lower edge about 51, 60, 68 and 75 down. None reaches the tip. A side leaf starts at the joint itself and is about 95 long; its rib leaves the joint about 27 degrees below level, with four long lines above it that leave it at the joint and about 17, 30 and 40 along it and sweep out beside the upper edge, and shorter lines below it. Ours: the middle spike 75 high in 五三桐 and 78 in 五七桐, its lowest buds reaching 27 and 20 from the stalk; the side spikes 56 out, 52 high in 五三桐 and 58 in 五七桐. The middle leaf is 86 long, with a midrib and four veins a side that leave it at the joint and 24, 37 and 51 down and stop 61, 69, 75 and 76 from the joint. A side leaf starts at the joint, is 93 long and 62 wide, and its midrib falls one for every two it runs (about 27 degrees below level); its four veins a side leave the midrib at the joint and 17, 29 and 40 along it. The book's florets are scalloped lumps that touch one another; ours are buds of three discs with white between them, as the crest is drawn solid. The book's rib sits low in its side leaf, with long lines above it and short ones below; ours is on the leaf's own axis with the same veins on both sides. The book's veins bow a little less than ours, and its middle leaf is narrower at the shoulders, so more of its side leaves shows beside the joint. Nothing is taken from Wikimedia Commons or from any modern book.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 38, left page, third row, the leftmost cell: 丸に五三きり, its ring left out (左頁、三段目、右から四列目（いちばん左）「丸に五三きり」（輪は除く）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

## Mountain cherry · 山桜 (`yamazakura`)

Five petals, each notched at its tip, round a small disc; on each petal three thin white stamens run out from near the centre and end in a dot.

先に切れ込みのある花弁を五枚、小さな円のまわりに並べます。各花弁に、中心の近くから伸びて先が点で終わる細い白いしべを三本入れます。

## Cherry blossom in middle shadow · 中陰桜 (`chu-kage-sakura`)

A band between two notched outlines defines each of five petals. A short ruled ray reaches out from the round centre.

五枚の花びらを、先の割れた二つの輪郭の間の帯で作ります。丸い中心から短い直線を伸ばします。

Measured: The five inner outlines meet the round heart at the same angle.

Five broad petals enclose the heart. Small arc loops mark the points where the petals meet. Three stamens have round tips and two have leaf-shaped tips.

Measured: The five small loops are regularised around the edges. The print’s two kinds of stamen remain distinct.

## Nine bamboo-grass leaves · 九枚笹 (`kumai-zasa`)

Three groups of three leaves, a third of a turn apart: a broad leaf from the centre to the outer circle, with a white midrib, and a narrow leaf from under each side of it to a point on the circle. The top group's right half is built in full, then each other half where it stands, clockwise.

三枚ひと組の葉を、120度ずつ離して三組置きます。広い葉は中心から外の円まで立ち、白い葉脈を一本通します。広い葉の左右の下から、細い葉が一枚ずつ出て、外の円の上でとがります。上の組の右半分を最後まで作図し、ほかの半分は、時計回りに、それぞれその場で作図します。

Measured: The 丸に九枚笹 of the 1892 crest book (frame 37, left page, row 4, column 4 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of the radius of the room inside its ring; the book prints no nine-leaf bamboo grass without a ring. A broad leaf runs from the centre to the ring and is 0.63 across at its widest, 0.6 up. A narrow leaf comes out from under each side of it, 0.24 wide there, and ends in a point on the ring a third of the way round to the next broad leaf. The book draws the leaves in outline; ours are solid, as a crest is worn, with a white midrib in each broad leaf.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 37, left page, fourth row, fourth column counted from the right: 丸に九枚笹 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler, without its ring) (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「丸に九枚笹」（寸法を測り、輪をのぞいて、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

6 guides, 6 shapes, 16 steps.

## Tachibana orange · 橘 (`tachibana`)

A round fruit whose top is parted into segments round its calyx, a pointed leaf standing above it and a leaf to each side, two more leaves below, and a short stem. The left half is drawn from the book and mirrored about the axis.

丸い実の上部を蔕のまわりで房に分け、その上に尖った葉を一枚立て、左右に一枚ずつ、下にも二枚の葉と短い枝をつけます。左半分を紋帳から写し取り、中心線で左右対称に返します。

Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, left page, third row, third column counted from the right: たちばな (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「たちばな」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

## One clove · 一つ丁子 (`hitotsu-choji`)

A drop-shaped bud, a round head that narrows to a point at the foot, with three round petals standing on the head, the middle one over the other two. A white stamen lies along each petal, on the line from the head's centre through the petal's, and the bud lies over the petals with a white line round it. Each side of the bud is one long arc that touches the head at its widest. The middle petal, the right petal and the bud are built in full, then the left petal where it stands.

しずくの形のつぼみです。丸い頭から、下のとがった先へ細くなります。頭の上に丸い花びらを三枚のせ、まん中の一枚をほかの二枚の上に重ねます。花びらには、頭の中心から花びらの中心へ向かう白いしべを一本ずつ入れます。つぼみは花びらの上に重ね、まわりに白い線を残します。つぼみの両わきは、頭のいちばん広いところに接する長い弧一本ずつです。まん中の花びら、右の花びら、つぼみを作図し、左の花びらはその場で作図します。

Measured: The 丸に一ッ丁子 of the 1892 crest book (frame 12, right page, row 1, column 1 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of the radius of the room inside its ring. The bud is a drop 1.5 tall: a round head 0.76 across, its top 0.55 over the centre, narrowing to a point 0.95 under it; halfway down it is 0.45 across. Three round petals stand on the head: the middle one 0.44 across with its top at 0.91, and one each side 0.48 across that reaches 0.59 from the axis. A short stamen runs out from the head along each petal. The book draws it in outline, a little to the left of its ring's middle, and its stamens are bold strokes; ours is solid, as a crest is worn, the left half the mirror of the right, the stamens white, and the bud's sides are one arc each, up to 0.03 fuller than the book's towards the point.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 12, right page, first row, first column counted from the right: 丸に一ッ丁子, without its ring (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「丸に一ッ丁子」の輪を除いたもの（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

14 guides, 4 shapes, 30 steps.

## Ivy · 蔦 (`tsuta`)

Five round lobes with scalloped edges about a small ring: the largest points down, one reaches out to each side, and two stand above with a white line between them. Each lobe is a round body with smaller circles on grid points along its edge. The lower lobe lies over the side lobes and they over the upper two, with a white line round each. White veins run from the ring and beside each straight middle vein, and end on the lobe's round body. The right half is built in full, then the lower lobe, then the left half where it stands.

小さな輪のまわりに、縁が波打つ丸い裂片を五つ置きます。いちばん大きい裂片を下に向け、左右に一つずつ、上に二つ並べ、上の二つの間は白い線で分けます。裂片はどれも、丸い本体の円と、縁に並べた小さな円でできています。下の裂片は左右の裂片の上に、左右の裂片は上の二つの上に重なり、境目に白い線を残します。白い葉脈は、輪から、そしてまっすぐな中央の葉脈の両側から伸び、本体の円の上で終わります。右半分、下の裂片の順に作図し、左半分はその場で作図します。

Measured: The つた of the 1892 crest book (frame 22, right page, row 4, column 4 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of half the leaf's width. The leaf is 1.15 times as wide as it is tall. The small ring where the veins meet stands 0.22 above the middle and is 0.2 across. The lobe that points down reaches 0.87 below the middle and is as wide as half the leaf; the side lobes are 0.85 tall; the two upper lobes reach 0.87 above the middle and meet in a notch 0.74 up the axis. The book draws the leaf in outline, with an uneven wavy edge and five to seven fine veins a side in each lobe. Ours is solid, as a crest is worn: every scallop of the edge is an arc of a circle on a grid point, each lobe has three veins a side or fewer, and the left half is the right half mirrored. A Wikimedia Commons picture of 蔦 (Japanese crest Tuta.svg) was read for comparison only: the same five lobes, ring and overlaps, three veins a side in the lower lobe, and a leaf about 1.25 times as wide as it is tall.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 22, right page, fourth row, fourth column counted from the right: つた (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から四列目「つた」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

54 guides, 4 shapes, 95 steps.

黒い丸の中に五つの広い蔦の葉と筋を白く出します。

Measured: The ivy apertures fit inside a separate circular boundary from this plate.

## Kōrin ivy · 光琳蔦 (`korin-tsuta`)

Five clear rounded lobes meet at a small heart, with no fine veins.

丸い五つの葉を細い筋なしで、小さな丸い中心に合わせます。

Measured: The two upper lobes and the two side lobes mirror each other about the broad lower lobe.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 22, left page, third row, first column from the right: 光林のつた (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「光林のつた」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

## Stylised ivy · 崩し蔦 (`kuzushi-tsuta`)

Five lobes with no ring and a white gap between neighbours: two upper lobes leaning together with a white lens between them, one lobe to each side under them, and a lobe that points down between the side ones, all five meeting at one place. Each lobe is round bodies cut by the arcs of large circles; the gap between the upper and the side lobes is the space between circles of 97 and 100.5, and the gap between the side and the lower lobes the space between circles of 86 and 89.5. The right half is built in full, then the lower lobe, then the left half where it stands.

輪のない五つの裂片を、となり合うものの間に白いすき間をあけて置きます。上の二つの裂片は寄りそって、間に細長い白いレンズ形を残し、左右の裂片はその下に置き、下向きの裂片は左右の裂片のあいだに置きます。五つの裂片は一点に集まります。どの裂片も、丸い形を大きな円の弧で切った形で、上と左右の裂片の間のすき間は半径97と100.5の二つの円の間、左右と下の裂片の間のすき間は半径86と89.5の二つの円の間です。右半分、下の裂片の順に作図し、左半分はその場で作図します。

Measured: The くづし蔦 of the 1892 crest book (frame 22, left page, row 3, column 3 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius of the circle round it. The five lobes meet about 38 above the middle; the gap between the upper and the side lobes is bounded by arcs of circles of 92.5 and 96.9 (read), that between the side and the lower lobes by arcs of 85.8 and 87.5, and the lens between the upper lobes by an arc of 39.5. The label prints くづし in kana, and ours is named 崩し蔦 after the word. The book draws the lobes in outline with a wavy edge; ours are solid, with plain round scallops and a lens a little narrower than the book's, and the left half the mirror of the right.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 22, left page, third row, third column counted from the right: くづし蔦 (five plain lobes with gaps; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「くづし蔦」（すき間をあけた飾りのない五つの裂片。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 3 shapes, 39 steps.

## Large split ivy · 大割蔦 (`owari-tsuta`)

Five round-edged lobes about a small ring, a white line between neighbours: two upper lobes parted by an upright line, one lobe to each side, and a lobe that points down between the side ones. Each lobe's outer edge is a run of round scallops, and the white lines between lobes are arcs of two large circles, each a white line wide; the small ring is a disc in a white ring. The right half is built in full, then the lower lobe, then the left half where it stands.

小さな輪のまわりに、丸い縁の裂片を五つ、となり合うものの間に白い線を一本ずつあけて置きます。上に二つ（縦の線で分けます）、左右に一つずつ、下向きの一つを左右の裂片のあいだに置きます。どの裂片も外側の縁は丸いふくらみの連なりで、裂片の間の白い線は、大きな二つの円の弧（白い線一本の幅）です。小さな輪は、白い輪の中の円です。右半分、下の裂片の順に作図し、左半分はその場で作図します。

Measured: The 大割蔦 of the 1892 crest book (frame 22, left page, row 1, column 1 counted from the right), measured as numbers in units of the radius of the circle round it. The small ring stands 22 above the middle and is 20 across; the white lines between the lobes are arcs of circles of 87 and 88 (read 87.3 and 87.5) and an upright line; the edge of each lobe is a run of circles of 14 to 54 fitted to the book's outline. The book draws the lobes in outline, with a single line between them; ours are solid with a white line 2 wide, plain round scallops, and the left half the mirror of the right, where the book's two halves differ a little.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 22, left page, first row, first column counted from the right: 大割蔦 (five plain lobes; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から一列目「大割蔦」（飾りのない五つの裂片。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

## Pink · 撫子 (`nadeshiko`)

Five broad petals with rounded teeth turn about a small round heart. A straight middle vein and two pairs of curved veins mark each petal. Narrow white lines separate the petals.

小さな丸い花芯のまわりに、丸みのある刻みを持つ幅広い花弁を五枚並べます。各花弁にまっすぐな中筋と二対の弧の筋を入れ、花弁の間を細い白線で分けます。

Measured: In the print the flower is about 300 pixels across, and the round centre about 35. The petals spread widely past the gaps between them. The drawing gives all five equal turns and uses shallow circular curves at the short serrations.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, right page, third row, second column from the right: なでしこ (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「なでしこ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

## Edo pink · 江戸撫子 (`edo-nadeshiko`)

Five toothed petals taper to points around a round heart. Ruled edges make the outer teeth and broad gaps divide the petals. Each petal has a straight vein between two pairs of curved veins.

丸い花芯のまわりに、先が尖って縁に刻みのある花弁を五枚並べます。刻みを定規で結び、花弁の間に深い切れ込みを作ります。各花弁には、二対の弧の筋の間にまっすぐな中筋を通します。

Measured: The printed Edo flower is about 300 pixels across, with a heart about 44 pixels across. It has deeper gaps between the petals and more pointed teeth than the plain pink in the same frame. The drawing uses equal turns and ruled teeth.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 24, right page, second row, third column from the right: 江戸なでしこ (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「江戸なでしこ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

51 guides, 6 shapes, 65 steps.

## Two holly leaves side by side in a circle · 丸に並び柊 (`maru-ni-narabi-hiiragi`)

Two holly leaves standing side by side in a ring, each upright on a thin stalk. A leaf's edge is shallow bays with a spine like a small flame between one and the next, four spines a side. Each bay is an arc of a large circle whose centre stands outside the leaf; the first starts at the leaf's point, on the midrib. At its lower end a bay runs into a small circle that turns the edge up to the spine's point; a second small circle, the flame's round back, crosses the first at the point and runs down into the next bay. Under the last flame a larger circle about a point inside the leaf rounds the edge in to the foot. A white midrib runs the whole length. Four white veins leave it on each side: each lies along a circle of one width, so that it rises from the midrib and curves up, and a short straight fork leaves it toward the edge. The stalk is the space between two upright lines under a level one, and the circle of the room inside the ring cuts its foot. The right leaf is built first: the half toward the axis, the stalk, then the outer half; the left leaf is built where it stands.

輪の中に、柊の葉を二枚、左右に並べて立てます。葉は細い柄の上にまっすぐ立ちます。葉の縁は、浅いへこみの間に、小さな炎のようなとげが立つ形で、とげは片側に四つです。へこみは、葉の外に中心がある大きい円の弧です。最初の弧は、葉のまん中の線の上の、葉の先から始まります。へこみの下の端は小さい円につながり、縁はそこで上へ曲がって、とげの先になります。とげの丸い背は別の小さい円で、とげの先で最初の小さい円と交わり、下のへこみへ続きます。最後のとげの下は、葉の中に中心がある大きい円で、葉の下の端まで丸くつなぎます。白い葉脈は、まん中に上から下まで一本です。その両側に四本ずつ、同じ幅の円に沿った白い葉脈があり、まん中から出て上へ曲がります。それぞれから、縁に向かう短いまっすぐな枝が一本出ます。柄は、横の直線の下の、二本の縦の直線の間で、下の端は輪の内側の円で切ります。右の葉から作図します。中心線に近い半分、柄、外側の半分の順です。左の葉は、その場で作図します。

Measured: The 丸にならび柊 of the 1892 crest book (frame 44, right page, row 4, column 2 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of the radius of the ring's inner line. Two leaves stand side by side, their midribs 0.44 either side of the axis, each with its point 0.86 up, at the ring, and its foot 0.69 down; under it a short stalk runs to the ring. On the side toward the axis each leaf has four spines, 0.30, 0.43, 0.51 and 0.51 from its midrib and 0.71 and 0.41 over the middle, at it, and 0.24 under it; the edge is hollow between one spine and the next, about 0.1 deep, and under the last it rounds in to the foot. Veins leave the midrib on both sides. The book draws it in outline, the leaves uneven, their inner spines touching and crossing the axis, each spine curled up at its point; ours is solid, as a crest is worn, each leaf the same on both sides and the left leaf the mirror of the right, its spines 0.26, 0.43, 0.46 and 0.46 out, with a hairline of white between the two on the axis; each spine is a small flame with a hooked point, between shallow bays about 0.07 deep, and the leaf has a white midrib and four white veins a side that curve up, each with a short fork.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, right page, fourth row, second column counted from the right: 丸にならび柊 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「丸にならび柊」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

70 guides, 7 shapes, 97 steps.

## Embracing holly leaves · 抱き柊 (`daki-hiiragi`)

Two holly leaves that embrace: each curves from its tip at the top of the axis round to the foot, with its spines on the outside, and the two stems cross under them. A leaf's outer edge is five shallow bays, each an arc of a large circle whose centre stands outside the leaf, with a spine like a small flame after each: a small circle hooks the edge up to the point and a second is the flame's round back, which runs down into the next bay; under the last a larger circle rounds the edge in to the foot. The inner edge makes the white between the two leaves a column of four hearts: each heart's side is a line ruled from its point on the axis to the far side of a small circle, its lobe, and the lobe's arc runs under to the axis, where the two leaves meet in a point and the next heart begins. The midrib is a white line along a circle about a grid point on the far side of the axis, and five white veins leave it on each side, ruled through grid points, each as long as the space between two circles about the midrib's centre. Each stem runs on from its own leaf's foot along a line ruled through two grid points, the left leaf's over the right's, with a white line between a stem and the other leaf and either side of the upper stem where the two cross. The right leaf is built first, then the left leaf where it stands, then the stems.

柊の葉二枚が、向かい合って抱き合う紋です。葉は、中心線の上の先から外へ丸く曲がって下の端まで続き、とげは外側にあります。下では二本の柄が交差します。葉の外の縁は、浅いへこみ五つと、そのあとに立つ小さな炎のようなとげです。へこみは、葉の外に中心がある大きい円の弧です。へこみの端は小さい円につながり、縁はそこで上へ曲がって、とげの先になります。とげの丸い背は別の小さい円で、次のへこみへ続きます。最後のとげの下は、大きい円で下の端まで丸くつなぎます。内側の縁では、二枚の葉の間の白いすき間が、ハートの形四つの列になります。ハートの横の辺は、中心線の上の先から、小さい円の遠い側へ引いた直線です。小さい円の弧は、下を回って中心線まで続きます。そこで二枚の葉の先が合い、次のハートが始まります。まん中の白い葉脈は、中心線の向こう側の点を中心とする円に沿った白い線です。その両側に五本ずつ、まっすぐな白い葉脈を引きます。長さは、同じ中心の二つの円の間です。柄は、それぞれの葉の下の端から、斜めの直線に沿って続きます。左の葉の柄を上に重ね、交わるところでは、上の柄の両側に白い線を残します。柄と、もう一枚の葉との間にも白い線を残します。右の葉を先に作図し、左の葉をその場で作図してから、柄を作図します。

Measured: The だきひいらぎ of the 1892 crest book (frame 44, right page, row 1, column 1 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of half the pair's height. Each leaf's midrib is one curve from the tip, on the axis 0.95 up, out to 0.50 from the axis at the middle and in again to the foot, where the two stems cross on the axis 0.88 down and end 0.33 either side, 1.0 down. The outer edge has five spines, each curled up at its point like a flame, about 0.45, 0.72, 0.96, 0.97 and 0.83 out and 0.88, 0.63 and 0.33 over the middle and 0.12 and 0.62 under it, with a bay about 0.07 deep between one and the next. The inner edges of the two leaves meet on the axis at four places, and the white between them is a column of four hearts, each about 0.35 tall and 0.26 wide at its two lobes, its point up. Veins leave the midrib on both sides, about five a side, forked. The book draws it in outline, uneven, with the left leaf's flames larger than the right's; ours is solid, as a crest is worn, the left leaf the mirror of the right, the flames all of one size, the veins straight and unforked.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, right page, first row, first column counted from the right: だきひいらぎ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「だきひいらぎ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

59 guides, 3 shapes, 79 steps.

## Embracing holly leaves in a circle · 丸に抱き柊 (`maru-ni-daki-hiiragi`)

The embracing holly leaves set inside a ring. Two holly leaves that embrace: each curves from its tip at the top of the axis round to the foot, with its spines on the outside, and the two stems cross under them. A leaf's outer edge is five shallow bays, each an arc of a large circle whose centre stands outside the leaf, with a spine like a small flame after each: a small circle hooks the edge up to the point and a second is the flame's round back, which runs down into the next bay; under the last a larger circle rounds the edge in to the foot. The inner edge makes the white between the two leaves a column of four hearts: each heart's side is a line ruled from its point on the axis to the far side of a small circle, its lobe, and the lobe's arc runs under to the axis, where the two leaves meet in a point and the next heart begins. The midrib is a white line along a circle about a grid point on the far side of the axis, and five white veins leave it on each side, ruled through grid points, each as long as the space between two circles about the midrib's centre. Each stem runs on from its own leaf's foot along a line ruled through two grid points, the left leaf's over the right's, with a white line between a stem and the other leaf and either side of the upper stem where the two cross. The right leaf is built first, then the left leaf where it stands, then the stems.

抱き柊を輪の中に収めます。柊の葉二枚が、向かい合って抱き合う紋です。葉は、中心線の上の先から外へ丸く曲がって下の端まで続き、とげは外側にあります。下では二本の柄が交差します。葉の外の縁は、浅いへこみ五つと、そのあとに立つ小さな炎のようなとげです。へこみは、葉の外に中心がある大きい円の弧です。へこみの端は小さい円につながり、縁はそこで上へ曲がって、とげの先になります。とげの丸い背は別の小さい円で、次のへこみへ続きます。最後のとげの下は、大きい円で下の端まで丸くつなぎます。内側の縁では、二枚の葉の間の白いすき間が、ハートの形四つの列になります。ハートの横の辺は、中心線の上の先から、小さい円の遠い側へ引いた直線です。小さい円の弧は、下を回って中心線まで続きます。そこで二枚の葉の先が合い、次のハートが始まります。まん中の白い葉脈は、中心線の向こう側の点を中心とする円に沿った白い線です。その両側に五本ずつ、まっすぐな白い葉脈を引きます。長さは、同じ中心の二つの円の間です。柄は、それぞれの葉の下の端から、斜めの直線に沿って続きます。左の葉の柄を上に重ね、交わるところでは、上の柄の両側に白い線を残します。柄と、もう一枚の葉との間にも白い線を残します。右の葉を先に作図し、左の葉をその場で作図してから、柄を作図します。

Measured: The 丸にだきひいらぎ of the 1892 crest book (frame 44, right page, row 2, column 1 from the right): the embracing pair of the cell above it inside a ring whose inner line is 0.84 of its outer radius. The pair fills the ring: its tip and its crossed stems touch the inner line, the midribs stand 0.37 of the outer radius from the axis at the middle, and the white between the leaves is the same column of four hearts. The book's ring cuts the leaves' outer edges, so that only the bays show, as drops of white along the ring; ours is the pair of 抱き柊, measured from the cell above (frame 44, row 1, column 1 from the right), set whole inside our ring with its flames, the left leaf the mirror of the right.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, right page, second row, first column counted from the right: 丸にだきひいらぎ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「丸にだきひいらぎ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

Three leaves and their knot stand at the top. Two mirrored clusters hang around a circle, with tapered rows of florets on each side of the white stem.

## Wisteria flower · 花藤 (`hana-fuji`)

A broad heart-shaped flower rises between two hanging sprays. Two wide leaves curve out above its shoulders.

二つの垂れた花房の間に、幅広い心形の花を置きます。花の肩の上から、二枚の幅広い葉が外へ曲がります。

## Two wisteria sprays · 二つ藤 (`futatsu-fuji`)

Two wisteria sprays form a wreath. Their curved stalks pass one another in the open centre.

Measured: Each leaf crown and curved raceme repeats by a half turn. The inner ends remain separate.

## Eight wisteria sprays · 八つ藤 (`yatsu-fuji`)

Eight paired sprays with plump florets turn around the four-petalled flower. Eight fine rays are cut from its heart.

丸みのある小花をつけた、対になった八つの花房が、四弁の花を囲みます。丸い中心から細い筋を八本白く抜きます。

Measured: The florets follow the same bowed profile as the hanging wisteria. Short diagonal rays alternate with four long rays at the heart.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 32, right page, third row, second column from the right: 八ッ藤 (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「八ッ藤」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

148 guides, 9 shapes, 215 steps.

## Naitō wisteria · 内藤藤 (`naito-fuji`)

A long oval leaf stands between two rounded shoulders. Two pairs of rows follow the white stems down to the low florets.

細長い楕円形の葉を、左右の丸い肩の間に置きます。小花の列が二組、白い花軸に沿って下の小花まで続きます。

Measured: The taller top leaf and rounder top shoulders distinguish this print from the hanging wisteria beside it.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 32, right page, fourth row, first column from the right: 内藤ふぢ (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「内藤ふぢ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

123 guides, 3 shapes, 234 steps.

## Ōkubo wisteria · 大久保藤 (`okubo-fuji`)

The character 大 stands above the three leaves between two rising sprays of wisteria.

上に伸びる二つの花房の間に三枚の葉を置き、その上に「大」の字を立てます。

Measured: Narrow petals leave room around the character. The two legs of 大 curve outward.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 32, right page, third row, first column from the right: 大久保ふぢ (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「大久保ふぢ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

134 guides, 4 shapes, 242 steps.

## One-leaf peony · 一葉牡丹 (`ichiyo-botan`)

A low peony head sits in front of a fan of pointed foliage. The round petals overlap in tiers, separated by narrow white curves. The points of the leaves rise above the head and spread outward to both sides.

尖った葉が扇のように広がり、その手前の低い位置に牡丹の花を置きます。丸い花弁を段ごとに重ね、細い白い弧で分けます。葉の先は花より高く立ち、左右にも広がります。

Measured: The printed crest is about 310 pixels across and 200 high. The low flower fills about three fifths of the width, with two large pointed leaves rising behind it. The two sides are mirrored in the drawing and the white petal curves regularised.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 9, left page, second row, first column from the right: 一葉ぼたん (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「一葉ぼたん」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

165 guides, 12 shapes, 266 steps.

## Large split peony · 大割り牡丹 (`owari-botan`)

Broad overlapping petals cover a round head in tiers. Each edge is formed from compass arcs. Lower lobes frame a round petal that stands in front near the foot of the flower.

幅広く重なる花弁が、丸い花全体を段ごとに覆います。縁はコンパスで描いた弧でつなぎます。下の裂片の間に、手前に出た丸い花弁を置きます。

Measured: The printed flower is about as high as it is wide. Its low front petal is about a third of the flower's width. The drawing reflects the lateral layers, rounds their overlaps and smooths the bottom corners.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 9, right page, third row, third column from the right: 大ワリぼたん (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「大ワリぼたん」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

161 guides, 15 shapes, 303 steps.

## Back-view peony · 裏牡丹 (`ura-botan`)

Four broad lobes turn round a pointed calyx. Fine arcing veins fan out along each lobe, and small outer lobes show through the gaps. A curved white cut marks the calyx stalk.

尖った萼のまわりに、幅の広い裂片を四枚並べます。各裂片には細い弧の筋が扇のように広がり、間から外側の小さな裂片がのぞきます。萼の茎には弧を描く白い切れ込みを入れます。

Measured: The printed head spans about 280 pixels. Four large ribbed sections and scalloped outer edges surround a small pointed calyx. The drawing regularises the four turns and spaces the ribs evenly, with a clear white line around the calyx.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 9, left page, third row, second column from the right: うらぼたん (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「うらぼたん」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

86 guides, 9 shapes, 123 steps.

## One pine · 一つ松 (`hitotsu-matsu`)

A cloud-shaped crown of five scallops over an arched branch, and a short trunk that spreads to a flat foot with an arch cut in each side. White needles fan up from a white midrib and from the branch, and end short of the crown's edge; white marks lie on the bark. Crown, branch and trunk are one shape each.

五つの丸い山がある、雲の形の松葉の茂みを、弓なりの枝の上に置きます。その下に、根元が平らに広がり、左右にアーチ形の切れ込みがある短い幹を置きます。白い松葉は、まん中の白い線と枝から上へ扇のように広がり、茂みの縁の手前で終わります。幹には白い模様を入れます。茂み、枝、幹を、それぞれ一つの形として作図します。

Measured: The 一ツまつ of the 1892 crest book (frame 30, left page, row 2, column 2 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of half the crown's width. The crown is a cloud of five scallops, 2 wide and 0.7 tall over the branch, the round lobe at each end 0.5 across. The branch arches under it, 0.09 thick with its lines. The trunk is 1.2 wide under the branch, 0.9 at its waist and 1.3 at its flat foot, 0.8 below the branch's top, with an arch 0.28 wide cut into the foot each side. The book draws it in outline, a little askew, with ten or eleven needles a side; ours is solid, as a crest is worn, the left half the mirror of the right, with ten white needles a side and two white marks at each of the four places the book marks the bark.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, left page, second row, second column counted from the right: 一ツまつ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「一ツまつ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

82 guides, 3 shapes, 113 steps.

## Embracing myoga · 抱き茗荷 (`daki-myoga`)

Two buds stand face to face, their backs one circle, with a white slit between them that opens into a drop; each ends in a round foot. A bud is three inner scales and four outer ones along its back, a white line between any two and a white lens outline on each, with a flower of four round petals on top. The right bud is built in full, then the left where it stands.

二つのつぼみを向かい合わせに置きます。二つの背中は一つの円になり、間の白いすき間は、下の方でしずく形に広がります。つぼみの下の端は丸いこぶです。つぼみのうろこは、内側に三枚、背中に沿って四枚で、間を白い線で分け、どのうろこにも白い線でレンズ形を一つ描きます。上には丸い花びら四枚の花を置きます。右のつぼみを作図し、左のつぼみはその場で作図します。

Measured: The だきめうが of the 1892 crest book (frame 42, right page, row 4, column 3), measured as numbers only, in units of the radius of the circle the two buds' backs make. The tops of the three inner scales cross the axis 0.67 and 0.32 above the centre and 0.06 below it: the first is an arc of radius 0.58, the other two of 0.94. The edges of the outer scales leave the back 38 and 16 degrees above the level line and 9 below it, as arcs of radius about 0.47, 0.66 and 0.66. The white drop between the buds is 0.4 wide and starts 0.05 below the centre; a foot is a knob of radius 0.09. The book draws the crest in outline: each scale has two or three strokes that meet at its tip, the flower has a ragged top, the wedge between the buds is wider on the right than on the left, and the knobs stand a little above the foot of the circle. Ours is solid, as a crest is worn: each scale's strokes are the white outline of a lens, the flower has four round petals, the two sides are mirrored, and the knobs stand on the circle's foot. A Wikimedia Commons picture of 抱き茗荷 (Japanese crest daki Myouga.svg) was read for comparison only: the same round back, layered scales with a mark on each, drop and knobs, with a plain slit and no wedges between the buds, and flowers that stand above the circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 42, right page, fourth row, third column: だきめうが (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目の三列目「だきめうが」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

63 guides, 2 shapes, 95 steps.

## Two flowering ginger shoots · 花抱き茗荷 (`hanadaki-myoga`)

Two arched ginger shoots frame an opening and carry round flowers low on either side.

丸い花を下につけた二本の茗荷で、中央の空きを囲みます。

Measured: Both flowers follow the same layout, reflected along with their stalks and broad bracts.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 42, right page, third row, third column from the right: 花抱めうが (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「花抱めうが」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

41 guides, 2 shapes, 56 steps.

## Embracing myoga in a circle · 丸に抱き茗荷 (`maru-ni-daki-myoga`)

The two buds inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, their backs a narrow white line from it.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、二つのつぼみを収め、背中を輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 42, right page, fourth row, third column counted from the right: だきめうが, drawn inside a ring (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「だきめうが」（輪に入った形）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

65 guides, 3 shapes, 101 steps.

## Quince blossom · 木瓜 (`mokko`)

Four lobes surround a flower with four petals. The lobes at the sides reach farther than those above and below. A broad arc and two round shoulders close against an inner arc on each lobe. Inside each one sits a narrow band whose arcs widen into rounded curls. Two ruled gaps part each petal from its neighbours, and a short white stamen points away from the round heart.

四枚の花びらを持つ花を、四つの裂片で囲みます。左右の裂片は上下よりも長く伸びます。各裂片は、外側の大きな弧と二つの丸い肩、内側の弧で囲みます。その内側には、両端が丸くふくらんで内側に巻く細い帯を置きます。花びらの間は定規で引いた白いすき間で分け、円い花の中心から外へ向かって、短い白いしべを入れます。

Measured: Frame 44: the height is about four fifths of the width. The flower reaches 0.38 of the half-width above and below and 0.48 at the sides. The outer lobes are cut back to 0.54 above and below and 0.62 at the sides. The curls follow the inside edge of each lobe. The white gaps and the opposite halves are evened out from the printed outlines.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, second row, first column from the right: 木瓜 (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

47 guides, 9 shapes, 81 steps.

## Chinese quince blossom · 唐木瓜 (`kara-mokko`)

Each of the quince blossom's four lobes ends in a large round scallop with a smaller one on either side. Three circles make the scallops. An arc cuts their inner edge to clear the curled band and flower.

木瓜の四つの裂片は、それぞれ大きな丸いふくらみ一つと、その両側の小さなふくらみ二つで終わります。ふくらみは三つの円で作り、内側は弧で切って、巻いた帯と花のためのすき間を残します。

Measured: Frame 44: each of the four outer lobes has a round centre tip and rounded shoulders. The upper and lower tips reach about four fifths as far as the side tips. The longest axis is horizontal, as in the plain quince.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, second row, second column from the right: 唐木瓜 (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「唐木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

47 guides, 9 shapes, 81 steps.

## Sword quince blossom · 剣木瓜 (`ken-mokko`)

Four blades fall between the four lobes of the quince. A blade is narrow at the heart and widens into two curved shoulders before the point. The bands and petals stop a white gap short of its edges.

木瓜の四つの裂片の間に四本の剣を置きます。剣は花の中心で細くなり、二つの曲がった肩へ広がって尖ります。帯と花びらは、剣の縁から白いすき間を残して止めます。

Measured: Frame 44: the four blades point diagonally between the lobes. The shoulders are about one third of the motif radius across and the tip reaches about 0.88 of the radius. The blade edges and the gaps beside them are cut on circles struck from the same centres.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, third row, second column from the right: 剣木瓜 (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「剣木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

48 guides, 9 shapes, 82 steps.

## Upright quince blossom in a circle · 丸に立ち木瓜 (`maru-ni-tachi-mokko`)

Four round petals meet at the centre of a ring. Each petal has three nested white arcs and narrows between two ruled seams. The side petals are longer than the upper and lower pair.

輪の中心で四枚の丸い花びらを合わせます。各花びらには三重の白い弧があり、定規で引いた二本のすき間の間で細くなります。左右の花びらは上下より長く伸びます。

Measured: Frame 44: the side petals reach about four fifths of the ring radius and the upright pair reaches about 0.86 of the side pair. The three nested arcs on each petal rise roughly 0.23, 0.36 and 0.56 of the side reach. Their white width is regularised to three units at full motif size.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, fourth row, second column from the right: 丸に立木瓜 (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「丸に立木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

19 guides, 5 shapes, 40 steps.

## Plum bowl in a lobed frame · 木瓜に梅鉢 (`mokko-ni-ume-bachi`)

Place five round petals around a small central disc. Short filaments stand in the gaps around the heart. Each petal has a rounded lobe and a hooked band beyond it, and the five outer pieces stand one fifth of a turn apart.

小さな円を囲んで丸い花びらを五枚置き、中心との間に短いしべを入れます。各花びらの外側に丸い裂片と両端を巻いた帯を置き、外の五組を5分の1回転ずつ離します。

Measured: Five circular petals sit around a small round heart with short filaments. The petals are about 60 pixels across, and their centres stand about 56 pixels out. Five broad lobes with curved shoulders surround them, and a band curls at both tips inside each lobe. The top lobe is centred above a round petal.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 28, right page, row 2, second column from the right: 瓜ニ梅ばち (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「瓜ニ梅ばち」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

26 guides, 11 shapes, 48 steps.

## Oda quince · 織田木瓜 (`oda-mokko`)

Five lobes round a band round a flower. A lobe's outer edge is the outer circle itself, between two round shoulders; each side is cut straight, down to a circle about the centre, so that two lobes meet in a point with a white line between them. Inside each lobe lies its piece of a thin band, with both edges opening into a curled bulb at each end. In the middle is a Chinese flower (karahana) of five petals: a petal has three round ends and a white stamen up its middle, a white line parts it from the next, and a white ring parts them all from the disc at the centre. The lobe on top and the petal on top are built in full, and the other four of each are built the same way where they stand, clockwise.

外側に五つの裂片、その内側に細い帯、真ん中に花を置きます。裂片の外の縁は外円そのもので、両方の肩を小さい円で丸くします。両脇はまっすぐに切り、中心のまわりの円まで下ろします。となりの裂片とは尖った形で出会い、間に白い線を残します。裂片の内側には、その裂片の分の細い帯を置き、帯の両端は、両縁が接する弧でふくらみ、内側に巻いて丸く終わります。真ん中は花びらが五枚の唐花です。花びらの先は三つの丸でできていて、花びらの真ん中に白いしべを一本入れます。となりの花びらとの間は白い線で分け、中心の円のまわりには白い輪を残します。上の裂片と上の花びらを先に作図し、それぞれ残りの四つは、同じ作り方でその場で時計回りに作図します。

Measured: The five-lobed quince of the 1892 crest book (frame 28, right page: the cell in the second row, second column, and the plain 窠 in the fourth row, third column), read along lines from the centre as parts of the outer radius. In both, the lobes' outer edges lie on one circle about the centre, and two lobes meet in a notch that reaches in to between 0.78 and 0.88; the band's two lines are at 0.68 to 0.69 and at 0.74; a petal's tip is at 0.57 to 0.62; and the flower has five petals of three round ends each, a stamen on each petal and a small ring at the centre. The book draws outlines only, so the widths of the white lines are set here: 0.04 between two lobes and between a lobe and the band, 0.02 between two petals. The Wikimedia Commons picture Oda emblem was read the same way, for comparison only: lobes from 0.72 to 1.00, the band from 0.64 to 0.68, petal tips at 0.60, the centre disc 0.09 in a white ring to 0.15, stamens to 0.31. The drawing has the lobes from 0.76 to the outer circle with shoulders of 0.22 and notches to 0.91, the band from 0.68 to 0.72, with both edges opening by tangent arcs into rounded curls, petal tips at 0.61, the centre disc 0.08 in a white ring to 0.14, and stamens to 0.30.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 28, right page, second row, second column: the five-lobed quince with a five-petalled flower, its label not read for certain (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目の二列目の五つ木瓜に唐花（名前の文字はまだ読み切れていません。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

26 guides, 11 shapes, 49 steps.

## Melon · 瓜 (`uri`)

Five broad lobes about the outside circle. Each lobe has two rounded shoulders and a curled band inside it. Five three-tipped petals, each with a short white stamen, meet around the small round heart.

外円に沿って五つの幅広い裂片を置きます。各裂片には二つの丸い肩があり、その内側に両端を巻いた帯を置きます。短い白いしべと三つの丸い先端を持つ花びらが五枚あり、小さな円い中心を囲みます。

Measured: Frame 28: five rounded lobes are contained by the outside circle. The curled bands reach about three quarters of the outside radius and the flower reaches three fifths. The heart is about one tenth of the outside radius. The paper between the five petals and round the heart has been evened out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 28, right page, fourth row, second column from the right: くわ (窠), the five-lobed melon (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「くわ」（窠、五つの裂片を持つ瓜）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

26 guides, 11 shapes, 49 steps.

## Three melons · 三つ瓜 (`mitsu-uri`)

Three broad lobes on the compass circle, with one at the top. A curled band and a small three-tipped petal sit inside each lobe. The petals share a round heart.

外円に沿って三つの幅広い裂片を並べ、うち一つを上に置きます。各裂片の内側には巻いた帯と三つの丸い先端を持つ小さな花びらを置き、花びらは円い中心を共有します。

Measured: Frame 28: the upper lobe and its partners reach the outer circle. The curled bands lie around 0.52 of its radius and the tips of the three inner petals reach about 0.43. The three axes stand 120 degrees apart. The shoulders of the top lobe are set farther apart, as in the print. Two tangent arcs widen each end of the inner band into a hooked bulb.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 28, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 三ッ瓜 (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「三ッ瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

29 guides, 7 shapes, 55 steps.

## Quince blossom in a hut · 庵木瓜 (`iori-mokko`)

Two ruled slopes meet over a level eave. Two rectangular posts descend from the eave, and the four-lobed quince sits low between them.

定規で引いた二本の斜線を水平な軒の上で合わせます。軒の下に長方形の柱を二本置き、その間の低い位置に四つの裂片を持つ木瓜を収めます。

Measured: Frame 44: the roof is a little over three times as wide as it is high. Each post is about one seventh of the roof width and about one and a third times its height. The quince hangs almost from the eave and nearly reaches the feet of the posts. The lobe that points up reaches a little farther than the one that points down. A narrow white gap is kept against the eave and the posts.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, third row, third column from the right: いほりに木瓜 (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「いほりに木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

74 guides, 12 shapes, 116 steps.

## Quince blossom in a circle · 丸に木瓜 (`maru-ni-mokko`)

The four-lobed quince set inside a circular band. Its upper and lower lobes are slightly fuller than in the plain crest. The circle and the four lobes share a centre.

四つの裂片を持つ木瓜を円い輪の中に収めます。上下の裂片は、輪のない木瓜よりわずかに高く伸びます。輪と木瓜の中心をそろえます。

Measured: Frame 44: the ringed flower is about 0.88 as high as it is wide. Its side lobes reach about 0.84 of the printed outer radius. The construction uses the package's medium ring and leaves the 3.5-unit white gap at the side tips. The upright pair is fuller, at 0.94 of the motif's width, to fill the heavier ring while keeping the horizontal axis longer.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, third row, first column from the right: 丸に木瓜 (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「丸に木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

48 guides, 10 shapes, 86 steps.

## Quince blossom with vines · 蔓木瓜 (`tsuru-mokko`)

Around a four-petalled flower, the four quince lobes carry their curved bands. Straight vine stems run diagonally out from the heart, with pairs of small curls at their tips.

四枚の花びらを持つ花のまわりに、内側に曲がった帯を持つ四つの裂片を置きます。中央から斜めに直線の蔓を伸ばし、それぞれの先を二つに巻きます。

Measured: The ordinary four-lobed arrangement has two vines crossing diagonally through the middle. Each vine runs into the space between the lobes and splits into a pair of recurved ends.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, row 2, third column from the right: つる木瓜 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「つる木瓜」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

69 guides, 13 shapes, 110 steps.

## Hotta quince blossom · 堀田木瓜 (`hotta-mokko`)

An upright quince with four white cutouts in a black disc. The end lobes reach almost to the outer circle. Between the four curled bands is a tall flower with a round centre.

黒い円の中に四つの白い抜きを入れ、木瓜を縦長に描きます。上下の裂片は外円の近くまで伸びます。四つの巻いた帯の間には、円い中心を持つ縦長の花を置きます。

Measured: Frame 44: the side cutouts extend a little over four fifths of the outside radius, and the upright pair nearly reach the rim. The side cutouts are tall with rounded ends. The inner flower has longer vertical petals and a circular heart. The upper and lower margins are regularised where the print meets the cell border.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 44, left page, first row, first column from the right: 堀田木瓜 (左頁、一段目、右から一列目「堀田木瓜」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

62 guides, 10 shapes, 101 steps.

## Bamboo grass and gentian · 笹竜胆 (`sasa-rindo`)

Five bamboo-grass leaves fanned below, the middle one upright with its midrib, and above them three gentian flowers, the middle one the largest, each a bell of five pointed petals. The left half is drawn from the book and mirrored about the axis.

下に笹の葉を五枚扇状に広げ、中央の葉は立てて中肋を入れます。その上に竜胆の花を三つ、中央を最も大きく、五枚の尖った花弁の鐘形に置きます。左半分を紋帳から写し取り、中心線で左右対称に返します。

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, third row, first column counted from the right: 笹りんどう (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「笹りんどう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

0 guides, 4 shapes, 0 steps.

## Gentian in seven treasures · 七宝竜胆 (`shippo-rindo`)

Four gentian flowers sit at the quarter points of a circle. Curved strands between neighbours form the body of the seven-treasures pattern, with a four-petalled flower inside.

円を四分した位置に竜胆の花を置きます。隣り合う花を弧で結んで七宝の形を作り、中央に四枚の花びらを置きます。

Measured: The outer circle is about 301 pixels across and the four border flowers reach a little beyond it. The centre flower spans about 151 pixels between its tips. The layout uses a circle 170 across and a centre flower 90 across. The book’s four curved divisions become compass arcs, with the border flowers placed on the axes.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, fourth row, second column from the right: 七宝りんだう (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「七宝りんだう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

72 guides, 14 shapes, 103 steps.

## Gentian lozenge · 竜胆菱 (`rindo-bishi`)

Two small pointed flowers stand inside a lozenge. Each joins an angled stem. Two long lance-shaped leaves meet on the upright axis, with a white vein inside each.

菱の中に先のとがった小花を二つ置き、斜めの茎につなげます。縦の軸で細長い葉二枚を合わせ、各葉に白い脈を入れます。

Measured: The print spans about 306 pixels by 245. Each flower is about 80 pixels across, and the two central leaves run from the middle towards the top and bottom points. The layout uses 192 by 154, flowers approximately 48 across and an opposing pair of lens-shaped leaves. The two halves are related by a half-turn.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, first row, fourth column from the right: りんだうびし (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「りんだうびし」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

58 guides, 7 shapes, 78 steps.

## Two gentians in a circle · 丸に二つ竜胆 (`maru-ni-futatsu-rindo`)

Two pointed gentian flowers face in opposite directions. Two lens-shaped leaves extend sideways from the round heart. White veins divide the flowers and leaves inside the ring.

先のとがった竜胆の花二輪を反対向きに置きます。丸い中心からレンズ形の葉を左右に伸ばします。輪の中で、白い脈が花と葉を分けます。

Measured: The printed ring is about 300 pixels in diameter. Each flower spans roughly 131 pixels, and the side points of the leaves are about 240 apart. The drawing uses the package medium ring, two flowers each about 71 across and a pair of leaves that reach to 73 from the centre. The fine central veins are white.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, third row, second column from the right: 丸に二ッりんだう (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「丸に二ッりんだう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

52 guides, 8 shapes, 76 steps.

## Three-part gentian in a thread ring · 糸輪に三つ割り竜胆 (`itowa-ni-mitsu-wari-rindo`)

Three flowers face the centre. Straight stems reach to rounded buds near the thread ring. Two long veined leaves lie beside each bud, following the round edge.

三輪の花を中心へ向けます。直線の茎を糸輪の近くの丸いつぼみまで伸ばし、各つぼみの両側に、脈の入った長い葉を輪に沿わせます。

Measured: The cell's ring spans about 302 pixels. The upper flower is about 120 pixels wide and the axis of its stem about 138 pixels from the rim to the inner point. The drawing uses a thread ring, a flower about 70 across, and three equal turns. The rounded buds and the two leaves beside each remain near the rim.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, first row, second column from the right: 糸ワニ三ッ割りんだう (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「糸ワニ三ッ割りんだう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

39 guides, 13 shapes, 57 steps.

## Three-leaf gentian · 三つ葉竜胆 (`mitsuba-rindo`)

Three pointed flowers alternate with three long leaves around a small disc. Each flower has a short stalk and white veins. A straight white vein divides each leaf.

先のとがった花三輪と長い葉三枚を、丸い中心のまわりに交互に並べます。花には短い茎と白いしべを置き、葉の中央に定規で引いた白い脈を入れます。

Measured: The printed crest has an upper flower and an upright leaf below. The heart is about 49 pixels across. Each pointed leaf runs about 130 pixels from the edge of the heart. The layout uses a heart 26 across and a maximum leaf reach of 96, and regularises the three alternating pairs by equal turns.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, fourth row, first column from the right: 三ッ葉りんだう (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「三ッ葉りんだう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

50 guides, 10 shapes, 73 steps.

## Four gentians in a thread ring · 糸輪に四つ竜胆 (`itowa-ni-yotsu-rindo`)

Four diagonal flowers alternate with four straight-veined leaves around a round heart. The horizontal leaves are longer than the upright leaves. A thread ring surrounds them.

斜めに向けた花四輪と真っすぐな脈の入った葉四枚を、丸い中心のまわりに交互に並べます。左右の葉は上下の葉より長くし、全体を糸輪で囲みます。

Measured: The printed ring spans about 300 pixels. Side leaves run about 134 pixels out from the centre and the upright leaves about 110. The scale uses 84 and 72 on a ring of diameter 192. The four flowers have matching arcs and ruled rays, and the ring follows the printed qualifier 糸ワ.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, fourth row, third column from the right: 糸ワニ四ッりんだう (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「糸ワニ四ッりんだう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

57 guides, 14 shapes, 91 steps.

## Morning-glory gentian · 朝顔竜胆 (`asagao-rindo`)

Eight faceted petals surround a round heart. The white cuts are ruled, with narrow necks that spread towards the rim. Eight angular points stand outside the broad band.

角のある花びら八枚で丸い中心を囲みます。白いすき間を定規で引き、細い根元から外側へ広げます。太い輪の外には角のある先を八つ置きます。

Measured: The crest is about 307 pixels wide over the external points. The rim is about 253 pixels across, its centre opening about 207 and the heart about 40. The drawing uses a 192-wide layout with rim 156, field 128.4 and heart 23.6. Eight equal angular units replace the source's slight differences in length and alignment.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, right page, fourth row, fourth column from the right: あさがほりんだう (右頁、四段目、右から四列目「あさがほりんだう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

322 guides, 11 shapes, 352 steps.

## Front-facing rabbit · 真向き兎 (`usagi`)

A rabbit crouched and seen from the front. Its body is two equal circles side by side, which cross on the axis and leave a notch under the chin, with two ruled lines from the point of the back touching them. Each ear is the lens between two equal arcs, leaning out. The face is a circle drawn as a white line, with ringed eyes, the line of the nose, four ruled whiskers a side, and an arm coming round to a three-clawed paw.

正面から見た、うずくまる兎です。体は同じ大きさの円を二つ並べ、中心線上で交わらせて顎の下に切れ込みを作り、背の頂点から二本の直線を円に接するように引きます。耳は同じ半径の二つの弧にはさまれた形で、外へ傾けます。顔は白い線の円で、輪の目、鼻筋の線、左右四本ずつの直線のひげ、三本爪の前足へ回る腕の線を入れます。

101 guides, 1 shapes, 121 steps.

## Three rabbits · 三つ兎 (`mitsu-usagi`)

The three rabbits look left. Compass arcs describe each rounded back, nose and pair of swept ears. One rabbit stands in front of the next in a gently rising row, with a narrow white line along each overlap.

三羽の兎が左を向きます。丸い背と鼻、後ろへ流れる二本の耳を弧で描きます。少しずつ高くしながら手前から順に重ね、重なりには細い白い線を残します。

Measured: The three rabbits cover almost 300 pixels from the first forehead to the last rump. Their rounded backs repeat about 65 pixels to the right and slightly higher at each place. Each head has two swept ears. The front body shows the full nose and belly, and the two behind it show only the visible arcs.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 25, left page, row 3, first column from the right: 三ッうさぎ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「三ッうさぎ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

45 guides, 3 shapes, 68 steps.

## Three clams · 三つ蛤 (`mitsu-hamaguri`)

Three open clams turn about the centre. Compass arcs define both valves and their growth lines.

開いた蛤を三つ、中心のまわりに並べます。二枚の殻と成長線をコンパスの円弧で描きます。

Measured: The three shells share a hinge near the middle. The open edge is on the same side of each shell, and short growth marks run across its broad face.

32 guides, 3 shapes, 56 steps.

## Two clams in a circle · 丸に二つ蛤 (`maru-ni-futatsu-hamaguri`)

Two open clams lie across the circle, one high and one low. The near shell has crossing white growth marks.

開いた蛤を上下に一つずつ置き、細輪に収めます。手前の貝に、交差する白い成長線を入れます。

Measured: The lower shell is larger and its markings are more closely ruled. The upper shell keeps sparser growth marks.

## Three clams in a circle · 丸に三つ蛤 (`maru-ni-mitsu-hamaguri`)

Three clams, each with a narrow opening and short growth marks, turn inside a thin ring.

口が細く開き、短い成長線を入れた蛤を三つ、中心のまわりに並べ、細輪に収めます。

Measured: The three shells have rounder shoulders and fewer marks than the unframed three clams in the cell above. The narrow white pockets keep the shells distinct.

## Three scallop shells joined together · 三つ組合せ板屋貝 (`mitsu-kumiawase-itayagai`)

Three scallop shells open from their hinges towards the centre. The outer ribs are longer, leaving a three-sided opening between the shells.

板屋貝を三つ、蝶番から中心へ向けて広げます。端の筋を長くし、貝の間に三角形の空きを残します。

70 guides, 6 shapes, 112 steps.

## One scallop in a medium ring · 中輪に一つ板屋貝 (`chuwa-ni-hitotsu-itayagai`)

Eight plain ribs spread upwards from a rounded hinge inside a medium ring. Each rib widens towards a rounded end. A grooved ear hangs below the ribs at either side, with three narrow arc-shaped cuts in each ear.

中輪の中に、丸いちょうつがいから上へ広がる八本のすじを収めます。すじは丸い先に向かって広がります。左右には耳が下がり、それぞれ三本の細い弧の溝を入れます。

Measured: The cell has a ring about 295 pixels across, with an upright fan about 226 across at the shoulders. The fan has eight plain radial ribs about 15 degrees apart, two broad hanging ears and one rounded hinge below. The drawing uses the package's medium ring, eight ribs on a 15-degree pitch and three rounded grooves on each ear.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, right page, row 1, first column from the right: 中ワニ一ッいたや貝 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「中ワニ一ッいたや貝」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

46 guides, 5 shapes, 74 steps.

## Facing scallop shells · 向かい板屋貝 (`mukai-itayagai`)

Two scallops face one another at their hinges. The ribs alternate between round pits and short white seams.

板屋貝を二つ、蝶番を向かい合わせて置きます。筋には、丸いくぼみと短い白い線を交互に入れます。

Measured: The rounded side panels stay inside the circular outline and fold across the seam. Three growth lines cross each side panel.

82 guides, 6 shapes, 135 steps.

## Four scallop shells · 四つ板屋貝 (`yotsu-itayagai`)

Four scallops spread outward from a small opening. Each fan is made from rounded ribs marked with small pits and seams.

中心に小さな空きを残し、板屋貝を四方へ広げます。丸い先の筋に、小さなくぼみと白い線を入れます。

Measured: The four hinges point towards an opening, and the rounded tips of the fans form an almost circular perimeter.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, right page, fourth row, first column from the right: 四ッいたや貝 (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「四ッいたや貝」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

46 guides, 8 shapes, 74 steps.

## Back-to-back clams in a medium ring · 中輪に尻合わせ蛤 (`chuwa-ni-shiriawase-hamaguri`)

Three clams face outwards from a triangular opening inside a medium ring. The wide upper part of each shell is cut by four paired growth marks. A compound arc separates the two valves and a small arc marks the hinge.

中輪の中に蛤を三つ収め、三角の白を囲んで外を向けます。それぞれの殻の広い上側に、左右四本ずつ成長線を弧で入れます。弧をつないで二枚の殻を分け、小さな弧でちょうつがいを表します。

Measured: The cell shows three clams, with an outward arc in each group and three blunt backs pointing into the middle. The top clam is about 183 pixels wide and 99 high. Four curved growth marks show on either side of its middle, above a broad opening. The drawing regularises the three places and uses the package's medium ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, left page, row 1, fourth column from the right: 中ワニ尻合蛤 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から四列目「中ワニ尻合蛤」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

52 guides, 4 shapes, 77 steps.

## Turtle in an old character form · 古文字亀 (`komonji-kame`)

Circular arcs draw the turtle's head, the shoulders and the four swept feet inside a thin ring. Four small ruled openings are cut in each half of its shell. The two halves mirror across the upright axis.

細輪の内側に、円弧で亀の頭と肩、外へ広がる四つの足を作ります。甲羅の左右に直線で囲む小さな穴を四つずつ開けます。左右は中心線を軸に対称にします。

Measured: The ring encloses a turtle with a round head at the top and four broad feet sweeping away from the shell. The pair of upper feet have inward hooks. Eight ruled openings, four on each side, form the markings of the shell.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, left page, row 1, second column from the right: 古文字亀 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「古文字亀」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

56 guides, 2 shapes, 74 steps.

## Wild goose · 雁金 (`karigane`)

A goose flying, seen from above with its head in profile. Two circles of one width struck from below give the wings' upper edges and cross on the axis; two more give the hollows under the wings and meet in the point of the tail. The head is a circle on the axis; the eye is a dot, a ring and the cheek's arc about one centre. The beak is ruled from its point to the head and to the wing, and the collar is two white lines ruled from a point on the axis up to the wings.

上から見た、飛ぶ雁です。頭は横向きです。下から描いた同じ幅の円二つが翼の上の縁になり、中心線の上で交わります。別の円二つが翼の下のくぼみになり、尾の先で出会います。頭は中心線の上の円です。目は、一つの中心のまわりに置いた点と輪、それに頬の弧です。くちばしは、先から頭と翼へ直線を引きます。襟は、中心線の上の一点から翼へ引いた、白い線二本です。

31 guides, 1 shapes, 46 steps.

## Flying goose · 飛び雁 (`tobi-gan`)

The wings spread wide and the tail rises to a point above them. Across the left wing a curved cut marks the far side of the body. Two arcs bring the long neck down to the small head, with a ring around its eye and a ruled line in the beak.

左右に長い翼を広げ、その間から尾をとがらせて立ち上げます。左の翼に白い弧を入れて胴の向こう側を表します。長い首を二本の弧で下へ曲げ、小さな頭の目に輪、くちばしに直線を入れます。

Measured: The cell is about 310 pixels from wing tip to wing tip. The tail rises about 68 pixels above the left wing tip. The neck curves down and left, and the little beak lies about 150 pixels to the left of the tail tip. The eye is about 10 pixels above the point of the beak. The drawing keeps the wings staggered by 9 drawing units and carries the two curves of the neck down to the beak.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, row 2, first column from the right: とびがん (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「とびがん」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

28 guides, 1 shapes, 53 steps.

## Daizen wild geese · 大膳雁金 (`daizen-karigane`)

Circular arcs make a wild goose with two outspread wings and a curled tail. The second bird turns half a turn from the first. A narrow white space separates their rounded tails.

円弧で、翼を左右に伸ばし尾を巻いた雁金を作ります。もう一羽を半回転させて置き、二つの丸い尾を中央で向かい合わせ、間を細く白く残します。

Measured: Two wild geese turn half a turn apart, heads pointing out. Each has a narrow wing spread and a rounded tail that curls beneath the wing. The two tails meet at the middle, with a small round opening in each curl.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, row 3, first column from the right: 大ぜんかりがね (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「大ぜんかりがね」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

24 guides, 2 shapes, 44 steps.

## Five wild geese · 五つ雁金 (`itsutsu-karigane`)

Five copies of one goose turn a fifth of a turn apart. Each wing rises from the shared centre to a swept tip. A small head fits into the bend below the tip.

同じ雁金を五分の一回転ずつ回して五羽置きます。翼は中央に集まり、先を外側へそらします。翼が曲がる内側に小さな頭が入ります。

Measured: Five wings turn about the same point. Each narrows outwards to a swept tip, then curves back towards the centre. A small head on the inside of each wing faces inwards. One wing tip stands upright in the print.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, row 3, third column from the right: 五ッ雁金 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「五ッ雁金」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

23 guides, 5 shapes, 38 steps.

## Wild goose in a circle · 丸に雁金 (`maru-ni-karigane`)

Two compass arcs rise to the shoulders from the wing tips. Two lower arcs run in to the pointed tail. The head looks left over the folded collar, with a ring around its eye, an arc through the cheek and a ruled division in the beak.

翼の左右の先から肩に向かって弧を描き、下は二本の弧をしぼって尾の先で合わせます。折り返した襟の上の頭は左を向き、目に輪、頬に弧、くちばしに直線の切れ込みを入れます。

Measured: The ring spans about 300 pixels. The two wings spread to about 245 pixels at its middle height and slope in to the long tail directly below. The bird looks to the left. Its eye lies on the upright axis, about 60 pixels above the wing tips, and has a curved cheek passing round it. The drawing centres the wing tips, sets the eye and tail on the same axis and uses the package's medium ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, row 1, first column from the right: 丸ニ雁金 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「丸ニ雁金」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

29 guides, 2 shapes, 49 steps.

## Facing geese, beaks joined · 向かい嘴合い雁金 (`mukai-hashiai-karigane`)

The wild goose stood on end and set against its mirror image, so that the two beaks join on the centre. Each goose's wings run up and down: a circle struck from the side gives each wing's edge toward the other goose, and a wider one the hollow behind it, the two hollows meeting in the point of the tail at the side. The head is a circle on the level axis that all but touches the other head, with a ringed eye above a ruled beak, and the collar is two white lines. The left goose is struck first and the right one in its place as its mirror image.

雁金を縦に立て、左右対称に向かい合わせて、くちばしを中心で合わせます。翼は上下に伸びます。横から描いた円が、相手側の翼の縁になり、それより幅の広い円が翼の後ろのくぼみになって、二つのくぼみは、横にある尾の先で出会います。頭は横の中心線の上の円で、相手の頭にほとんど触れます。引いた直線のくちばしの上に、輪のある目を置き、襟は白い線二本です。左の雁を先に描き、右の雁は、その場所に左右対称に描きます。

28 guides, 2 shapes, 42 steps.

## Three geese in flight · 三羽雁金 (`sanba-karigane`)

Three geese descend from upper left to lower right. Each has one wing higher than the other, a ringed eye, and a long hollow under the higher wing that ends in the point of the tail. A curved white line across the body meets the short ruled division in the beak.

三羽の雁が左上から右下へ降りてきます。一方の翼を高く上げ、目に輪を入れます。高い方の翼の下を弧でくぼませ、尾をとがらせます。胴に入れた白い弧は、くちばしの短い直線につながります。

Measured: The top bird measures about 198 pixels from wing tip to wing tip and about 92 from crown to the lower shoulder. The birds repeat 37 to 43 pixels to the right and about 85 down, with the beaks still pointing left. The drawing spaces the three geese evenly and turns the lower two to follow their higher leading wings. Each has a ringed eye and pointed tail.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, right page, row 2, second column from the right: 三羽雁金 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「三羽雁金」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

67 guides, 3 shapes, 88 steps.

## Puffed sparrow · ふくら雀 (`fukura-suzume`)

A plump sparrow seen from the front, its wings and tail spread into a fan. The belly, the back and the fan are circles about one point on the axis. The tail is ten feathers, a thirtieth of a turn each, with a white line between any two; each wing is as wide as five feathers, with four white lines along it, and its lower edge is ruled from the same point. A white line parts the tail from each wing, another runs along the top of each wing to the head, and a white line goes round the top of the head down to the level line through its middle. The eyes are white discs with a dot, and the beak is a white triangle. The right half of the tail is built in full, then the body with both wings, then the left half of the tail where it stands.

正面から見た、ふっくらした雀です。翼と尾を扇のように広げています。腹、背、扇は、中心線の上の一点を中心にした円です。尾は羽根十枚で、一枚は一周の三十分の一、羽根の間に白い線があります。翼は羽根五枚分の広さで、白い線が四本あります。翼の下の縁は、同じ点から引いた直線です。尾と翼の間に白い線が一本、翼の上の縁から頭まで白い線が一本あります。頭の上半分のまわりにも白い線があり、頭の中心を通る横の線で終わります。目は点のある白い円、くちばしは白い三角です。尾の右半分を作図し、次に体と両方の翼を作図し、尾の左半分はその場で作図します。

Measured: The ふくらすゞめ of the 1892 crest book (frame 45, left page, row 1, column 2 from the right), measured as numbers only, in units of the radius of the fan of its wings and tail. The fan, the back and the belly are circles about one point: the back 0.59 and the belly 0.51 of the fan. The tail is ten or eleven feathers over the back; each wing has five lines along it and its lower edge runs from the fan back to the belly a third of a turn from upright. The head is a circle 0.26 in radius, its middle 0.1 over the fan's, drawn only round its top; the eyes are rings 0.06 in radius, 0.17 each side of the axis, and the beak is a triangle 0.18 wide and 0.12 tall. In its cell the fan's middle stands 0.22 under the middle of the whole bird; ours stands 0.1 of the layout circle under the crest's centre, so that the wing tips stay inside the circle. The book draws it in outline; ours is solid, as a crest is worn, the left half the mirror of the right, with ten tail feathers a thirtieth of a turn each, four white lines along each wing, white discs with a dot for the eyes and a white beak.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, left page, first row, second column counted from the right: ふくらすゞめ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「ふくらすゞめ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

52 guides, 3 shapes, 70 steps.
