## Vine sparrow · 蔓雀 (`tsuru-suzume`)

Three compass arcs make a single curled band. One broad lobe rises above two round lower lobes. The lower ends curve up beside the middle and finish in two rounded tips.

コンパスの弧三本をつないで、巻いた帯を一つ作ります。二つの丸いふくらみの上に、幅の広いふくらみを置きます。下の両端を中央のそばで上へ曲げ、先を丸くします。

Measured: A single open band makes three rounded lobes. The upper lobe forms a broad dome. Two low, almost circular lobes curl towards one another and end beside the upright axis. The whole shape is about 310 pixels across and about 264 high.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, left page, row 4, third column from the right: つるすゞめ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から三列目「つるすゞめ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 7 shapes, 31 steps.

## Crossed hawk feathers · 違い鷹の羽 (`chigai-takanoha`)

Two feathers of one size crossed at right angles, each half a right angle from upright, the one whose tip is at the upper right in front. A feather is two ruled sides and two half circles of its own width; a black shaft with a white line each side runs its length and out past the quill end; two bars cross it, each three white chevrons with their points on the shaft; and three fine arcs of down stand outside the quill end.

同じ大きさの鷹の羽を二枚、直角に交差させ、それぞれ垂直から45度傾け、先が右上を向く羽を手前にします。羽は、定規で引いた二本の辺と、羽と同じ幅の半円二つでできます。両側に白線のついた黒い羽軸が全長を通り、根元の先まで出ます。羽を横切る斑は二か所で、それぞれ羽軸の上に頂点を置いた三本の白い山形です。根元の外側には、細い弧の綿毛を三本添えます。

73 guides, 2 shapes, 88 steps.

## Crossed hawk feathers in a circle · 丸に違い鷹の羽 (`maru-ni-chigai-takanoha`)

The crossed feathers inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, their tips and the ends of their shafts a narrow white line from it.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に違い鷹の羽を収め、羽の先と羽軸の端を、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

75 guides, 3 shapes, 94 steps.

## One hawk feather in a circle · 丸に一つ鷹の羽 (`maru-ni-hitotsu-takanoha`)

One hawk feather standing upright, tip up, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick: the feather of the crossed pair, turned to stand.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、鷹の羽を一枚、先を上にして立てます。羽は違い鷹の羽の一枚を、立てたものです。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 21, right page, second row, second column counted from the right: 丸に一本鷹 (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「丸に一本鷹」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

46 guides, 2 shapes, 63 steps.

## Embracing hawk feathers · 抱き鷹の羽 (`daki-takanoha`)

Two hawk feathers bent round a round hole, their rounded tips meeting at the top and their quills crossing below. Each feather is a band about the middle, between circles struck about it: the outer vane, the shaft and the inner vane, with white lines between. Its tip is a circle of half the band's width, and its vanes are striped by pairs of ruled white lines that run from the shaft out towards the tip. The quill runs on from the shaft at the foot in a ruled bar with a rounded end, the left one over the right. The left feather is built first, and the right one is the same mirrored, built where it stands.

丸い穴のまわりに、二枚の鷹の羽を曲げて置きます。丸い羽先は上で向き合い、羽の根もとの軸は下で交わります。羽は、中心のまわりに描いた円の間の帯で、外側の羽弁、軸、内側の羽弁の三つに分かれ、その間に白い線があります。羽先は帯の半分の幅の円です。羽には、直線で引いた二本ずつの白いすじが、軸から外へ、羽先のほうに向かってななめに入ります。根もとの軸は、羽の下のはしから先が丸い直線の棒になってのび、左の軸が右の軸の上を通ります。左の羽を先に作図し、右の羽は同じものを左右反転させて、その場で作図します。

Measured: The だき鷹 of the 1892 crest book (frame 21, right page, fourth row, third cell from the right), measured in units of the layout circle 96, the cell 1.61 pixels to a unit, the middle the centre of the feathers' outer edge. The two feathers are a band about the middle: the outer vane from 80 to 95, the shaft from 72 to 78, the inner vane from 53 to 70 round the round hole, the white lines between them 2 wide. Each feather's tip is a circle of 21, half the band, its centre on the band's middle circle 17 degrees to its side of the top; the vanes end 165 degrees round from the top, and the quill runs on from the shaft across the axis to a rounded end 91 below the middle and 15 to the other side, the left quill over the right. Each vane is striped by pairs of white lines 9 apart at 38 and 102 degrees from the top, and the outer vane again at 158, leaning 30 degrees off the radius so that from the shaft out they run towards the tip. The left feather is built and the right one is its mirror image; the book's two differ by hand. The book writes 鷹 (or たか) for 鷹の羽 throughout: on the same page 丸に一本たか is 丸に一つ鷹の羽 and 丸に二本たか is 丸に並び鷹の羽, so the label だき鷹 is 抱き鷹の羽, read だきたかのは. No picture of the crest was found on Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 21, right page, fourth row, third cell from the right: だき鷹 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「だき鷹」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

61 guides, 2 shapes, 76 steps.

## Two hawk feathers side by side in a circle · 丸に並び鷹の羽 (`maru-ni-narabi-takanoha`)

Two hawk feathers standing upright side by side, tips up, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, each the feather of the crossed pair, turned to stand.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、鷹の羽を二枚、先を上にして並べて立てます。羽はどちらも、違い鷹の羽の一枚を立てたものです。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 21, right page, second row, first column counted from the right: 丸に二本鷹 (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「丸に二本鷹」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

50 guides, 3 shapes, 68 steps.

## Plover · 千鳥 (`chidori`)

A plump plover in flight, looking to the left, set out as the drawing manuals do: compass circles of set widths, each placed against the ones before, and the outline inked from their arcs. The head, the wing and the belly are three circles, and one long arc runs down the breast to the tail. Four circles are struck from outside: the saddle of the back, which touches the head and the wing; the bite under the wing tip; the notch of the tail; and the throat, through the point of the beak. The beak's upper edge is ruled from its point to touch the head. The eye is a white disc, the wing's line the white crescent between two circles, and each of the four claws the crescent between two circles that cross at its point.

左を向いて飛ぶ、ふっくらした千鳥です。作図の本のとおり、決まった幅のコンパスの円を、前の円に合わせて順に置き、その弧をなぞって輪郭にします。頭、翼、腹は三つの円で、胸から尾までは長い弧一本です。外から描く円は四つあります。頭と翼に接する背のくぼみ、翼の先の下の切れ込み、尾の切れ込み、くちばしの先を通るのどです。くちばしの上の線は、先から、頭の円に接する直線にします。目は白い円、翼の線は二つの円の間に残る白い三日月で、四本の爪は、それぞれ先で交わる二つの円の間の三日月です。

Note: Its construction re-strikes the compass construction shown in a modern crest-drawing manual; it will be rebuilt from a public-domain source when one with a plain plover is found. (この千鳥の作図は、現代の家紋の作図の本にあるコンパスの作図と、同じ手順で描き直したものです。飾りのない千鳥がのっている、著作権の切れた資料が見つかったら、それをもとに作り直します。)

24 guides, 3 shapes, 48 steps.

## Swallowtail butterfly · 揚羽蝶 (`agehacho`)

Seen from the side, every edge an arc of a struck circle or a ruled line. The near forewing stands upright behind a ruled front edge: a band of four round lobes with three eyes, each a ring with two white arcs under it, and at the band's foot three scallops, each a pair of white lines. Below the band the veins are white lines ruled from the centre of the head, a long one to each point of the scallops and a short one between. The far forewing shows at the left with two eyes and a pointed tip. The hindwing lies behind at the right: its veins are arcs of one compass width, their centres stepped along a line, its rim is cut in three deep scallops, and above it stand two mitten tails, each a long finger and a short thumb, a white hole in the lower palm. One feeler curls above the far wing, its stem down the front edge; the other runs along the far wing's foot to a small hook. The head is a dome inside two rings, with a ringed eye; three legs bent at the knee, two blades and three bars are drawn below. Each part is built in turn where it stands: the far forewing first, then the hindwing and the near forewing, then the feelers, the legs, the blades and bars, and the head last.

横から見た姿で、どの辺も、コンパスで描いた円の弧か、定規で引いた直線です。手前の前翅は、直線の前縁の後ろに立てます。丸い山を四つ並べた帯に目玉模様を三つ（輪と、その下の白い弧二本）入れ、帯の下の縁は、白い線二本ずつの波形を三つ並べます。帯の下の翅脈は、頭の中心から引いた白い線で、波形の角へ長い線を一本ずつ、その間に短い線を入れます。左に、目玉模様二つと尖った先のある奥の前翅がのぞきます。右奥の後翅の翅脈は、同じコンパス幅の弧で、中心を一本の線に沿ってずらして描き、縁を深い波形三つに切り、上に、長い指と短い親指のあるミトンの形の尾を二つつけます。下の尾の手のひらには、丸い白い穴をあけます。触角は、一本を奥の翅の上で巻いて、軸を前縁に沿わせ、もう一本を奥の翅の下の縁に沿わせて小さく曲げます。頭は二重の輪の中の半円で、輪のある目を入れます。下に、ひざで曲がった脚三本、葉の形を二つ、棒を三本描きます。奥の前翅、後翅、手前の前翅の順に、それぞれその場で作図します。そのあと、触角、脚、葉の形、棒を作図し、頭は最後に作図します。

Measured: Proportions measured, as numbers only, from a present-day drawing of 丸に揚羽蝶 and checked against the 1892 crest book (frame 34, 上羽てふ and 丸に上羽てふ); no drawing or outline from either is included. In the drawing the butterfly reaches 0.79 to 0.81 of its ring's outer radius in most directions, as ours does at 0.79. Its tails are two mittens: a finger about 0.08 of that radius wide with a round end and a thumb about 0.07 across, the lower finger's thumb on its outer side; a white bay between the tails, and a round white hole in the lower palm. Its three legs bend at the knee, the shin about 0.11 of the radius long, the thigh short. The book's hindwing rim is wavy, in several scallops; ours has three, cut in deep.

225 guides, 11 shapes, 298 steps.

## Small butterfly · 小蝶 (`kocho`)

Four rounded wings spread around a pointed body. Four white veins fan out in each upper wing, two in each lower wing, and the antennae end in compass curls above the eyes.

先のとがった胴のまわりに丸い羽を四枚置きます。上の羽に四本ずつ、下の羽に二本ずつ白いすじを入れ、目の上から伸びる触角の先を弧で巻きます。

Measured: The wings span about 279 pixels. The small upper body sits close to the level of the wing roots, below two antennae that sweep up and out and curl at their ends. The upper wings have four large veins apiece, while the lower ones have two shorter curves. The wings and the two pairs of white lines are made symmetric.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 34, right page, row 1, second column from the right: こてふ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「こてふ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

96 guides, 3 shapes, 133 steps.

## Knotted butterfly · 結び蝶 (`musubicho`)

Bands of compass arcs cross a ruled upright stem and loop round to form two broad wings and two smaller lower wings. Two outer curls and two shorter inner curls spread above them.

コンパスの弧で描いた帯を縦の直線に交差させ、左右に広い羽を二枚、下に小さな羽を二枚作ります。上には、外側の渦を二つと、それより短い内側の渦を二つ置きます。

Measured: The print is about 278 pixels wide and 200 high. It has four lobes formed by thin, continuous lines. The two large lobes rise at the sides, the two lower lobes meet at the tip of the upright stem, and four ends curl upwards. The drawing uses the same intersections and mirrors the two sides.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 34, left page, row 2, second column from the right: むすびてふ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「むすびてふ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

110 guides, 1 shapes, 143 steps.

## Swallowtail butterfly in a circle · 丸に揚羽蝶 (`maru-ni-agehacho`)

The same swallowtail, struck from the same circles made smaller, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick with a narrow gap inside it.

同じ揚羽蝶を、同じ円を縮めて描き、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、狭い隙間を残して収めます。

227 guides, 12 shapes, 304 steps.

## Thin embracing antlers · 細抱き角 (`hoso-daki-tsuno`)

Join short compass arcs from the rounded base through the outer tines and inward towards the top. Turn the inner tines back to the centre line. Mirror the antler across the upright axis.

丸い根元から外側の枝を通って頂部へ向かう短い円弧をつなぎます。内側の枝を中心線へ返し、もう一方を左右対称に置きます。

Measured: The side tines rise in two heights. The inner tines meet on the centre line around two pointed openings. The outlines are narrow and the round bases touch.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 23, right page, row 3, second column from the right: 細だき角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「細だき角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

72 guides, 2 shapes, 114 steps.

## Crane in a circle · 鶴の丸 (`tsuru-no-maru`)

A crane seen from the front, its wings raised round a round window so that their tips cross over the top of it. In the window the head looks to the left: a circle with a ringed eye and three white slivers at its back for its crest of feathers, a beak of two ruled edges with the white line of the mouth between them, and a neck that is the band between two circles about one point, the inner one touching the head, with a white line down its front into the breast. The white lines between a wing's long feathers are four arcs about one point on the other side of the axis, so that the feathers follow the rim, and one feather of each wing crosses over the window to the other side. Six lines across the lower feathers are arcs about a point high on the other side, and one across the upper feathers an arc about a point low on the other side. The breast is six rows of round scales about the window's centre, each row over the next, with a white tick on each scale, and the tail is a lens at the foot with a white line down it. The right half is built in full, then the head and the neck, and the left half where it stands.

正面から見た鶴です。翼を上げて丸い窓を囲み、翼の先は窓の上で交差します。窓の中の頭は左を向きます。頭は円で、目は輪と点、後ろの冠羽は白い細い切れ込み三本です。くちばしは定規で引いた二本の辺で、間に口の白い線が一本あります。首は、同じ中心の二つの円の間の帯で、内側の円は頭に接します。首の前には、胸まで白い線が一本伸びます。片方の翼の長い羽の間の白い線は、中心線の反対側の一点を中心とする四本の弧で、羽は外の円に沿います。それぞれの翼から羽が一枚ずつ、窓の上を越えて反対側へ渡ります。下の羽を横切る六本の線は、反対側の高い一点を中心とする弧で、上の羽を横切る一本の線は、反対側の低い一点を中心とする弧です。胸は、窓の中心のまわりに並ぶ丸いうろこ六列で、上の列が下の列に重なり、うろこにはそれぞれ白い切れ込みが一つあります。尾は下の端のレンズ形で、中に白い線が一本あります。右半分を作図してから、頭と首を作図し、左半分はその場で作図します。

Measured: The 鶴の丸 of the 1892 crest book (frame 21, left page, row 3, column 4 from the right), read on a grid as parts of the outer radius: a crane from the front whose wings rise from the body in curved feathers that follow the rim, the outermost of each wing crossing over the other about 0.78 up; a head that looks left, its top 0.63 up, its eye 0.08 left and 0.50 up and its beak pointing down to 0.40 left and 0.24 up; a breast of scales; and a tail and feet at the foot. The book draws it by hand, in outline, and not quite alike on the two sides. A modern drawing on Wikimedia Commons (Japanese crest Tsuru no Maru.svg) was measured for comparison only, for how the crest reads: a round window of radius 0.51 about a point 0.40 above the centre; the eye 0.05 left and 0.67 up and the beak's point 0.40 left and 0.42 up; the white lines between a wing's long feathers 0.61, 0.73 and 0.86 out from the axis at 0.30 up, and lines across the lower feathers that run in from the rim nearly level. The drawing follows the two where they agree, and the modern drawing where they differ: the head stands higher and the beak nearly level, and the tips cross just over the window. It makes the two wings mirror images, sets the scales in even rows, and leaves out the feet.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 21, left page, third row, fourth column counted from the right: 鶴の丸 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から四列目「鶴の丸」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

143 guides, 3 shapes, 189 steps.

## Dragonfly · 蜻蛉 (`tonbo`)

A long body on the centre line, a round head, and two pairs of long wings spread across it, the lower pair a little shorter.

中心線上に細長い胴を置き、丸い頭をつけ、左右に長い翅を二対、広げます。下の翅はやや短くします。

14 guides, 8 shapes, 34 steps.

## Three lightning spirals · 三つ稲妻 (`mitsu-inazuma`)

A regular hexagon drawn as a cube, its three faces round the middle. The hexagon's corners are marked by stepping the compass round the outer circle from the top; its rim is the band between two hexagons 5 apart, and the three edges that part the faces run from the middle to every second corner. Each face holds a square spiral, a band 5 wide whose runs lie along the face's two edges, that starts on one edge and turns in three turns to a short hook. The spiral of the top face is built in full and the other two where they stand, a third and two thirds of a turn round.

正六角形を、三つの面が中心に集まる立方体の形に描きます。六角形の角は、外円の上でコンパスを12時から時計回りに進めて印をつけます。縁は、5ずつ離した二つの六角形の間の帯で、面を分ける三本の線は、中心から一つおきの角へ引きます。どの面にも、幅5の四角い渦を入れます。渦の帯は面の二つの辺に沿って走り、一つの辺から始まって三回曲がり、短いかぎ形で終わります。上の面の渦を先に作図し、あとの二つは3分の1回転と3分の2回転まわった位置に、その場で作図します。

Measured: The 三ッいなづま of the 1892 crest book (frame 6, left page, row 3, third cell from the right), measured from the book's strokes mapped into the top face's own coordinates (one step along each of its two edges; the cube is a regular hexagon, so the faces are rhombi with a 120 degree angle at the middle): the spiral's runs lie at 0.20, 0.835, 0.87, 0.225, 0.425, 0.685 and 0.69 of an edge, ending 0.40 along, and the double line that draws each run is about 0.04 of an edge apart. The three faces are the same spiral turned a third of a turn. No picture of the crest was found on Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, third row, third cell from the right: 三ッいなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目の右から三つめ「三ッいなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

67 guides, 4 shapes, 79 steps.

## Three lightning spirals in a circle · 丸に三つ稲妻 (`maru-ni-mitsu-inazuma`)

A thin ring encloses three diamond-shaped faces, one upper face and two below. A broad ruled spiral fills each face, with white gaps between the turns and between the faces. The two lower faces are placed a third and two thirds of a turn from the top face.

細い輪の中に菱形の面を三つ収め、上に一面、下に二面を置きます。どの面にも直線で太い渦を作り、巻いた帯の間と面どうしの間に白いすき間を残します。下の二面は上の面から3分の1回転と3分の2回転した位置に置きます。

Measured: The outer ring in the print spans about 290 pixels with a band about 14 pixels thick. The cube is about 249 pixels high. Each face contains the broad spiral band, measured in hundredths along its two axes, with the inner four runs near 25, 46, 52 and 56 from a corner. The drawing uses a cube layout radius of 82 inside a ring of the package's thin weight.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 4, third column from the right: 丸に三ッいなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から三列目「丸に三ッいなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

60 guides, 4 shapes, 74 steps.

## Four lightning lozenges in a thread ring · 糸輪に四つ稲妻菱 (`itowa-ni-yotsu-inazumabishi`)

Four lozenge cells sit inside a thread ring. Each holds a spiral where every straight run lies in one of the two directions of its edges. The cells are laid out in a low diamond with narrow spaces between them.

糸輪の中に菱形の区画を四つ置きます。各区画に四角く曲がる渦を入れ、すべての直線を菱形の辺と同じ二方向にそろえます。区画の間を細く白く残し、全体を横長の菱形にまとめます。

Measured: Four lozenge cells fill a low diamond about 258 pixels wide and 191 high. The outer ring is about 281 pixels across. Each cell encloses one angular spiral, with broad paper between its inner turns. The drawing keeps the height-to-width ratio near three quarters and uses the package's thread ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 2, third column from the right: 糸に四ッいなづま菱 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「糸に四ッいなづま菱」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

68 guides, 5 shapes, 82 steps.

## Lightning lozenge · 稲妻菱 (`inazumabishi`)

A broad band winds in from the bottom of a low lozenge, whose height is 0.73 of its width. All the sides run in the two directions of the lozenge. The band leaves a thin white gap between its turns and ends in a short hook.

高さが幅の0.73の横長の菱形で、太い帯を下から内へ巻き込みます。辺はどれも菱形の二つの方向にそろえます。巻いた帯の間には細い白いすき間を残し、中心近くは短いかぎ形で終えます。

Measured: The book's outlines span about 310 by 229 pixels, with the outer return near the lower tip and a central hook at about the level of the two side tips. The levels of the parallel runs were read along the two slants of the lozenge. The drawing uses a height 0.73 of its width and leaves white gaps between the adjacent runs.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 3, second column from the right: 稲妻菱 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「稲妻菱」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

26 guides, 1 shapes, 31 steps.

## Hexagonal lightning · 六角稲妻 (`rokkaku-inazuma`)

Mark the six corners of the outer hexagon by stepping the compass round the layout circle. Lines parallel to its sides bound the spiral band. It starts near the foot of the left side, winds inward and ends in a short hook. A narrow white gap separates the turns.

外円をコンパスで一周しながら、外側の六角形の六つの角に印をつけます。その辺に平行な直線で帯を作ります。帯は左の辺の下のほうから内へ巻き、中心近くは短いかぎ形で終えます。巻いた帯の間には細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The cell has a hexagon 312 pixels high with the left side cut near its lower end. Each edge of the band follows one of three directions. At the upper point, the boundaries of the white gaps lie at about 80 and 76, 57 and 53, and 34 and 30 of the drawing's 96-unit radius. The central hook starts in the lower part of the last turn.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 3, fourth column from the right: 六角いなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から四列目「六角いなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

52 guides, 1 shapes, 58 steps.

## Lightning square on its corner · 隅立て稲妻 (`sumitate-inazuma`)

The four corners of a square stand on the outer circle. The broad band starts at the bottom and turns inwards between lines ruled at 45 degrees. A narrow gap separates the turns. The inner return narrows before ending in a hook.

正方形を角で立て、四つの角を外円の上に置きます。太い帯は下から始まり、45度に引いた直線の間で内側へ巻きます。巻いた帯の間には白いすき間を細くあけます。内側では幅を狭め、かぎ形で終えます。

Measured: The book's outline spans about 325 by 300 pixels. Both sets of sides are close to 45 degrees. The turns step inward along two sets of parallel levels, with thin breaks and an inward-facing hook at the centre. The drawing regularises the nearly square outline and keeps the narrower inner return printed in this cell.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 4, second column from the right: 角立いなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「角立いなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

26 guides, 1 shapes, 31 steps.

## Lightning cross in a square on point · 隅立て十文字稲妻 (`sumitate-jumonji-inazuma`)

A square stands on one corner. The same bent seam turns with each side of the square. It works inward through straight runs and square turns. Four small squares open the centre.

正方形を隅立てに置き、同じ折れ曲がる白い筋を四辺に向きを変えて入れます。直線と直角だけで内側へ折り進めます。中央には小さな四角い穴を四つ置きます。

Measured: The square stands on one corner. Each ruled edge is broken about a third of the way along by a narrow angular seam. The four seams each curl into a broad hook and two finer fingers. Four tiny square openings sit in the middle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 1, third column from the right: 角立十もんじいなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「角立十もんじいなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

71 guides, 1 shapes, 76 steps.

## Four lightning spirals · 四つ稲妻 (`yotsu-inazuma`)

Four square cells in a block, two by two. The circle is struck about the middle and the lines that bound the cells and their thin frames and the runs of the spiral are ruled from side to side of it, each set together. The top left cell is built in full: a thin square frame, and inside it a spiral band 5 wide that starts on the cell's right edge, runs left, up, right, down, left, up and right, and ends in a short hook. The other three cells are the same cell turned a quarter turn, a half turn and three quarters of a turn round the middle, each built where it stands.

四つの四角い区画を、縦に二つ、横に二つ並べます。中心に円を描き、区画とその細い枠の辺と、渦の帯の辺にあたる直線を、円の端から端まで、平行な線ごとにまとめて引きます。左上の区画を先に作図します。細い四角い枠の中に、幅5の渦の帯を入れます。帯は区画の右の辺から始まり、左、上、右、下、左、上、右と進んで、短いかぎ形で終わります。ほかの三つの区画は、同じ区画を4分の1回転、半回転、4分の3回転まわした位置に、その場で作図します。

Measured: The 四ッいなづま of the 1892 crest book (frame 6, left page, row 1, leftmost cell), measured as numbers from the book's strokes, the block 132 across: each cell 64 across with 4 between them, a thin frame 2 wide, and in the top left cell the spiral band 5 wide, its runs at 10 to 15, 46 to 51, 48 to 53, 31 to 36, 23 to 28 and 21 to 26 from the cell's edges, ending 40 across. The other three cells are the first turned a quarter turn each time. No picture of the crest was found on Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, first row, fourth cell from the right (the leftmost): 四ッいなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目の右から四つめ（いちばん左）「四ッいなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

69 guides, 4 shapes, 81 steps.

## Five lightning spirals · 五つ稲妻 (`itsutsu-inazuma`)

Five lozenge-shaped cells stand one fifth of a turn apart, with a point of the first cell at the top. One straight-edged spiral lies in each cell. The seams remain white between the five spirals.

菱形の区画を5分の1回転ずつ回して並べ、最初の区画の先を上に向けます。各区画の中に直線で折れ曲がる渦を一つ入れ、五つの渦の間に白いすき間を残します。

Measured: Five pointed cells turn around the centre. One tip stands upright, and the five narrow seams widen slightly towards the rim. The lines inside each face follow the directions of its sides. The drawing places the five cells exactly one fifth of a turn apart.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 6, left page, row 2, fourth column from the right: 五ッいなづま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「五ッいなづま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

101 guides, 5 shapes, 114 steps.

## Rain clouds · 雨雲 (`ama-gumo`)

Two clouds that chase each other round an open middle, each ending in a curling head. The clouds are a mass of ink, and the book's own brush lines are the white lines in them, their edges arcs of circles struck in full and ruled lines. Where the book leaves the clouds' inner side open, four short arcs close it across the middle, each bowed a little into the cloud.

まん中をあけて、追いかけあう二つの雲を描きます。どちらの雲も、先がうずを巻く頭になります。雲は墨のかたまりで、本に描かれた筆の線が、その中の白い線になります。白い線のふちは、全部の円を描いたうえで、その弧と直線で作図します。本で雲の内側が開いているところは、雲の側へ少しふくらんだ四つの短い弧で、まん中との境を閉じます。

Measured: The 雨くも of the 1892 crest book (frame 27, right page, second row, the leftmost cell), taken from the book in the cell's own units, the layout circle 96: two clouds that chase each other round an open middle, each ending in a curling head, the one at the top and the other at the foot, with streaks of cloud along them. The book draws them in thin lines; ours is solid, like the other crests: the clouds, chosen by hand as the space inside their outline, are ink, and the book's lines are white gaps of their own width. The open middle is closed by four short arcs, each struck through the two ends it joins and bowed a little into the cloud, where the book leaves the clouds' inner side open. The outline is the book's lines vectorised and outlined in arcs and ruled lines to within about a unit. The reading あまぐも: the label 雨くも gives くも for 雲 and no kana for 雨, and a standard dictionary gives the word 雨雲 the one reading あまぐも. No Wikimedia Commons number was taken.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 27, right page, second row, the leftmost cell: 雨くも (the clouds' lines vectorised from the book and made the white lines in solid ink) (右頁、二段目の左はし「雨くも」（雲の線は本から写し取って墨の中の白い線にしたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

178 guides, 1 shapes, 320 steps.

## Nine luminaries · 九曜 (`kuyo`)

A large disc with eight smaller discs round it, each a little clear of its neighbours and of the large one.

大きな円のまわりに小さな円を八つ並べ、隣どうしも大きな円とも、わずかに離します。

8 guides, 9 shapes, 19 steps.

## Nine luminaries in a circle · 丸に九曜 (`maru-ni-kuyo`)

A large disc with eight smaller discs round it, each a narrow white from its neighbours and from the large one, laid out inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

大きな円のまわりに小さな円を八つ並べ、となりどうしの間にも大きな円との間にも細い白を残します。これを、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に割り付けます。

7 guides, 10 shapes, 21 steps.

## Nine luminaries in a square · 角九曜 (`kaku-kuyo`)

Nine discs of one size in three rows of three. A circle of the spacing of the centres is struck about the middle, and the upright and level lines through its crossings with the two axes are ruled full length; a disc of radius 0.49 of the spacing is struck on each of the nine crossings, so a narrow white lies between neighbours. The outermost points of the four corner discs stand on the outer circle.

同じ大きさの円を、三段に三つずつ、九つ並べます。中央に、円の中心どうしの間隔を半径とする円を描き、その円と縦横の中心線との交点を通る縦と横の直線を端から端まで引きます。九つの交点にそれぞれ、半径が間隔の0.49の円を置き、となり合う円の間に細い白を残します。四隅の円のいちばん外の点は、外円の上に置きます。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest kaku Kuyoh, as numbers in its own units: each disc 91.3 across the radius, the centres 186.8 apart (0.4888); the white between neighbours is 0.0075 of the crest's width.

17 guides, 9 shapes, 40 steps.

## Seven luminaries · 七曜 (`shichiyo`)

A disc with six more round it, all of one size: the compass is stepped round the circle of 0.675 from the top to mark the six centres, and each disc is 0.325 across the radius, so each stands a narrow white from its neighbours and from the middle one.

同じ大きさの円を七つ、一つを中央に、六つをそのまわりに置きます。半径0.675の円の上に、コンパスを同じ幅のまま12時から時計回りに進めて六つの中心を印します。円の半径はどれも0.325で、となりの円や中央の円との間に細い白を残します。

7 guides, 7 shapes, 18 steps.

## Seven luminaries in a circle · 丸に七曜 (`maru-ni-shichiyo`)

A disc with six more round it, all of one size, laid out inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick: the compass is stepped round the circle of the six centres from the top, and each disc stands a narrow white from its neighbours, from the middle one and from the ring.

同じ大きさの円を七つ、一つを中央に、六つをそのまわりに置き、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に割り付けます。六つの中心を通る円の上を、コンパスの幅を変えずに12時の位置から時計回りに進めて中心を決め、どの円も、となりの円、中央の円、輪との間に細い白を残します。

Measured: The seven discs are those of the seven luminaries (the Wikimedia Commons picture Shichiyoumon: discs 0.328 of the outer circle, the six 0.673 out), set inside the package's measured ring and white; no picture of the ringed crest was found to read them from.

8 guides, 8 shapes, 22 steps.

## Crescent moon · 三日月 (`mikazuki`)

A thin crescent: the disc of the layout circle with a slightly smaller circle cut from it, struck a little up and to the left of the centre, so that the horns nearly close.

細い三日月です。割付の円から、中心より少し左上に置いたやや小さい円を切り取り、両端がほとんど閉じる形にします。

2 guides, 1 shapes, 8 steps.

## Embracing waves in a circle · 丸に抱き波 (`maru-ni-daki-nami`)

A thin ring, and inside it two waves that rise on either side and curl in towards each other. Each wave is a mass of ink, and the book's own brush lines are the white lines in it, their edges arcs of circles struck in full and ruled lines. Where a line runs into the ring in the book, a narrow gap between two ruled lines carries its white on to the white inside the ring. The left wave is drawn first; the right wave is the same mirrored, built where it stands, and the two overlap a little on the middle so that no seam shows.

細い輪の中に、左右から立ち上がって中へ巻き込む二つの波を収めます。波は墨のかたまりで、本に描かれた筆の線が、その中の白い線になります。白い線のふちは、全部の円を描いたうえで、その弧と直線で作図します。本で線が輪までのびているところは、二本の直線の間の細いすき間で、白を輪の内側の白までつなぎます。先に左の波を作図し、右の波は同じものを左右反転させて、その場で作図します。真ん中では少し重ねて、継ぎ目が出ないようにします。

Measured: The 丸に抱き波 of the 1892 crest book (frame 23, left page, row 3, second cell from the right), taken from the book in the cell's own units, the ring's outer circle 96: two great waves, one to each side, that rise and curl over towards the middle in three curls each, with a pool of nested lines below them that opens upwards. The book draws them in thin lines; ours is solid, like the other crests: the body of each wave, chosen by hand as the space inside its outline, is ink, and the book's lines are white gaps of their own width. The white between the curls, above the top curls and below the lowest line of the pool stays white. The left half is outlined in arcs and ruled lines to within about a unit, and the right half is its mirror image, since the book's two halves differ by hand. The book's ring is a band of about 0.11 of the radius; ours is the package's thin ring. No Wikimedia Commons number was taken.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 23, left page, third row, second cell from the right: 丸に抱き波 (the wave's lines vectorised from the book and made the white lines in solid ink, the ring drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目の右から二つめ「丸に抱き波」（波の線は本から写し取って墨の中の白い線にし、輪はコンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

119 guides, 3 shapes, 201 steps.

## Three stars · 三つ星 (`mitsu-boshi`)

Three discs of one size set like the character 品, one above two: their centres are alternate points of the circle of 0.56, the compass stepped round it from the top, and each disc is 0.44 across the radius, so a narrow white lies between neighbours and all three touch the outer circle.

同じ大きさの円を三つ、品の字に並べます（上に一つ、下に二つ）。中心は、半径0.56の円の上に、コンパスを同じ幅のまま12時から時計回りに進めて印をつけた六つの点のうち、一つおきの三点です。円の半径は0.44で、となり合う円の間に細い白を残し、三つとも外円に接します。

6 guides, 3 shapes, 15 steps.

## Three stars in a circle · 丸に三つ星 (`maru-ni-mitsu-boshi`)

The three stars laid out in the room inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white between them and the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、細い白線を残して三つ星を割り付けます。

7 guides, 4 shapes, 19 steps.

## One bar over three stars · 一文字に三つ星 (`ichimonji-mitsuboshi`)

The character 一 as the brush writes it, over three discs of one size set like the character 品, one above two. The stroke's top is a ruled line and its underside a wide arc; its left end is a circle as large as a disc, cut slantwise above, and its right end a smaller circle with a slanting line under it; the top rises to a point at each end, in a hollow arc.

筆で書いた「一」の字を上に置き、その下に同じ大きさの円を三つ、品の字に並べます（上に一つ、下に二つ）。一の字は、上が定規の直線、下が大きな弧です。左の端は下の円と同じ大きさの円で、上を斜めの直線で切ります。右の端は少し小さい円で、下に斜めの直線を引きます。上の線は、両端でとがって上がります。

12 guides, 4 shapes, 28 steps.

## Three stars over one bar · 三つ星に一つ引両 (`mitsu-boshi-ni-hitotsu-hikiryo`)

Three discs of one size, 27 across the radius, set like the character 品, one above two, and a level bar under them, 71 to each side and 40 high. The centres of the discs are (0, 68), (32, 15) and (-32, 15); the bar lies between the level lines at -24 and -64, and its corners stand inside the layout circle.

同じ大きさの円を三つ、品の字に並べ（上に一つ、下に二つ）、その下に横長の棒を一本置きます。円の半径は27で、中心は (0, 68)、(32, 15)、(-32, 15) です。棒は -24 と -64 の水平線の間にあり、中心から左右に71、高さは40で、四つの角は外の円の内側にあります。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 10, right page, third row, first column counted from the right: 三ッほし一ッ引 (three discs over one bar; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「三ッほし一ッ引」（三つの円の下に棒が一本ある図。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

7 guides, 4 shapes, 18 steps.

## Three mountains · 三つ山 (`mitsu-yama`)

Three equal facets fill a circle around a small triangular opening. Each facet has an outer compass arc and two ruled sides. A smaller arc makes one side of the opening.

小さな三角の白を囲んで、同じ大きさの山を三つ円に収めます。各山の外はコンパスの弧、両脇は直線で描き、白に面する辺にも小さな弧を使います。

Measured: Three broad facets fill a circle about 288 pixels across. The straight runs meet a small triangular opening with curved sides. The point at the foot of this opening is about 17 pixels below its upper edge. The drawing spaces the three parts in exact thirds with one standing at the top.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, right page, row 3, first column from the right: 三ッ山 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「三ッ山」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

4 guides, 3 shapes, 11 steps.

## Five mountains · 五つ山 (`itsutsu-yama`)

Five equal facets fill the circle. Narrow ruled seams lead towards a five-sided opening. The five inner edges are compass arcs, and the upper facet turns slightly towards the right.

同じ大きさの山を五つ円に収めます。細い直線のすき間が五辺の白へ伸びます。白に面する五辺はコンパスの弧で描き、上の山をわずかに右へ振ります。

Measured: Five facets run from a circle about 292 pixels wide to a small opening about 45 across. The sides of the opening are curved. The upper facet stands a little to the right. The drawing sets five equal facets at fifths of a turn and gives each divider the same width.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, right page, row 4, first column from the right: 五ッ山 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「五ッ山」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

4 guides, 5 shapes, 11 steps.

## Mountain path in a circle · 丸に山道 (`maru-ni-yamamichi`)

Inside a round band, set a broad path with three low rises and a slim bearing line below it. Shallow compass arcs make the low rises and the dips through them. Both runs touch the round band at their ends.

丸い帯の内側に、低い起伏が三つある太い道と、その下に細い帯を置きます。なだらかな山と谷をコンパスの弧で作り、両端を丸い帯につなぎます。

Measured: Across the inside of a broad ring lie two dark runs separated by a narrow opening. The upper run is wider. Both rise in three shallow shoulders with a slightly higher middle, and both meet the ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, right page, row 2, first column from the right: 丸に山みち (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「丸に山みち」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

32 guides, 2 shapes, 44 steps.

## Four mountain forms · 四つ山形 (`yotsu-yamagata`)

Rule two nested lozenges with equal band thickness. Mark a narrow gap halfway along each side to leave four solid chevrons, all pointing outward.

等しい太さの帯になるよう、菱形を二つ引きます。各辺の中央に細い白いすき間を置き、外を向く四つの山形に分けます。

Measured: Four wide chevrons stand on the points of a low lozenge. White seams interrupt the long sides at their midpoints. The neighboring cell, row 4 column 2 from the right, also reads 四ッ山がた but shows an interwoven form. This drawing follows the four separate bands of row 4 column 3.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 28, left page, row 4, third column from the right: 四ッ山形 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から三列目「四ッ山形」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 4 shapes, 28 steps.

## Snow ring · 雪輪 (`yukiwa`)

A band that rises and falls in twelve round bumps, a big one and a small one in turn. Every bump is a circle, and the band is the space between it and the same circle taken in by 9. Each big bump curls into an ear, a round curl with a small white drop in it that points out. One unit, a small bump and the big one after it, is built in full, and the other five where they stand, clockwise.

大小の丸い山が、かわるがわる十二個並ぶ帯です。山はどれも一つの円で、帯は、その円と、同じ中心でそれより9小さい円との間です。大きい山は巻いて耳（丸い巻き）になり、耳の中には、外へ向かう小さな白いしずくがあります。小さい山と、そのあとの大きい山を一組とし、この一組を先に作図します。残りの五組はその場で、時計回りに作図します。

Measured: The ゆきわ of the 1892 crest book (frame 15, left page, row 4, column 1 from the right), read along lines from the centre as parts of the outer radius: the band is 0.095 across between the middle of its two lines, rises and falls in bumps that reach 0.96 to 1.0 and notch to 0.93, and has six ears, each a curl that reaches in to 0.72 to 0.74 with a small drop in it. The book draws it in outline and by hand: the ears stand about 35 degrees from upright and 60 apart, and two or three bumps lie between them. The Wikimedia Commons picture Yukiwa (Japanese crest, inverted) was read the same way, for comparison only: twelve bumps, big and small in turn, the ears on the big ones at 30 degrees and 60 apart, the band 0.083 across, and a curl 0.12 across. The drawing has a big bump of 45 about 53 out and a small one of 27 about 73 out, a band 9 wide, and a curl of 12 about 87 out holding a white drop of 5.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, left page, fourth row, first column counted from the right: ゆきわ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から一列目「ゆきわ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

15 guides, 6 shapes, 27 steps.

## Iwakaze snow ring · 岩風の雪輪 (`iwakaze-no-yukiwa`)

Three compass lenses alternate with three straight spokes around a round centre. Each lens reaches the outer circle, and a thin white gap parts it from the centre. The uppermost arm is a straight spoke.

丸い中心のまわりに、コンパスで作るレンズ形と、直線で作る細い棒を三つずつ交互に置きます。レンズ形の先は外円に届かせ、丸い中心との間に細い白いすき間を残します。いちばん上は直線の棒です。

Measured: The printed arms reach about 140 to 155 pixels from the centre. The three pointed arms are about 45 pixels wide at mid-length, and the three arms between them are ruled lines. The central disc spans about 42 pixels. The drawing uses a hub radius of 13, compass arcs of radius 84 for the lens arms and plain spokes 88 from the middle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, left page, row 2, third column from the right: 岩風のゆきワ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「岩風のゆきワ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 7 shapes, 34 steps.

## Striped snow ring · くもの雪輪 (`kumo-no-yukiwa`)

Three straight rays divide the circle into thirds. Parallel bands run from the sloping edges of each sector to the outer circle. At the end of each ray stand three narrow compass lenses, with the middle one on the ray.

まっすぐな三本の線で円を三等分します。それぞれの区画の斜めの辺から外円まで、平行な帯を並べます。三本の線の先には細いレンズ形を三つずつ置き、真ん中のレンズ形を線の上にそろえます。

Measured: The print has three equal sectors in a circle about 310 pixels across, with about nineteen parallel hatches to a sector and a three-leaf cluster on the end of each of the three dividing rays. The drawing takes a spacing of 7.5 and a line weight of 3.2, with each ray ending 70 from the middle at a cluster of narrow lenses.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, left page, row 4, first column from the right: くものゆきワ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から一列目「くものゆきワ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

158 guides, 6 shapes, 171 steps.

## Icicle snow ring · つらら雪輪 (`tsurara-yukiwa`)

A broad hexagonal band surrounds a central disc. One straight stem rises from each corner and forks into three branches, each tipped with a small disc. The branches and discs repeat at intervals of a sixth of a turn.

丸い中心を太い六角形の帯で囲みます。六つの角からまっすぐな軸を一本ずつ伸ばし、三本に分け、それぞれの先に小さな丸を置きます。枝と丸は6分の1回転ずつ回したところに作ります。

Measured: The outer hexagon in the print reaches about 94 pixels from the middle, the inner one about 69, and the central disc about 22. Each short stem forks to three discs at about 140 from the middle. The drawing takes hexagon radii 58 and 44, a 14-unit central disc, and 10-unit discs at the ends of the forks.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, left page, row 4, second column from the right: つららゆきワ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「つららゆきワ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

25 guides, 8 shapes, 39 steps.

## Three nested thread spools · 三つ入れ子糸巻 (`mitsu-ireko-itomaki`)

Concave-sided spool outlines nest about one centre. Their inward-bowing sides sit between four outward points.

辺のくぼんだ糸巻の輪郭を、中心をそろえて入れ子にします。内側へ曲がる辺の間に四つの角が出ます。

Measured: Four profiles of about 157, 114, 74 and 35 in reach can be seen in the cell. The layout uses 96, 71, 47 and 23, and follows the same concave shape at each size.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, left page, second row, second column from the right: 三ッ入子いとまき (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「三ッ入子いとまき」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 2 shapes, 30 steps.

## Round fan · 団扇 (`uchiwa`)

A round fan: a circle of paper, cut below by two ruled lines that run into the top of a plain handle. Twenty thin white ribs fan out from the pivot at the paper's foot to a narrow black rim, and a white arc about the pivot binds them. A rib is the thin wedge between two lines ruled through the pivot; the ribs at the top are built first, and the rest where they stand, clockwise.

丸い紙の下を二本の直線で切り、まっすぐな柄の上につないだ団扇です。紙の下の要から、細い白い骨を二十本、扇のように広げ、外側には細い黒いふちを残します。要を中心とする白い弧を一本引いて、骨を束ねます。骨は、要を通る二本の直線にはさまれた細い三角形で、上の骨を先に作図し、残りはその場で時計回りに作図します。

Measured: The ringed round fan of the 1892 crest book (frame 26, 丸にうちは), in units of the radius of its paper: the pivot of the ribs 0.92 below the paper's centre; a handle 0.21 wide between the middles of its two lines (0.24 to their outsides), reaching 0.69 below the pivot; 20 ribs about 6.4 degrees apart, 18 reaching the top of the paper and one more each side below the binding arc, each line 0.03 wide; and the binding arc 0.84 about the pivot. Ours has a paper of 70, the pivot 64 below its centre, a handle 14 wide reaching 48 below the pivot, 20 ribs 6.4 degrees apart, and the arc 58 about the pivot: 0.91, 0.20, 0.69 and 0.83; each rib is a narrow wedge from the pivot, 0.02 wide at the arc and 0.04 at the top of the paper, where the book's lines are even. The book draws in outline; its lines are the white lines here, and the ring is left out.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, fourth row, second column counted from the right: 丸にうちは (measured, with its ring left out, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「丸にうちは」（輪を除いて寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

52 guides, 1 shapes, 63 steps.

## Feather fan · 羽団扇 (`ha-uchiwa`)

One long feather and five matching pairs spread from a rounded handle with two scrolls. Each feather is a lens between two compass arcs with a fine white shaft.

巻きのある丸い柄から、中央の長い羽一枚と左右五対の羽を広げます。羽は二つの円弧にはさまれた形にし、細い白い羽軸を入れます。

Measured: The print has eleven pointed feathers. The longest reaches about 212 above the handle crown in the crop. The six tip heights and widths on one side are rounded to layout coordinates, and mirrored. The centre lens is 132 long, with equal arcs of radius 97. The two scrolls have radius 6.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, second row, second column from the right: 羽うちは (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「羽うちは」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

56 guides, 4 shapes, 81 steps.

## One Chinese round fan · 一つ唐団扇 (`hitotsu-kara-uchiwa`)

A long central handle ends in a pointed cap. The two matching halves of the fan have a rounded crown and foot with a narrow waist between them. Two curved scrolls and nine short ribs open in white on each half.

長い柄の上にとがった飾りを置きます。左右同じ形の地紙は、上と下が丸くふくらみ、中央が細くなります。それぞれに二つの巻きと九本の短い骨を白く抜きます。

Measured: The cell's outer faces span about 228 units in the crop against a height of about 290. The upper and lower curls are about 36 and 24 wide. On the layout the faces are approximately 144 by 150 with curls of diameter 24 and 16, alongside a 12-wide handle tipped by a 46-wide cap.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, third row, first column from the right: 一ッ唐うちは (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「一ッ唐うちは」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

110 guides, 1 shapes, 127 steps.

## Palm-leaf fan · 棕櫚団扇 (`shuro-uchiwa`)

Pleated palm blades spread in pairs around the upright one. Each blade has a rounded tip, a white midrib and three V-shaped pleats. The handle has two small scrolls.

中央の一枚を真上に置き、折り目のある棕櫚の葉を左右に広げます。葉先を丸くし、白い中肋と三つのV字の折り目を入れます。柄には小さな巻きを二つ置きます。

Measured: The print repeats a narrow blade with rounded ends, a centre line and chevron marks. Six tip positions on one side set out the half-fan. The tip radii range from 12 to 17 and the upper blade reaches 134 above its root. The small variations in the print's fold spacing are made regular.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, first row, second column from the right: 棕櫚うちは, with しゅろ beside the kanji (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「棕櫚うちは」（「しゅろ」の振り仮名）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

133 guides, 4 shapes, 151 steps.

## Two round fans in a thin ring · 細輪に二本団扇 (`hosowa-ni-nihon-uchiwa`)

Two matching Chinese round fans stand side by side in a thin ring. Each has a straight handle, paper that narrows at its waist, two curls and four short ribs on either side.

細輪の中に同じ形の唐団扇を二本並べます。それぞれの団扇には、まっすぐな柄と、中央がくびれた地紙があり、柄の両側に二つの巻きと四本の短い骨を入れます。

Measured: The cell's ring is about 300 wide in the crop and its black band about 12. Each stem is about 212 long, and the pair's axes are roughly 120 apart. The construction uses a 192-unit ring of the package's thin weight, stems 125 long and axes 82 apart. Four ribs are visible on each face.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, first row, first column from the right: 細ワニ二本うちは (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「細ワニ二本うちは」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

60 guides, 3 shapes, 80 steps.

## Three round fans · 三つ団扇 (`mitsu-uchiwa`)

Three round paper faces stand 120 degrees apart. The ribs appear near the centre, below a curved binding. Three ruled handles cross in the middle and project between the paper faces.

丸い地紙を三枚、120度ずつ並べます。骨は中心の近く、束ねる円弧の下に入れます。まっすぐな柄は中央で交差し、地紙の間から突き出ます。

Measured: The paper circles are about 130 units wide in the crop. The lower pair stand about 99 from the common crossing, the upper one about 82. This is drawn with a radius of 42 and centres 53 from the crossing, on equal 120 degree divisions. The binding arc about the pivot is 41, the handle is 10 wide and reaches 93 from the crossing.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, first row, third column from the right: 三ッうちは (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「三ッうちは」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

33 guides, 3 shapes, 43 steps.

## Round fan in a circle · 丸に団扇 (`maru-ni-uchiwa`)

The round fan set in a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, the top of its paper and the foot of its handle a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、団扇を収めます。紙の上の端と柄の下の端は、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The ringed round fan of the 1892 crest book (frame 26, 丸にうちは), measured as the round fan with no ring is. The book draws in outline; its lines are the white lines here. The ring and the white inside it are the package's measured ones; the fan reaches the same 0.79 of the radius as every ringed crest.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, fourth row, second column counted from the right: 丸にうちは (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「丸にうちは」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

53 guides, 2 shapes, 67 steps.

## Three Chinese round fans with swords · 三つ剣唐団扇 (`mitsu-ken-kara-uchiwa`)

Three scroll-edged fans point away from a round hub. Small ruled swords fill the three spaces between them. The fans and swords are each set 120 degrees apart.

丸い中心から、縁の巻いた唐団扇を三つ外へ向けます。団扇の間に、直線で作った剣を三つ置きます。団扇も剣も120度ずつ間をあけます。

Measured: The print has three paired scroll faces, each about 130 wide in the crop, alternating with three swords about 50 wide across their broad tips. The layout uses three faces of about 70 units across and sword tips 34 across. The small round hub is 22 wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, right page, second row, first column from the right: 三ッけん唐うちは (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「三ッけん唐うちは」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

93 guides, 7 shapes, 119 steps.

## Open fan · 扇 (`ogi`)

An open folding fan. The paper is the band between two arcs about the pivot, 56 degrees each side of upright, with thin white fold lines; a guard runs up each edge and five ribs fan out between them from the pivot.

開いた扇です。地紙は、要を中心とする二つの弧にはさまれた帯で、垂直から左右に56度ずつ開き、細い白い折り目を入れます。両端に親骨を一本ずつ、その間に要から五本の骨を広げます。

54 guides, 9 shapes, 64 steps.

## Stepped folding fan · 雁木扇 (`gangi-ogi`)

The paper has a ruled stepped rim and a circular cut along its foot. The guard stands to the right of the six other ribs, with a narrow white gap between.

地紙の上を段のある直線で作り、下を円弧で切ります。六本の骨の右に親骨を置き、その間に細い白を残します。

Measured: The numbered cell shows a paper crown about 310 wide and 100 high in the crop, measured down to the side tips. The rim is redrawn symmetrically on six steps each side of the top, with tips 186 apart and the crown 63 above them. The lower paper arc is set 53 from the pivot.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, right page, first row, fourth column from the right: がんぎあふぎ (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「がんぎあふぎ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

45 guides, 8 shapes, 56 steps.

## Five-rib fan · 五本骨扇 (`gohonbone-ogi`)

An open folding fan of five bones. The paper is the band between two circles about the pivot, from the outer edge of the left bone to the white beside the guard on the right; the bones stand at 30 degrees from one another, the guard a plain bar on top with a white either side of it and a small round hole at the pivot. The other four pass under the guard, showing below the paper, and come out beyond the pivot as round-ended butts that are narrow where they start.

五本の骨のある、開いた扇です。地紙は、要を中心とする二つの円にはさまれた帯で、左は左端の骨の外側の縁から、右は親骨のわきの白までです。骨は30度ずつ間をあけて並べ、親骨は右に置いた平らな棒で、両側に細い白を残し、要に小さな丸い穴をあけます。ほかの四本は親骨の下を通り、地紙より下にも見えて、要を越えたところで先の丸い骨尻になり、付け根は細くなります。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Gohonnhone Oogi, in units of half its width: the pivot 41 below the middle with a hole of 1.1; the paper the band between circles of 43.2 and 94.3 about it; five bones 3.5 wide, at 0, 30 and 60 degrees each side of upright, those of the left and the right at the paper's edges; the guard on the right 3.6 wide with a white of 0.7 between it and the paper, running from 11.9 beyond the pivot out to 94; the other four bones passing under it, their tops 42.6 from the pivot and their butts 11.9 beyond it. The 1892 crest book (frame 35, 五本骨日の丸扇) has the same fan with a sun disc.

31 guides, 3 shapes, 47 steps.

## Three folding fans joined together · 三つ組合せ扇 (`mitsu-kumiawase-ogi`)

Three closed fans radiate from a small opening, one upright and the other two set equally to either side. Each has two tapered panels of paper and a straight spine, with a white seam on either side.

閉じた扇を三本、中心に小さな空きを残して並べ、一本を真上に、残りの二本を左右に等しい角度で置きます。扇は先の広い二枚の地紙とまっすぐな親骨で作り、親骨の両側に細い白い線を残します。

Measured: The upper fan's paper is 125 units long and 73 to 57 wide in the cell crop, and its spine projects about 28 beyond the paper. At the crest's scale these are rounded to 71, 42 to 32 and 16. The side fans are inclined about 16 and 28 degrees from horizontal in the print. Their axes are set at 22.5 degrees from horizontal on both sides.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 35, left page, first row, second column from the right: 三ッ組合あふぎ (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「三ッ組合あふぎ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

28 guides, 3 shapes, 37 steps.

## Folding-fan wheel · 扇車 (`ogi-guruma`)

Eight closed fans radiate from a small round hub. Each straight spine divides two tapered paper panels. The fans are set exactly 45 degrees apart.

小さな丸い中心のまわりに、閉じた扇を八本並べます。扇はまっすぐな親骨をはさんで、先の広い二枚の地紙を置きます。扇の間隔はちょうど45度です。

Measured: The wheel in the cell spans about 290 units in the crop, and the hub about 33. The upper fan's paper measures about 112 long, from 60 wide at its outer edge to 28 near the spine's foot. The construction has a 20-unit hub and paper 72 long and 38 to 18 wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, right page, first row, second column from the right: 扇ぐるま (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「扇ぐるま」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

58 guides, 9 shapes, 80 steps.

## Five-rib fan in a circle · 丸に五本骨扇 (`maru-ni-gohonbone-ogi`)

The five-rib fan set low in a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its two lower paper corners and the tip of its guard a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、五本骨扇を低めに置きます。地紙の両端の角と親骨の先は、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Maru ni Gohonnhone Oogi, in units of its ring's outer radius: the fan's pivot 37 below the centre, the paper from 41 to 89.5 about it, the bones 3.4 wide and the guard 3.45, a white of 0.67 beside the guard, the bones' butts 11.3 beyond the pivot; the fan's farthest corners stand a narrow white line from the ring. The ring and that white are the package's measured ones.

32 guides, 4 shapes, 51 steps.

## Two crossed folding fans · 違い扇 (`chigai-ogi`)

Two tapered closed fans cross on diagonals, each 47.5 degrees from upright. The front fan rises from the lower left and a narrow white gap separates it from the back fan.

先の広い閉じた扇を、縦から47.5度の斜めの線に沿って交差させます。手前の扇は左下から右上へ伸ばし、奥の扇との間に細い白を残します。

Measured: The paper in front is about 76 units wide at the broad end and 49 at the shoulder in the cell crop. The butt is about 160 from the centre crossing. On the layout the near paper is 44 to 28 wide and the butt 93 from the crossing. The slightly narrower paper on the fan behind is 40 to 24 wide. Both axes use the mean of the 45- and 50-degree angles in the print.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, right page, fourth row, first column from the right: ちがひあふぎ (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「ちがひあふぎ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

21 guides, 2 shapes, 29 steps.

## Two overlapping folding fans · 重ね扇 (`kasane-ogi`)

Two open fans overlap. The rounded paper faces follow concentric arcs about their pivots, with the rear pivot set higher. Seven ribs radiate from the front pivot, with rounded feet and a fine white gap beside the right guard.

開いた扇を二つ重ねます。丸い地紙はそれぞれの要を中心とする円弧で作り、奥の要を高いところに置きます。手前の要から丸い骨尻のある七本の骨を広げ、右の親骨と地紙の間に細い白を残します。

Measured: The front paper has an opening about 108 degrees wide in the crop. Its outer and inner arcs are about 185 and 87 from the pivot, and the rear paper rises 53 above the front. The construction uses radii 115 and 54 and raises the rear pivot by 32. The rear face is slightly narrower to stay within the layout circle. The visible ribs are ruled four units wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, right page, third row, second column from the right: かさねあふぎ (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「かさねあふぎ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

38 guides, 9 shapes, 54 steps.

## Two folding fans in a circle · 丸に二本扇 (`maru-ni-nihon-ogi`)

Two closed fans stand side by side in a ring. Each has tapered paper panels beside a straight spine and a small round rivet hole at the foot.

輪の中に閉じた扇を二本並べます。それぞれの扇はまっすぐな親骨の両側に先の広い地紙を置き、親骨の下に小さな丸い穴をあけます。

Measured: The ring is about 312 units across in the crop. The two spine axes are about 104 apart, the paper about 145 long and 82 wide at the top, and the feet about 122 below the ring centre. The motif uses axes 60 apart, paper 86 long and 52 wide, and feet 67 below the layout centre, with the package's medium ring weight and a four-unit rivet hole in each spine.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 35, left page, fourth row, second column from the right: 丸ニ二本扇 (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「丸ニ二本扇」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

12 guides, 3 shapes, 22 steps.

## Two crossed half-open folding fans · 中開き違い扇 (`nakahiraki-chigai-ogi`)

Two half-open fans cross, with the right-hand paper in front. Each paper has a ruled stepped tip, a short arc above the pivot and a straight guard. Two narrow ruled seams mark the folds.

中ほどまで開いた扇を二つ交差させ、右の地紙を手前に置きます。それぞれの地紙に段のある先と要の上の短い円弧を作り、まっすぐな親骨を置きます。折り目には二本の細い白を入れます。

Measured: The cell's pivot and side tips are rounded to grid positions. The front pivot is 59 left and 54 below centre; its paper begins along a 47-radius arc and reaches up to 65 above centre on its inner stepped edge. The left paper is constructed with the same opening and the front guard separates the crossing.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 35, left page, third row, third column from the right: 中ひらきちがひ扇 (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「中ひらきちがひ扇」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

54 guides, 10 shapes, 65 steps.

## Three crossed folding fans · 三本違い扇 (`sanbon-chigai-ogi`)

Three closed fans cross along three axes, with the broad ends pointing down and to the upper corners. A white gap beside each spine separates it from the fans beneath.

閉じた扇を三本交差させ、広い先を下と左右の上に向けます。親骨のわきに白いすき間を残し、その下の扇と分けます。

Measured: The lower fan's paper is about 120 units long in the cell crop, its tip and shoulder 70 and 49 wide. Its spine reaches about 150 above that shoulder. Rounded on the layout these are 70, 40, 32 and 89. The two upper fans stand 60 degrees off upright and their tips are 44 units wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 35, left page, second row, first column from the right: 三本違ひあふぎ (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「三本違ひあふぎ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

21 guides, 3 shapes, 30 steps.

## Sun-disc fan · 日の丸扇 (`hinomaru-ogi`)

The open fan with a sun disc standing on the upper edge of its paper, a white line round the disc where it lies over the paper.

開いた扇の地紙の上の縁に日の丸を重ね、地紙と重なるところは円のまわりを白く抜きます。

41 guides, 10 shapes, 55 steps.

## Crossed paddles in a thread ring · 糸輪に違い櫂 (`itowa-ni-chigai-kai`)

Two paddles cross at the base of the blades. Their transverse grips face down inside the thin ring.

二本の櫂を先の付け根で交差させ、糸輪の中でT字形の柄を下へ向けます。

Measured: The blades in the printed ring sit close to a right angle, and the right blade crosses in front. The upper tips and lower grips fall just inside the ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, third row, third column from the right: 糸ワニちがひかい (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「糸ワニちがひかい」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

21 guides, 3 shapes, 33 steps.

## Sonobe plaque · 園部額 (`sonobe-gaku`)

A plaque in a ring. Tangent compass arcs form the shallow waves around the outside. Four white seams mark the mitred corners. Three nested rectangles set out the inner band and the empty centre.

輪の中に額を収めます。外縁の浅い波を、接するコンパスの弧でつなぎます。四隅の継ぎ目には白いすき間を入れます。三つの長方形を重ね、内側の帯と中央の空きを作ります。

Measured: The cell's ring is about 306 pixels across and the plaque about 162 by 236. Inside it are three rectangular outlines about 96 by 172, 76 by 153, and 57 by 130 pixels. The redrawing regularises the paired rolls using radii 12 and 16. The rectangles are 54 by 98, 44 by 88 and 32 by 76, reduced with the plaque to leave a white gap inside the package's medium ring. The diagonal lines in the book become narrow cuts that stop just inside the edges of the bevels.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, second row, second column from the right: そのべがく (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「そのべがく」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

54 guides, 3 shapes, 66 steps.

## Plaque with the characters two and eight · 額に二八 (`gaku-ni-nihachi`)

A plaque with rounded corners, paired curls and an inset rectangular band. Two ascending brush strokes form 二. Below, a tapered diagonal and a longer curved stroke form 八. The strokes use ruled edges and compass arcs.

丸い角と対になった渦を持つ額に、長方形の帯をはめ込みます。右上がりの二画で「二」を作ります。その下には、先を細くした斜めの一画と、長く曲がる一画で「八」を作ります。筆画の縁には定規の直線とコンパスの弧を使います。

Measured: The plaque in the cell is about 215 pixels across and 306 high. Its inner opening is about 120 by 230 pixels. The layout takes 108 by 158 for the outside and 62 by 118 for the opening. The corners use radius 10 and the curls are paired at the printed heights, mirrored to match on opposing edges. The numeral strokes keep the print's rise, tapered left foot and broader right foot. The printed がくニ supplies the first part of the reading, and the Nihon Kokugo Daijiten reads 二八 as にはち.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, first row, second column from the right: がくニ二八 (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「がくニ二八」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

90 guides, 3 shapes, 107 steps.

## Skein-winding frame · 桛 (`kase`)

Two wide bars cross on the centre axes. Their central square has a smaller pierced square at each corner.

幅の広い二本の棒を中心の軸に沿って交差させます。中央の四角の四隅には小さな四角い穴をあけます。

Measured: The engraved outline is a cross with a nearly square envelope, arms of the same width and four square holes inset at its middle. The small holes share one width.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, fourth row, third column from the right: かせ (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「かせ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 1 shapes, 24 steps.

## Two opposed sickles · 二つ鎌 (`futatsu-kama`)

Two sickles turn half a circle apart to enclose a diamond-shaped opening. Each crescent passes behind the other sickle's rounded handle.

二本の鎌を半回転ずつ向かい合わせ、中央にひし形のすき間を残します。弧を描く刃は、相手の丸い柄の後ろを通ります。

Measured: The tips of the blades in the cell reach about 150 from the centre, and the handles angle about 35 degrees from the upright. The ends of the handles are rounded and each contains a small round hole. The pair is regularized to a half-turn.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, second row, first column from the right: 二ッ鎌 (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「二ッ鎌」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 4 shapes, 31 steps.

## Six sickles turning around a hub · 六つ鎌 (`mutsu-kama`)

Six straight handles radiate from a round hub. The arcs of the six blades lead round the outside in the same direction.

丸い中心から六本のまっすぐな柄を放射状に伸ばします。六枚の弧を描く刃は、外側で同じ方向に巡ります。

Measured: Six handles join the hub in the native cell. The hub radius is about one seventh of the outside circle; each blade extends out along nearly one sixth of the perimeter. The six positions are made equally spaced.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, third row, first column from the right: 六ッ鎌 (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「六ッ鎌」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 13 shapes, 32 steps.

## Crossed sickles · 違い鎌 (`chigai-kama`)

Two crescent blades point away from the crossing of their handles. A narrow gap marks the handle that passes in front.

二本の柄を交差させ、弧を描く二枚の刃を外側に向けます。手前の柄に沿って細いすき間を残します。

Measured: The printed blades extend to an overall width of about 320. The two handles run from about 64 above to 103 below their crossing, with small variations balanced by mirror symmetry in the drawing.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, left page, fourth row, fourth column from the right: ちがひ鎌 (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「ちがひ鎌」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

16 guides, 4 shapes, 29 steps.

## Folded pot stand · 畳釜敷 (`tatami-kamashiki`)

Six triangular folds make a pot stand. The parallel lines within each fold turn with it.

六つの三角形の折り目で釜敷を作ります。各面の平行線を折り目とともに回します。

Measured: A nearly regular hexagon divides into six triangles about the center. One side of each triangle is parallel to its ruled weave. The drawing repeats the fold at equal turns.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 18, left page, first row, fourth column from the right: たゝみ／かましき (左頁、一段目、右から四列目「たゝみ／かましき」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

226 guides, 6 shapes, 241 steps.

## Gion amulet · 祇園守 (`gion-mamori`)

Two scrolls crossed at right angles on a ring, with a band with a round head, a hooked band, a cloud and a ginkgo leaf round them. A scroll lies along a line through the centre at half a right angle and is 27 wide: a roll on its upper side and a shorter sheet on its lower side, with a white line between them, and at each end a block the scroll's whole width. Beyond the upper end stand the roll's round face and a square knob, and beyond the lower end a smaller knob. The scroll that rises to the right lies on top, and four white lines across it where the two cross leave the three bands of the tie; the other scroll is cut back 3 from it. The ring is 59 and 40 about the centre and shows between the scrolls. Below is a ginkgo leaf, a narrow blade and a wing each side with white veins; its lower edge is the layout circle, cut flat twice, with a round curl. On the left is a band 24 wide with a round head. The band over the top begins in a point, steps down once, and ends in a hook beside the right scroll's knob. The cloud on the right has three bumps above and three below, and two white curls. The ring is built first, then the scrolls, then the leaf with its left wing where it stands, and then the left band, the hooked band and the cloud, clockwise.

輪の上に、二本の巻物を直角に交差させて置きます。そのまわりを、丸い頭のある帯、鉤の形の帯、雲、銀杏の葉が囲みます。巻物は、中心を通る斜め45度の線に沿って置き、幅は27です。上側が巻いた部分、下側がそれより短い紙の部分で、間に白い線があります。両端には、巻物の幅いっぱいの部分があります。上の端の外には、巻いた部分の丸い面と四角いつまみがあり、下の端の外には、小さいつまみがあります。右上がりの巻物が上に重なります。交差するところに白い線を四本入れて、結びひもの三本の帯を残します。もう一本の巻物は、そこから3だけ離して切ります。輪は中心から59と40の円で、巻物の間に見えます。下には銀杏の葉を置きます。細い中央の葉と、左右に一枚ずつの葉があり、白い葉脈が入ります。葉の下の縁は外の円で、二か所を平らに切り、丸い渦を一つ入れます。左には幅24の帯があり、先に丸い頭があります。上の帯は、尖った先から始まり、一段下がって、右の巻物のつまみの横で鉤の形に終わります。右の雲は、上と下に丸い山が三つずつあり、中に白い渦が二つあります。輪、巻物、葉の順に作図し、左の葉はその場で作図します。そのあと、左の帯、上の帯、雲を時計回りに作図します。

Measured: The ぎおんまもり of the 1892 crest book (frame 31, left page, row 3, column 3 from the right) and a Wikimedia Commons picture of 祇園守 (Gion Mamori (No background and Black color drawing).svg, CC BY-SA 3.0, the same drawing as Gion Mamori.svg), measured as numbers only, in hundredths of the crest's half width. In the Commons picture the ring is 59 and 40; a scroll is 27 wide with a white line 2 to 3 wide along it, and its knobs stand 68 and 70 from the centre; the left band is 24 wide with a head 28 across; the hooked band is about 20 wide over the top; the cloud begins 62 from the centre; the ginkgo leaf is 130 wide and its veins meet 30 below the centre. The book draws the same crest by hand and in outline, with the same ring, crossed scrolls, tie, left band, hooked band, cloud and leaf; its scrolls are narrower, its tie is a few curved threads, its leaf has seven or more fine veins and a wavier edge, and its cloud is smaller. The book also has a plainer cell (row 2, column 4 from the right: a ring, crossed scrolls and a level bar), which Commons does not show; the crest drawn is the one both show. Ours follows the Commons picture's proportions. Its edges are arcs and ruled lines, so the leaf's lower edge is the layout circle cut flat where the picture has shallow scallops, the tail of the hooked band is straight, and the two scrolls are mirror images.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 31, left page, third row, third column counted from the right: ぎおんまもり (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「ぎおんまもり」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

101 guides, 17 shapes, 149 steps.

## Nail-puller · 釘抜 (`kuginuki`)

A square stood on its corner, its four corners on the outer circle where the upright and the level line through the centre meet it, with a small square hole in the middle whose corners lie 0.2 of the way out on the same two lines. Each side is a line ruled full length.

正方形を角で立てます。四つの角は、中心を通る縦の線と横の線が外円と交わる点です。真ん中に小さな四角い穴をあけ、穴の四つの角は、同じ二本の線の上、中心から半径の0.2の距離に置きます。辺はどれも端から端まで引いた直線です。

Measured: Two public pictures of the ringed crest, read as distances along the level line through the centre: the Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Maru ni Kuginuki (hole 0.148 of the diamond across) and the repository's reference sheet of ringed crests (0.249); the drawing takes the middle, 0.20. No unringed picture was found, so the diamond is the ringed crest's, 0.78 to 0.80 of the ring's diameter.

12 guides, 1 shapes, 18 steps.

## Nail-puller in a circle · 丸に釘抜 (`maru-ni-kuginuki`)

The nail-puller laid out inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its corners a narrow white from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、角が輪に触れないよう細い白を残して釘抜を割り付けます。

Measured: The same two pictures as the nail-puller, read as parts of the ring's diameter: ring 0.090 in both (the package's 0.087), white between ring and diamond 0.019 and 0.011 (the package's 0.018), diamond across 0.782 and 0.798.

14 guides, 2 shapes, 24 steps.

## Crossed nail pullers · 違い釘抜 (`chigai-kuginuki`)

Two hooked brackets stood on their corner, one hooked into the other. A bracket is a spine and two arms of one thickness, each arm ending in a short tab turned in; the second is the first turned half a turn about the middle. Twenty lines are ruled full length, eight one way and twelve the other, and the two outermost corners stand on the outer circle. The first bracket is built in full, the second where it stands.

かぎ形の金具を二つ、角で立て、一方をもう一方に引っかけます。金具は、同じ太さの背と二本の腕でできていて、腕の先は内側へ短く折れています。二つめは、一つめを中心のまわりに半回転させた形です。直線は、斜めの二つの向きに八本と十二本、合わせて二十本を端から端まで引き、いちばん外の二つの角は外円の上に置きます。一つめの金具を先に作図し、二つめはその場で作図します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Chigai kuginuki, read from the numbers it is drawn with: a bracket 42 across, its spine and arms 10 thick, its tabs 4 deep and 10 wide, and the second bracket the first turned half a turn about (29, 29), the whole stood on its corner at half a right angle; so the pair is 58 across the grid and 82 along its diagonal. The 1892 crest book draws the same two interlocked brackets, with thicker bars and the diamond a little wider than high (frame 26, left page, row 3, column 4: 違ひ釘ぬき). The drawing takes the picture's, with the two outermost corners on the outer circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, left page, third row, fourth column: 違ひ釘ぬき (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目の四列目「違ひ釘ぬき」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

21 guides, 2 shapes, 28 steps.

## Thick horse bit · 太轡 (`futo-kutsuwa`)

A solid disc has four small openings cut in quadrants, leaving a broad cross and a wide shoulder of ink around it.

円盤の四隅に小さな穴をあけます。広い十字と、その外側を囲む太い縁を残します。

Measured: The printed circle is about 148 in radius and the openings extend about 61 from the middle. The cross is made a little wider than one fifth of the radius.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed.
