Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, left page, third row, second column from the right: ふとくつわ (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「ふとくつわ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 1 shapes, 16 steps.

## Flower horse bit · 花轡 (`hana-kutsuwa`)

The broad cross of a horse bit reaches across four quadrant openings. A circular rim holds the ends of the cross.

轡の広い十字で四つの穴を区切ります。十字の四つの端を丸い縁でつなぎます。

Measured: The four openings reach about 103 out to the curved edge within the printed radius of about 153. Their corners and the arms of the cross line up on the centre axes.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, left page, second row, second column from the right: 花くつわ (左頁、二段目、右から二列目「花くつわ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 1 shapes, 16 steps.

## Square horse bit · 角轡 (`kaku-kutsuwa`)

A rounded square contains four small square openings. Two ruled axes align the openings in pairs.

角を丸めた四角に四つの小さな四角い穴をあけます。二本の軸に沿って二つずつ並べます。

Measured: The printed body is about 229 wide. Its four openings are each about 37 wide, with their centres about 41 to either side of the two axes. The outer corners are balanced with one compass width.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, left page, first row, third column from the right: 角くつわ (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「角くつわ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

35 guides, 1 shapes, 42 steps.

## Horse bit in a circle · 丸に轡 (`maru-ni-kutsuwa`)

A circular bit with four rounded openings sits inside a ring. The narrow white space follows the edges of both circles.

丸みのある四つの穴を持つ轡を輪の中に置きます。二つの円の縁に沿って細い白のすき間を残します。

Measured: The cell uses one ring, a large circle for the face of the bit and four relatively open quarter-circle apertures. The layout uses the standard medium ring and central white gap.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 26, left page, third row, third column from the right: 丸にくつわ (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「丸にくつわ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

8 guides, 2 shapes, 18 steps.

## Genji cart wheel · 源氏車 (`genji-guruma`)

A cart wheel: a rim in eight parts, a ring of eight felloes inside it, eight spokes that widen outwards, and a hub.

牛車の車輪です。八つに分かれた外輪、その内側の八つの輪木、外へ向かって広がる八本の輻、そして轂でできます。

61 guides, 11 shapes, 76 steps.

## Pinwheel · 風車 (`kazaguruma`)

Eight fine spokes lead from the hub to a narrow rim. Outside it eight square vanes meet the same axes at their corners.

轂から細いふちに八本の細い輻を渡します。その外側に八枚の正方形の羽根を置き、各羽根の角を輻と同じ軸に合わせます。

Measured: The points of the blades are about 156 from the centre in the printed cell. The wheel rim is 68, the hub is 17, the blade centres are 111 from the centre and their half diagonals are 43. The layout uses 96, 42, 10, 69 and 27.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 27, right page, second row, third column from the right: かざぐるま (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「かざぐるま」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

59 guides, 18 shapes, 76 steps.

## Six-part Genji wheel · 六つ割り源氏車 (`mutsu-wari-genji-guruma`)

Divide the tyre and felloes into six pieces each, staggering their joins. Six widening spokes lead out from the hub.

外輪と輪木をそれぞれ六つに分け、継ぎ目を互い違いにします。轂から外に向けて、幅の広がる輻を六本引きます。

Measured: From the cell, the outside circle is about 154 in radius, the tyre ends at 128, the felloes end at 96 and the hub is 21. The layout uses 96, 80, 60 and 12, with equally spaced joins.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 27, right page, second row, second column from the right: 六ッワリ源氏車 (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「六ッワリ源氏車」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

48 guides, 9 shapes, 64 steps.

## Three-part Genji wheel · 三つ割り源氏車 (`mitsu-wari-genji-guruma`)

Place three cart-wheel hubs around one circle. Each wheel turns a third of the way around the layout and is cut back at its neighbour's outer edge.

一つの円周に車輪の轂を三つ置きます。車輪を3分の1回転ずつ並べ、隣の車輪の外縁で切りそろえます。

Measured: The surviving hubs lie roughly 154 from the centre of the plate's circle, whose radius is about 156. From each hub the arcs dividing the spokes, felloes and rim lie about 75, 104 and 155 out. The drawing regularizes the three turns to 120 degrees.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 27, right page, first row, third column from the right: 三ッワリ源氏車 (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「三ッワリ源氏車」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

40 guides, 3 shapes, 52 steps.

## Three crossed helmet horns · 三つ違い鍬形 (`mitsu-chigai-kuwagata`)

Three helmet horns a third of a turn apart, and under them three bars that cross at the middle. A horn is a cup with a round foot whose sides flare out into two pointed horns; a lobe to each side rises over the circle that sits in the cup, and the white line round the circle opens at the top between the lobes. Every edge is an arc of a circle struck in full. The horn at the top is built first, then the same horn where it stands a third and two thirds of a turn round, clockwise. The bars are 8 wide, their edges ruled full length: the upright bar lies on top of the other two, and where those two cross, the piece of each that runs down to its horn lies on top. Each horn lies on the end of its own bar, with a white line between.

三つの鍬形を、3分の1回転ずつまわした位置に置き、その下に、まん中で交わる三本の棒を描きます。鍬形は丸い底の器の形で、両わきが外へ反って、先のとがった二本の角になります。器の中には円が一つ入り、左右のふくらみが円の上にかぶさります。円のまわりの白い線は、上のふくらみの間で外に開きます。ふちはどれも、一周描いた円の弧です。上の鍬形を先に作図し、同じ鍬形を3分の1回転と3分の2回転まわした位置に、時計回りに、その場で作図します。棒は幅8で、ふちの直線は端から端まで引きます。縦の棒がほかの二本の上を通り、その二本が交わるところでは、それぞれの鍬形へ下りていく側が上になります。どの鍬形も自分の棒の端の上にのり、間に白い線を残します。

Measured: The 三ッ違ひくはがた of the 1892 crest book (frame 28, right page, second row, the rightmost cell), measured in units of the layout circle 96, the cell 1.7 pixels to a unit, the middle where the three bars' axes cross: the book's three horns laid on one another a third of a turn apart, each with its mirror image, give one horn, and its edges were read as circles and rounded to whole numbers on grid points: the bowl a circle of 31 about a point 54 above the middle, cut at its centre's level; the flares 34 about (63, 38) to each side, touching the bowl on the line through their centres; the horns' upper edges 22 about (41, 54); the lobes over the circle 19 about (12, 60); the circle 18 about (0, 56), its white line out to 21. The horns' points reach 90 from the middle. The bars are 8 wide and run from 50 out on the far side of the middle to under their horn's foot; the white lines are 3 wide, the book's own line. The book's horns differ by hand (its lower two stand a little further out than the top one, and their circles are a little long); ours are the one horn turned. The reading みつちがいくわがた: the label gives くはがた (くわがた) in kana; 三ッ is みつ, and 違ひ, the kanji 違 with the kana ひ, is the word 違い, which a standard dictionary (大辞林) reads one way, ちがい, as in 違ひ矢. No picture of the crest was found on Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 28, right page, second row, the rightmost cell: 三ッ違ひくはがた (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「三ッ違ひくはがた」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

26 guides, 4 shapes, 40 steps.

## Upright koto bridge · 押し琴柱 (`oshi-kotoji`)

A single koto bridge has a squared head, outward shoulders and two feet divided by a long rounded opening.

一つの琴柱の頭を角形にし、肩を外に広げます。二本の足の間には上端が丸い長いすき間があります。

Measured: The plain bridge spreads about 246 across its feet and rises about 250 in the cell. Its head is about 31 across. The two shoulders and the arch between the feet have been centered on the upright.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 33, left page, second row, first column from the right: おしことぢ (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「おしことぢ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

12 guides, 1 shapes, 21 steps.

## Three koto bridges in a circle · 丸に三つ琴柱 (`maru-ni-mitsu-kotoji`)

Three bridges point their small ends toward the centre of the ring. Their arched openings face out.

三つの琴柱の細い頭を輪の中心へ向けます。足の間のアーチは外へ向けます。

Measured: The small heads meet near the centre of the printed ring. Each bridge has an outward opening and two feet. The three bridges are balanced at 120-degree turns and set inside the catalogue medium ring.

13 guides, 4 shapes, 26 steps.

## Five angular tops · 五つ独楽 (`itsutsu-goma`)

Five angular tops point inward around a star-shaped opening. Their broad heads face the perimeter.

星形のすき間を囲んで、角形の独楽を五つ内側へ向けます。広い頭は外側に向けます。

Measured: The printed top form has a wide ruled head and a narrow angle at its inside end. The five positions are spaced by a fifth of the circle through the outer corners.

31 guides, 5 shapes, 44 steps.

## Three tiers of fan paper · 三階地紙 (`sangai-jigami`)

Three sheets overlap up one axis. Their upper arcs show in tiers, while the lowest sheet shows its notch.

三枚の地紙を一つの軸に沿って重ねます。上の弧を段にして見せ、一番下にくぼみを出します。

Measured: The upper edges are three similarly bowed arcs of nearly equal span. The lowest sheet is in front and shows both bevelled sides and the smaller lower arc.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 36, right page, fourth row, third column from the right: 三がい地紙 (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「三がい地紙」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

## Shogi piece · 将棋駒 (`shogi-koma`)

A five-sided shogi piece shows one side and the two characters 龍王 on its face.

五角形の将棋駒の側面を見せて、正面に「龍王」の二字を入れます。

Measured: The printed piece is about 270 wide and 290 high. The side begins near the upper point and narrows the visible face on the right. The ruled and curved strokes are placed from the printed 龍王, with the broader character above 王.

119 guides, 1 shapes, 126 steps.

## Sandbar tray · 州浜 (`suhama`)

A round lobe above two round lobes side by side, as a sandbar is drawn. Three circles are struck: 67 about a point 29 above the centre, and 44 about the two points 46 either side of the centre and 24 below it; the top of the big lobe stands on the outer circle. The outline runs over the big lobe to where the lower circles cross it, round the outside of each lower lobe, and in along two ruled lines to a little roof over the narrow slit between them. 

砂州（すなす）のように、上に丸いふくらみを一つ、その下に丸いふくらみを二つ並べます。円は三つ。中心の29上の点を中心にした半径67の円と、中心から左右に46、下に24の二点を中心にした半径44の円です。上のふくらみの頂は外円の上に置きます。輪郭は、上のふくらみを回り、下の二つの円と交わる点から、それぞれの下のふくらみの外側を回って、二本の直線で、二つの間のせまいすき間の小さな屋根へ入ります。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Suhama, read in its own units from the circles its outline is made of: the upper lobe 160.4 across the radius about a point 126.6 above the lower lobes' centres, the lower lobes 106 across the radius and 220.9 apart, so the slit between them is 8.9 wide, with a small roof at 78.1 under the upper lobe's centre; that is 0.661, 0.790 and 0.689 of the upper radius. The 1892 crest book draws it in a ring as 丸にすはま (frame 45, left page, row 1, column 3) with the same three lobes and the slit, the lobes a little wider apart; its plain 州浜 beside it (column 4) has no slit, its three lobes run together. The drawing takes 67, 44 and 46, so every width is a whole number.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, left page, first row, third column: 丸にすはま, the same three lobes in a ring (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目の三列目「丸にすはま」、輪の中の同じ三つの丸（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

6 guides, 1 shapes, 13 steps.

## Sandbar tray in a circle · 丸に州浜 (`maru-ni-suhama`)

The sandbar tray inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, smaller than the plain tray and centred, a good white all round it. Its three circles are 48 about a point 9 above the centre and 32 about the two points 33 either side and 25 below it; the roof of the slit is two ruled lines.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、州浜を小さめに、中央に収め、まわりに十分な白を残します。円は三つ。中心の9上の点を中心にした半径48の円と、中心から左右に33、下に25の二点を中心にした半径32の円です。すき間の屋根は二本の直線です。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Maru ni Suhama, read in its own units: the ring 0.0905 of the diameter (the package's 0.087), the tray's width 0.814 of the ring's inner radius to each side and its height 0.713 above and 0.710 below the middle, so 0.874 high to wide where the plain tray is 0.91. The 1892 crest book draws it as 丸にすはま (frame 45, left page, row 1, column 3 from the right), its ring a double line. The drawing sets the tray's three lobes at 0.72 of the plain tray's size: 48 about (0, 9) and 32 about (33, -25) and (-33, -25), so the tray is 130 across and 114 high, centred in the ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, left page, first row, third column counted from the right: 丸にすはま (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「丸にすはま」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

7 guides, 2 shapes, 17 steps.

## Six coins · 六文銭 (`roku-mon-sen`)

Six coins in two rows of three, each touching its neighbours. A coin is a disc with a narrow white ring inside its edge, a black rim outside it, and a square hole a third of its width.

円形の銭を二段に三枚ずつ、隣どうしが接するように並べます。銭は黒い円で、縁の内側に細い白い輪を入れ、その外側が黒い縁になります。中央には、幅の3分の1の四角い穴をあけます。

Measured: The six coins of the 1892 crest book (frame 45, row 3, column 3 from the left of the right page), each ring fitted by least squares: a coin's outer circle line of radius 51.7 and its inner one of 41.0, the lines 4.7 thick so that the edge is at 54, the centres 105.7 apart so that the coins touch, and the square 32 across its outer edge. Here a coin's radius is 24: a rim 2 thick, the white between the book's two circles from 19 to 22, and the hole 16 across, a third of the coin.

18 guides, 6 shapes, 29 steps.

## Yongle coin · 永楽銭 (`eiraku-sen`)

A coin in a thin ring: two thin square frames round the hole, one inside the other, each 2 wide, and the four characters 永楽通寳 round it, 永 above, 寳 to the left, 通 to the right and 樂 below, in the brush hand of the 1892 crest book. The ring is struck first, then the squares, each frame being the space between four long ruled lines and four more; each character is the outline of its strokes in arcs of struck circles and ruled lines.

細い輪の中に銭を置きます。穴のまわりに、幅2の細い四角い枠を二重に作り、そのまわりに「永楽通寳」の四文字を、上に永、左に寳、右に通、下に樂と並べます。文字は1892年の紋帳の筆の書き方です。先に輪を描き、次に四角を描きます。四角の枠は、長い直線四本と、さらに四本の間のすき間です。文字は、筆の線の輪郭を、描いた円の弧と直線で囲んだものです。

Measured: The 永楽銭 of the 1892 crest book (frame 45, right page, row 2, second cell from the right), measured from the book's lines in its own units, the coin's radius 94: the ring a single thin line, the hole inside two squares (each line about 2.4 wide, the outer 31 out from the middle and the inner 21; the book's are a little wider than high), and the four characters 永 above the hole, 寳 to its left, 通 to its right and 樂 below, in a brush hand whose strokes are 3 to 5 across. The strokes are the book's own, outlined in arcs and ruled lines to within about a unit. From the Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese crest Eirakusenn only the count (four characters) and their places (永 above, 通 right, 寳 left, 樂 below) were taken.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, right page, second row, second cell from the right: 永楽銭 (the characters vectorised from the book, the ring and the squares drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目の右から二つめ「永楽銭」（文字は本から写し取り、輪と四角はコンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

210 guides, 4 shapes, 337 steps.

## Two coins · 二文銭 (`ni-mon-sen`)

Two coins lie side by side, with the left one in front. Each has a fine ring inside its rim and a square hole framed by a second square.

銭を二枚並べ、左の銭を手前に置きます。どちらも縁の内側に細い輪を入れ、中央の四角い穴をもう一つの四角で囲みます。

Measured: The print has radius about 92 in the crop and centres about 116 apart. The two outer circle lines are about 10 apart and the nested squares about 31 and 60 wide. The layout uses a radius of 58 and centres 74 apart, a white ring inside the rim, and squares 22 and 38 wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, right page, first row, third column from the right: 二文銭 (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「二文銭」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

24 guides, 2 shapes, 35 steps.

## Four-mon coin · 四文銭 (`shi-mon-sen`)

A circular rim frames a central square hole. Four fields of curved bands meet around the hole, with the lower field spanning the width of the coin.

丸い縁の中央に四角い穴をあけます。そのまわりに弧を重ねた四つの区画を置き、一番下の区画は銭の幅いっぱいに広げます。

Measured: The outer circle and inner rim in the cell are about 154 and 142 in radius. The outer and inner squares are about 72 and 46 wide. The layout uses radii 96 and 89 and squares 40 and 24 wide. The lower band begins about 18 below the centre, and the upper and side faces each use two arcs measured from the cell.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, right page, fourth row, second column from the right: 四文銭 (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「四文銭」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

37 guides, 1 shapes, 58 steps.

## Hasebe coins · 長谷部銭 (`hasebe-zeni`)

Eight smaller coins surround a large one. Their centres are set 45 degrees apart, and each coin has an upright square hole.

大きな銭を中心に、小さな銭を八枚並べます。小さな銭の中心は45度ずつ離し、どの銭にも縦横のそろった四角い穴をあけます。

Measured: The print puts eight smaller circles, of radius about 43 in the crop, around a central circle of radius about 75. The layout has outer coin radii 26 about centres 69 from the middle, a centre coin of radius 43, and square holes 14 and 24 wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, right page, second row, third column from the right: 長谷部ぜに (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「長谷部ぜに」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

16 guides, 9 shapes, 28 steps.

## Treasure knot · 宝結び (`takara-musubi`)

One cord, 8 wide, tied in a knot twice as wide as it is high. Its six straight runs lie on lines that rise or fall three in four, 30 apart; six round turns, circles of 15 with the cord 4 each side of them, join the runs: a loop at each side whose legs cross, and four hairpins at the corners. At each of the nine places two runs cross, one passes over the other, and over and under alternate along the cord; the run beneath stops a gap of 2 short of the one over it, along lines struck beside it.

幅8の一本のひもを、横が縦の約2倍の形に結びます。まっすぐな部分は六本で、3対4の傾きで右上がりの三本と右下がりの三本を、30ずつ離して引きます。曲がりは六つで、半径15の円を使い、ひもは円の内側と外側に4ずつ（半径11と19）です。左右には足が交わる輪、四すみにはU字形の曲がりがあります。二本が交わる九か所では、片方が上、もう片方が下を通り、ひもをたどると上と下が交互になります。下の一本は、上の一本の横に引いた線から2離れたところで切れます。

Measured: The 宝むすび of the 1892 crest book (frame 21, left page, row 3, column 1 from the right) and the Wikimedia Commons picture of 宝結び (Japanese Crest Takara Musubi), read as numbers only and built again from lines and circles. In the 1000 px Commons picture the knot is 920 wide and 556 high, the cord 39 across, its straight runs at 37 degrees from level (the edges, by Hough count, peak at 36.75 to 37: read as three in four, 36.87), the runs 147 apart across, the middle circle of a turn 74 in radius, the two side loops centred 368 out with their legs crossing at 247, and the four corner turns centred 123 across and 185 up. Set in the crest's units (4.9 px each) these are 8, 30, 15, 75, 50, 25 and 37.5. The book draws the same loops tilted, with a heavier cord and more crossings; the crest follows the Commons proportions and its weave, which the book's agrees with where it can be read.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 21, left page, third row, first column counted from the right: 宝むすび (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「宝むすび」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

37 guides, 1 shapes, 44 steps.

## Three joined weaving beams · 三つ千切 (`mitsu-chigiri`)

Three slanting square heads fit together. Small right-angled cuts at the joins keep the three forms readable.

傾けた四角い頭を三つ組み合わせます。継ぎ目に小さな直角の切り込みを残し、三つの形を見せます。

Measured: The top point is over the middle between the lower two heads. Both side joins have two short edges stepping inward before the outer square resumes. The drawing balances the notches about the vertical.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 11, left page, third row, first column from the right: 三ッ千切 (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「三ッ千切」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

21 guides, 1 shapes, 26 steps.

## Ship under sail · 帆掛け船 (`hokake-bune`)

A crescent hull, a mast on its middle, and a square sail a little taller than it is wide, cut across by three seams.

三日月形の船体に、中央のマストと、幅より少し丈の高い帆を立て、帆に三本の継ぎ目を白く抜きます。

0 guides, 4 shapes, 0 steps.

## Scale weight · 分銅 (`fundo`)

A disc with a round bite taken from each side, the shape of a balance weight seen from the side. A circle of 96 about the centre, and two circles of 32 about the two points 65 either side of the centre on the level line; the bites are cut where they pass the disc's edge, so each leaves two sharp points. The right bite is struck first, the left one where it stands.

秤（はかり）の分銅を横から見た形です。中心の円（半径96）から、左右に一つずつ丸い欠けを取ります。欠けは、中心から左右に65離れた二つの点を中心にした、半径32の円です。円は円盤の縁から少しはみ出すので、欠けごとに先が二つとがります。右の欠けを先に作図し、左はその場で作図します。

Measured: The 分銅 of the 1892 crest book (frame 32, left page, row 4, column 4 from the right), a circle with two smaller circles in it at the two sides, read as parts of the big circle's radius: each small circle 0.34 across the radius, its centre 0.68 out, so that its outer edge just passes the big circle. The Wikimedia Commons picture Fundou-Kamon gives the same, 0.326 and 0.687, its small circles' edges 0.01 past the big circle. The drawing takes a big circle of 96 and two circles of 32 about (65, 0) and (-65, 0).

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 32, left page, fourth row, fourth column counted from the right: ふんどう (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「ふんどう」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

3 guides, 1 shapes, 9 steps.

## Flower scale weight · 花分銅 (`hana-fundo`)

The two side notches cut into the scalloped edge of a balance weight. At each shoulder a pair of fine curls turns inward.

花弁のある分銅の左右を円で欠き取ります。それぞれの肩には、内側へ巻く細い渦を二つずつ入れます。

Measured: The printed design has a radius of about 150 out to the scallops. The side bites have a radius of about 44 and centres about 108 out. The four paired curls are set on the shoulders, with arcs about 10 or 11 in radius in the print, reduced in the drawing with its other proportions.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 33, right page, third row, first column from the right: 花ふんどう (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「花ふんどう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

45 guides, 1 shapes, 56 steps.

## Flower scale weight in a circle · 丸に花分銅 (`maru-ni-hana-fundo`)

A balance weight with circular scallops above and below and a rounded notch on either side sits inside a plain ring.

上下に円弧の花弁を並べ、左右を円で欠き取った分銅を、丸い輪の中に収めます。

Measured: Inside the cell's ring, the weight is about 124 in radius. The side bites have a radius of about 50 and their centres lie about 83 from the middle. Twelve regularly spaced arcs set the simpler scallops in the frame.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 33, right page, second row, first column from the right: 丸に花ふんどう (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「丸に花ふんどう」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

14 guides, 2 shapes, 24 steps.

## Sail in a circle · 丸に帆 (`maru-ni-ho`)

A sail hangs from a horizontal yard. Two curved seams follow the billowing cloth, and ruled lines join the yard to the top of the mast.

水平な帆桁から帆を垂らします。ふくらむ布に沿って縫い目を二本入れ、帆桁と帆柱の上端を直線で結びます。

Measured: The printed yard spans about three fifths of the outer ring. The drawn yard is narrower. The tip of the sail sweeps right, with two curved seams down its face. The frame takes the standard catalogue ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 10, left page, fourth row, first column from the right: 丸に帆 (左頁、四段目、右から一列目「丸に帆」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

29 guides, 3 shapes, 46 steps.

## Five sails gathered at the centre · 五つ寄せ帆 (`itsutsu-yose-ho`)

Five curved sails lead to one point, each with a short yard and mast at the outside.

弧を描く五枚の帆を一点へ寄せ、外側にそれぞれ短い帆桁と帆柱を置きます。

Measured: The five corners of cloth meet near the centre in the cell. The outside assembly of each sail occupies about one fifth of the circle. The turns have been set to equal angles.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 10, left page, third row, second column from the right: 五ッよせ帆 (左頁、三段目、右から二列目「五ッよせ帆」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 5 shapes, 27 steps.

## Crossed battle axes · 違い鉞 (`chigai-masakari`)

Two battle axes, mirror images, their long handles crossed below and their heads up and out. A head is the ground between four circles and a ruled line: the round top of the bevel, the line of its back, the curve under the stub of the handle, the full round of the blade, and the arch both heads share over the crossing. A white saw-toothed line runs along the bevel and three white strokes lie along the blade. Each handle is 12 wide with round ends and runs at half a right angle; it goes under its own head with a white gap of 2, and at the crossing the rising handle lies over the falling one with the same gap. The left head is built first and the right one where it stands.

二本の鉞（まさかり）を左右対称に置き、長い柄を下で交差させ、頭を上と外に向けます。頭は、四つの円と一本の直線ではさんだ部分です。刃の上の丸み、背の線、柄の先の下のくぼみ、刃の丸み、そして二つの頭が交差の上で分け合う一つのアーチです。刃の上には白いぎざぎざの線、刃の面には白い線を三本入れます。柄は幅12で先が丸く、45度に傾けます。自分の頭の下では幅2の白いすき間を空け、交差のところでは、右上がりの柄を上にして、同じ幅のすき間を空けます。左の頭を先に作図し、右の頭はその場で作図します。

Measured: The crossed battle axes of the 1892 crest book (frame 30, right page, row 3, the cell at the page's left edge, 違ひまさかり), measured from a skeleton of the drawing's strokes in the drawing's own units, the pair about 200 across: each head's top arc a circle of 45, the blade's round 37, the notch under the stub of the handle 37, the arch both heads share over the crossing 66, each handle 12 across with round ends, the two handles crossing 8 below the middle. The book's two handles lean 45 and 40 degrees, its heads meet 5 off the page's middle and the heads differ a little, so the pair is the average of the two, as a mirror image. The saw-toothed line along each bevel has nine teeth (the book's has about nine to twelve), 2 each side of its line; the three strokes on a blade are two straight and one arc of 41. From the Wikimedia Commons picture of the ringed crest only the count (two axes) and the handles' half right angle were taken.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, third row, the cell at the page's left edge: 違ひまさかり (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目の左はしの「ちがひまさかり」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

69 guides, 4 shapes, 86 steps.

## Crossed battle axes in a circle · 丸に違い鉞 (`maru-ni-chigai-masakari`)

Two battle axes in a ring, mirror images, their long handles crossed below and their heads up and out. A head is the ground between four circles and three ruled lines: the round top of the bevel, the line of its back, the blade's round, the lower outer curve, the line of its foot, the line of its inner side and the arch both heads share over the crossing. A white wavy line runs from the point of the blade to the foot of the head, and three white strokes lie on the blade. A handle is 8 wide with square ends, leaning 42 degrees; under its own head it leaves a white gap of 2, and at the crossing the rising handle lies over the falling one with the same gap. The left head is built first and the right where it stands; the ring is the medium ring, with a thin white line between it and the axes.

二本の鉞（まさかり）を輪の中に左右対称に置き、長い柄を下で交差させ、頭を上と外に向けます。頭は、四つの円と三本の直線ではさんだ部分です。刃の上の丸み、背の線、刃の丸み、下の外側の曲線、足の線、内側の線、そして二つの頭が交差の上で分け合う一つのアーチです。刃の先から頭の足まで白い波線を一本引き、刃の面には白い線を三本入れます。柄は幅8で先が四角く、42度に傾けます。自分の頭の下では幅2の白いすき間を空け、交差のところでは、右上がりの柄を上にして、同じ幅のすき間を空けます。左の頭を先に作図し、右の頭はその場で作図します。輪は中くらいの太さで、鉞との間に細い白線一本分のすき間を空けます。

Measured: The crossed battle axes in a ring of the 1892 crest book (frame 30, right page, row 4, the cell at the page's left edge, 丸にちがひまさかり), measured from a skeleton of the drawing's strokes in the cell's own units, the ring's inner circle 79: the top of each bevel a circle of 47, the blade's round 39, the lower outer curve 26, the arch both heads share over the crossing 62, the heads' back 49.5 out and their foot 10 down, the wavy line from the point of the blade to the foot in seven teeth, three strokes on the blade, each handle 8 wide leaning 42 degrees. The book's axes stand 3 left of the ring's middle and its two heads differ a little by hand; the left head is taken, set on the middle and mirrored. The ring is the package's medium ring. No Wikimedia Commons number was taken.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, fourth row, the cell at the page's left edge: 丸にちがひまさかり (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目の左はしの「丸にちがひまさかり」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

63 guides, 5 shapes, 83 steps.

## Two parallel axes in a thin ring · 細二本鉞 (`hoso-nihon-masakari`)

Two broad axe heads face the same way on parallel upright shafts. Their bevels and three short marks are picked out in fine white gaps.

幅の広い鉞を二本、柄を平行に立てて同じ方向に向けます。刃の境と三本の短い線を細い白のすき間で表します。

Measured: The printed ring is about 153 in radius; the two shaft centres are about 111 apart. Their exposed lower lengths are about 129. Each scalloped head has three short marks in the printed cell.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, left page, first row, first column from the right: 細二本まさかり (左頁、一段目、右から一列目「細二本まさかり」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

56 guides, 5 shapes, 70 steps.

## Three turning axes in a thin ring · 細三つ鉞 (`hoso-mitsu-masakari`)

Three broad axe heads turn about the same centre inside a fine ring. Short lengths of the shafts show behind the heads.

幅の広い三つの鉞の頭を、細い輪の中で中心のまわりに巡らせます。頭の後ろから短い柄の端を見せます。

Measured: Each swept head reaches roughly 132 from the centre in the printed circle with a radius of about 151. Its inside edge runs toward the middle and a short shaft shows between adjacent heads. The turns are set to 120 degrees.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, first row, fourth column from the right: 細三ッまさかり (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「細三ッまさかり」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

40 guides, 7 shapes, 59 steps.

## Five axes around a hub · 五つ鉞 (`itsutsu-masakari`)

Five handles run evenly out from the round hub. The axe heads stand across the handles, with each blade facing the next.

丸い中心から五本の柄を等間隔に伸ばします。柄を横切る鉞の刃は、どれも隣を向きます。

Measured: The printed hub radius is about 22, its arm reaches about 149 out and the five heads sit across the handles at about two thirds of that reach. The five positions are evenly spaced in the drawing.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, second row, fourth column from the right: 五ッまさかり (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「五ッまさかり」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

51 guides, 11 shapes, 75 steps.

## Outlined measuring box · 陰の枡 (`kage-no-masu`)

Two square outlines sit one inside the other. A ruled band crosses the innermost square from its lower left to its upper right.

正方形の枠を二重に置きます。内側の四角には、左下の角から右上の角へ斜めの帯を渡します。

Measured: The cell's outer square is about 264 across, its rule about 13 wide. The inner square is about 174 across with a border about 11 wide. The diagonal is about 8 across. The layout uses widths 136 and 90, borders 6.1 and 5.6 and a band 4.2 across.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 31, left page, third row, first column from the right: かげの升 (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「かげの升」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 2 shapes, 26 steps.

## Measuring box in a medium ring · 中輪に枡 (`chuwa-ni-masu`)

Set a square measuring box and its corner brace below the upper crosspiece, then enclose them in a ring.

上の横木の下に正方形の枡と隅の斜めの帯を置き、全体を中輪で囲みます。全体を中輪で囲みます。

Measured: The printed box is about 174 wide. The narrow upper piece reaches from about 65 to 94 down the cell, with the square below extending from about 102 to 274. The corner brace rises across the opening. The frame follows the catalogue medium ring and its usual gap.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 31, left page, first row, third column from the right: 中ワニ升 (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「中ワニ升」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

14 guides, 3 shapes, 24 steps.

## Three-tier measuring box in a thin ring · 糸輪に三階枡 (`itowa-ni-sangai-masu`)

Stack three square measuring boxes in decreasing sizes. Rule the sloping seams for their top faces and the upright seam where their two sides meet.

大きさの違う三つの枡を、大きい順に下から重ねます。上面に斜めの継ぎ目を、左右の側面の間に縦の継ぎ目を引きます。

Measured: The stack spans about 222 wide at the bottom, 160 at the middle and 90 at the top. The side faces are about 71, 52 and 32 high. The layout uses widths 160, 116 and 66 and heights 48, 38 and 24 before setting the stack in the thin ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 31, left page, second row, third column from the right: 糸ワニ三がい升 (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「糸ワニ三がい升」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

40 guides, 4 shapes, 52 steps.

## Ball in a six-point holder · 鞠挟みに鞠 (`maribasami-ni-mari`)

Six concave arcs meet at points around a ball. A curved seam crosses the ball above its middle.

六本の内側にくぼむ弧を角でつなぎ、その中央に鞠を置きます。鞠の中央より上に弧を描く縫い目を渡します。

Measured: The ball spans just over half the breadth of the holder. Its seam runs between the sides in a shallow arc. The six outer points are balanced about the circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, second row, third column from the right: まり挟ニまり (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「まり挟ニまり」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

18 guides, 2 shapes, 30 steps.

## Ball holder in a circle · 丸に鞠挟み (`maru-ni-maribasami`)

Six points of a curved holder reach toward a circle. A round opening sits at the centre.

弧を描く鞠挟みの六つの角を輪に向けます。中央には丸い穴を残します。

Measured: The empty centre is about half as wide as the inside of the ring. The six points fill the ring and the arcs cut inward between them.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, third row, third column from the right: 丸にまりばさみ (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「丸にまりばさみ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 2 shapes, 19 steps.

## Crossed arrows · 違い矢 (`chigai-ya`)

Two whole arrows crossed below their middles, each 50 degrees from upright, the feathered ends up; the one whose nock is at the upper right passes over, a narrow white each side of it. An arrow is a ruled shaft with a vane each side: the space between the shaft's white, an outer line and two slanting ends, crossed by two bands of three white lines. The arrow in front ends in a forked head, the black between two circles; the one behind in a leaf-shaped head, a circle drawn to a point by two circles that touch it, with a drop cut from it.

二本の矢を、真ん中より下で交差させます。どちらも垂直から50度傾け、羽のある端を上にします。矢筈が右上にある矢を手前にし、その両側に細い白を残します。矢は、定規で引いた軸の両側に羽を一枚ずつつけた形です。羽は、軸のわきの白、外側の線、斜めの両端の線にはさまれた部分で、三本ずつの白い線を二か所に入れます。手前の矢の先は、二つの円にはさまれた二股の矢じり、奥の矢の先は、円に二つの円を外から当てて先をとがらせた木の葉形の矢じりで、しずく形の穴をあけます。

Measured: The crossed arrows of the 1892 crest book (frame 29, 違ひ矢), in units of half the crest's width, from the crossing: the arrows lean 50 degrees each way; the nock 98 up the shaft, the head's foot 73 down it, the shaft 4 wide; a vane out to 29 from the shaft's middle and from 9 to 83 up the shaft, its ends rising 0.54 outwards; two bands of three lines across each vane; the forked head two circles of 16 and 15 struck 8 apart, the leaf head a circle of 17 with a point 25 beyond its centre and a drop cut from it. The arrow whose nock is at the upper right passes over. The book draws in outline; its lines are the white lines here.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, left page, first row, first column counted from the right: 違ひ矢 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から一列目「違ひ矢」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

85 guides, 2 shapes, 97 steps.

## Arrow nock · 矢筈 (`yahazu`)

The end of an arrow that takes the bowstring, as six ruled lines: two sides that lean out towards the top, a notch cut into the top and a point at the foot, the notch's lines and the point's rising outwards at one slope.

弓の弦にかける矢の端を、六本の直線で描きます。左右の辺は上へ行くほど外へ開き、上には切れ込み、下にはとがった先を作ります。切れ込みの線と先の線は、外へ向かって同じ傾きで上がります。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese crest Maru ni Yahazu, in units of its ring's outer radius: the sides 29.65 out at the middle, leaning out 0.098 for each unit up; the foot of the notch 52.9 up and the point 78.4 down, their lines rising 0.48 outwards. No picture of the nock with no ring was found, so this is the ringed crest's nock with its ring left out; the 1892 crest book (frame 29) has the same form three in a ring and two crossed.

8 guides, 1 shapes, 13 steps.

## Crossed arrows in a circle · 丸に違い矢 (`maru-ni-chigai-ya`)

The feathered ends of two arrows crossed on the centre inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, each half a right angle from upright; the one whose nock is at the upper right passes over, a narrow white each side of it. The feet of the shafts are a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、二本の矢の羽の部分を、中心で交差させて収めます。どちらも垂直から45度傾け、矢筈が右上にある矢を手前にし、その両側に細い白を残します。軸の下の端は、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture 家紋-丸に違い矢, in units of the ring's outer radius, along and across each shaft: the shafts half a right angle from upright, crossing on the centre, 4.8 wide, 71 to the nock and 78.5 to the foot, a white of 2.3 each side; a vane 27.3 out at the crossing, leaning out 0.05 for each unit up; three white lines 2.7 wide and 7.7 apart near each end; a white of 2.3 where one arrow passes under. Checked against the 1892 crest book (frame 29, 丸に違矢). The ring and the white inside it are the package's measured ones.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, left page, second row, first column counted from the right: 丸に違矢 (compared; measured from a public picture, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「丸に違矢」（見比べ用。寸法は公開されている図から測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

76 guides, 3 shapes, 87 steps.

## Arrow nock in a circle · 丸に矢筈 (`maru-ni-yahazu`)

The arrow's nock set on the centre of a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its upper corners and its point a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の真ん中に矢筈を置きます。上の角と下の先は、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese crest Maru ni Yahazu, in units of its ring's outer radius: the ring from 82 to 100, the nock on the ring's centre, its sides 29.65 out at the middle and leaning out 0.098 for each unit up, the notch's foot 52.9 up and the point 78.4 down, their lines rising 0.48 outwards. Here the nock's farthest corners stand a narrow white line from the ring; the ring and that white are the package's measured ones. Checked against the 1892 crest book (frame 29, 中輪に三つ矢はず), which has the same nock three in a ring.

10 guides, 2 shapes, 19 steps.

## Arrows side by side · 並び矢 (`narabi-ya`)

The feathered ends of two arrows, upright and side by side. Each is a ruled shaft with a narrow white each side and a vane beyond it: the vane's outer line leans out towards the top, and its top, its foot and its bands all rise outwards at one slope, two in three, so the top is a notch and the foot a point. Three white lines cross each vane near each end, three bindings cross the shaft, and the nock at the top is slit.

二本の矢の羽の部分を、まっすぐ立てて横に並べます。矢は、定規で引いた軸の両側に細い白を残し、その外に羽をつけた形です。羽の外側の線は上へ行くほど外へ開きます。羽の上の端、下の端、白い線は、どれも外へ向かって同じ傾き（横3に対して縦2）で上がるので、上は切れ込み、下はとがった形になります。羽の上下の端の近くに白い線を三本ずつ入れ、軸には横の白い線を三本入れ、上の端の矢筈に切れ目を入れます。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese crest Narabi Ya, in units of half a shaft's length: the shafts 45.65 either side of the middle and 7.7 wide, a white of 1.5 each side; a vane 38.4 out at the shaft's middle, leaning out 0.058 for each unit up; its top, foot and bands rising two in three; three white lines 2 wide and 12 apart near each end; two bindings across the shaft at the vanes' top and one at their foot; the nock slit 1.6 wide. Checked against the 1892 crest book (frame 29, 二本ならび矢), which has the same arrows in outline.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, right page, fourth row, fourth column counted from the right: 二本ならび矢 (compared; measured from a public picture, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から四列目「二本ならび矢」（見比べ用。寸法は公開されている図から測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

84 guides, 2 shapes, 91 steps.

## Arrows side by side in a circle · 丸に並び矢 (`maru-ni-narabi-ya`)

The two arrows side by side inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, set a little below the middle so that the upper corners of the vanes and the feet of the shafts are a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に並び矢を収めます。真ん中より少し下に置き、羽の上の角と軸の下の端を、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The ringed pair of the 1892 crest book (frame 29, 丸に二本矢), in units of its ring's radius: the shafts 38 either side of the middle and 170 long, the vanes 131 long beside the shaft and 37 out at the top, 29 at the foot: the arrows of the crest with no ring at 0.85 of their size, set a little below the middle, so the corners of the vanes and the feet of the shafts reach the ring. Here they stop a narrow white line short of it; the ring and that white are the package's measured ones.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, left page, third row, first column counted from the right: 丸に二本矢 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「丸に二本矢」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

86 guides, 3 shapes, 97 steps.

## Eight-arrow wheel · 八つ矢車 (`yatsu-yaguruma`)

Eight arrows round a small hub, each a shaft with a vane to either side. A vane lies between the shaft and a ruled outer edge, its top end and its foot ruled across at 30 degrees, and two sets of three white stripes cross it at the same slant. The arrow at the top is built first, then the same arrow where it stands each eighth of a turn round, clockwise. Near the hub each arrow's left vane lies over the right vane of the arrow before it, with a white line between. Every edge is a ruled line, full length, but the hub's circle and its white line.

小さな丸い軸のまわりに、八本の矢を描きます。矢は、棒の左右に羽が一枚ずつつきます。羽は、棒と外側の直線の間にあり、上のはしと下のはしは、棒に対して30度にななめに引いた直線です。羽には、同じ向きの白いすじを三本ずつ、二か所に入れます。上の矢を先に作図し、同じ矢を8分の1回転ずつまわした位置に、時計回りに、その場で作図します。軸の近くでは、どの矢も、左の羽が一つ前の矢の右の羽の上になり、間に白い線を残します。軸の円とそのまわりの白い線のほかは、ふちはどれも、端から端まで引いた直線です。

Measured: The 八ッ矢車 of the 1892 crest book (frame 29, left page, fourth row, second cell from the right), measured in units of the layout circle 96, the cell 1.7 pixels to a unit, the middle at the hub's centre (the point about which the cell's eight arrows best fall on one another a quarter turn round). The eight arrows were laid on one another to read one: the shaft's lines 2 to each side of its axis, the shaft ending 94 out; each vane between the shaft's line and an outer edge ruled through (32, 93) leaning 15 degrees off the shaft, its top end and its foot ruled at 30 degrees across the shaft through 76 and 22 up it, and two sets of three stripes at the same slant through 67, 63 and 59, and 46, 42 and 38. The hub is a disc of 12 with its white line out to 14. The white lines are 1.6 wide, the book's line. Each arrow's left vane lies over the right vane of the arrow before it, as the book draws them; the book's arrows differ by hand, and ours are the one arrow turned an eighth of a turn at a time. The reading やつやぐるま: 八ッ is やつ, and 矢車 is a word a standard dictionary reads one way, やぐるま. No picture of the crest was found on Wikimedia Commons.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, left page, fourth row, second cell from the right: 八ッ矢車 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「八ッ矢車」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

283 guides, 9 shapes, 306 steps.

## Hermitage · 庵 (`iori`)

Two ruled slopes meet on the centre line above a level eave. Two rectangular posts descend to a common level, leaving the space under the roof open.

定規で引いた二本の斜線を、水平な軒の上の中心線で合わせます。二本の長方形の柱を同じ高さまで下ろし、屋根の下に空きを残します。

Measured: In the cell the eave is about 295 pixels across and the peak rises 98 above it. The posts are about 48 pixels wide and 103 high, their inner edges about 94 pixels apart. At an eave of 192, the roof rises 64 and the two posts are each 32 wide and 67 high with 60 between them. The print's slightly off-centre peak and leaning posts are set square and symmetric.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, left page, fourth row, second column from the right: いほり (左頁、四段目、右から二列目「いほり」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 1 shapes, 17 steps.

## Well frame · 井桁 (`igeta`)

Four bars laid as a square stood on its corner, like the character 井 turned an eighth of a turn: two lean each way half a right angle, and their ends stand out past the crossings by about a bar's width.

四本の棒を、井の字を45度回したように、角で立つ正方形に組みます。二本ずつ左右に45度傾け、棒の端は、交わるところから棒の太さほど外に出します。

13 guides, 4 shapes, 24 steps.

## Well frame in a circle · 丸に井桁 (`maru-ni-igeta`)

The well frame, stood on its corner, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

角で立てた井桁を、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に収めます。

15 guides, 5 shapes, 30 steps.

## Well curb · 井筒 (`izutsu`)

Two upright bars and two level bars crossing square, each 0.54 of the length from a bar's end to the centre (a little over half of it), their inner edges 0.275 from the centre, so a small square is left open in the middle; the ends stand out a fifth past the crossings. The outermost corners stand on the outer circle.

縦の棒二本と横の棒二本を、直角に組みます。棒の端から中心までの長さを1とすると、棒の幅は0.54（半分強）、内側の辺は中心から0.275の位置にあり、真ん中に小さな四角い穴が残ります。棒の端は、交わるところから0.185ほど（約5分の1）外に出し、いちばん外の角は外円の上に置きます。

13 guides, 1 shapes, 18 steps.

## Well curb in a circle · 丸に井筒 (`maru-ni-izutsu`)

The well curb, standing square, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its outermost corners a narrow white from the ring.

辺を縦横にそろえた井筒を、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、いちばん外の角が輪に触れないよう少し離して収めます。

15 guides, 2 shapes, 24 steps.

## Torii gate · 鳥居 (`torii`)

A lintel hollowed along its top and curved up at its ends, over a plain bar; two posts that lean out and widen towards the foot; a strut up the middle; and a crossbar whose ends stand out past the posts, each in a notch with a white line round it. The right post and the right end of the crossbar are built in full, the left ones where they stand.

神社の門です。いちばん上に、上側がくぼみ、両端が反り上がった太い横木（笠木）を置き、その下に、まっすぐで細い横木（島木）を重ねます。柱は二本で、下へいくほど外へ広がり、太くなります。二本の柱の真ん中に、島木と貫をつなぐ短い支え（額束）を一本立てます。その下に、柱を通る横木（貫）を渡し、両端は柱の外へ出して、それぞれ柱の切れ込みに収め、まわりに白い線を残します。右の柱と右の貫の端を先に作図し、左はその場で作図します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese crest Torii, read in units of the circle through the tips of the lintel and the feet of the posts, taken as 100. The lintel's top is level from -49 to 49 at 62 and rises in a hollow arc to a tip at 74, 67; its end is a round belly of 24 about 50, 67, cut level at 46. The bar is 40 to 45 and 62 either side; the posts start at 39, the outer edge from 53 to 68 at the foot and the inner from 40 to 50, the foot at -73; the strut is 7 either side from 27 to 39; the crossbar is 14 to 26, 40 from the middle to the post, and its ends reach 51 to 65, each in a notch of the post with a white line round it. The picture is of the Myojin kind, with its lintel curved up at the ends. No book of the period draws it: none of its frames names a torii.

28 guides, 8 shapes, 51 steps.

## Stone-pavement wheel · 石畳車 (`ishidatami-guruma`)

Eight equal squares share a corner and turn an eighth of a turn apart. The edges of each cross the edges of its neighbour. Thin white seams show where one square overlaps the next.

同じ大きさの正方形を八つ、一つの角を中央にそろえ、八分の一回転ずつ回して並べます。隣り合う正方形の辺が交差し、重なるところを細い白い筋で分けます。

Measured: Eight squares meet at a corner in the centre. Each turns an eighth of a turn from its neighbour and covers part of the next square. Four corner tips lie on the axes and the four other tips lie between them. The outlines of the overlapping stones are narrow in the print.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, right page, row 2, third column from the right: 石たたみ車 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「石たたみ車」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

29 guides, 8 shapes, 48 steps.

## Nine paving squares on point · 隅立て九つ石畳 (`sumitate-kokonotsu-ishidatami`)

Rule three columns and three rows. Put a square on one corner at each intersection. The tips of neighbouring squares meet and leave four white squares between them.

縦横それぞれ三本の割付線を引き、交点ごとに正方形を隅立てに置きます。隣同士の角を接し、その間に白い四角を四つ残します。

Measured: Nine squares stand on their corners in three rows of three. The figures meet tip to tip along each row and down each column. Four openings between them repeat the same square shape.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, right page, row 3, third column from the right: 角立九ッ石たたみ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「角立九ッ石たたみ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 9 shapes, 40 steps.

## Single bar · 一文字 (`ichimonji`)

The character 一 as the brush writes it, from side to side of the outer circle: a level top ruled across, a bowed underside, a heavy round heel at the left and a rising rounded end at the right. Its nine edges are two lines ruled full length and seven arcs of circles struck for them; the two ends touch the outer circle.

筆で書いた「一」の字を、外円の左端から右端まで、横いっぱいに置きます。上は水平に引いた直線、下はゆるく反った弧で、左の端は筆を置いた重い丸み、右の端は反り上がる丸い終わりです。九つの辺は、端から端まで引いた二本の直線と、そのために描いた七つの円の弧です。両端は外円に接します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Ichimonji, read row by row as the outline's positions in its own pixels (the bar 807 across and about 200 high, the top level from 227 to 600 at 444, the underside bowed 5 to 8 up in the middle, the heel's leftmost point at 96 and the right end's at 903). The seven arcs are the circles through three of those positions each, and the other positions lie within 0.7 of the circle's radius of 100.

10 guides, 1 shapes, 22 steps.

## Three scales · 三つ鱗 (`mitsu-uroko`)

An equilateral triangle halved on each side; the three corner triangles are drawn and the middle one is left open.

正三角形の各辺の中点を結び、三つの角の三角形だけを描いて、中央の逆三角形は空けておきます。

8 guides, 3 shapes, 19 steps.

## Three scales in a circle · 丸に三つ鱗 (`maru-ni-mitsu-uroko`)

The three scales set inside a ring of about a tenth of the diameter, the triangle's corners a finger's width off it.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、三つ鱗を、角が輪に触れないよう少し離して収めます。

9 guides, 4 shapes, 23 steps.

## Hojo scales · 北条鱗 (`hojo-uroko`)

A wide triangle, 180 across and 66 high, with its base corners on the outer circle. The lines that join the midpoints of its three sides are ruled full length; the three corner triangles are inked and the middle one is left white, so that the three touch at their corners. Six lines are ruled: the three sides and the three midlines.

横180、高さ66の、横に長い三角形を置き、底の両端の角を外円の上に置きます。三つの辺それぞれの中点を結ぶ直線を、端から端まで引きます。角の三つの三角形に墨を入れ、真ん中の逆さの三角形は白く残すので、三つは角でふれ合います。直線は六本です。三つの辺と、三本の中点の線です。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Houjou Uroko, read in its own units: the whole triangle 556 across and 205 high, so 0.368 high to wide, three triangles of 277 across and 102 high that touch at their corners. The 1892 crest book draws the same wide triangle cut in four and names it 北条鱗, ほうでううろこ (frame 25, left page, row 1, column 2 from the right), a little taller, 0.43. The drawing takes the picture's 0.368 as 66 high to 180 across, its base corners on the outer circle; the plain 三つ鱗 (`mitsu-uroko`) is the book's upright triangle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 25, left page, first row, second column counted from the right: 北条鱗, ほうでううろこ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「北条鱗」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

7 guides, 3 shapes, 16 steps.

## Cut-corner square · 隅切り角 (`sumikiri-kaku`)

A square frame with its four corners cut at half a right angle, so it has eight sides. The outer corners stand on the outer circle, a third of the radius from the axes; the inner outline is the same eight lines moved in by the band, 0.144 of the width. Every side is ruled full length.

四つの角を45度に切り落とした四角い枠で、辺が八つあります。外側の角は外円の上、縦横の中心線から半径の3分の1の位置にあります。内側の輪郭は、同じ八本の線を枠の太さ（幅の0.145）だけ内へ寄せたものです。線はどれも端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 角切 (frame 45, left page, third row, fourth column counted from the right), an octagon drawn as two outlines, read as the positions of its lines in the frame's own pixels (the outer one 305 across between its upright sides, the sides 102 long and the corners cut 96 at half a right angle, so the cut is 0.31 of the width and the straight side 0.34; the band between the two outlines 46 across the sides and 42 across the slanting sides, 0.15 and 0.14 of the width). The drawing takes the whole eight-sided frame with its corners on the outer circle, at 1/3 of the radius from the axes, and a band 0.144 of the width across, 26 of 181.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, left page, third row, fourth column counted from the right: 角切 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から四列目「角切」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

17 guides, 1 shapes, 22 steps.

## Square with stepped corners · 角入り角 (`kakuiri-kaku`)

Rule two concentric squares, one large and one small. Each corner steps in, with two short perpendicular sides and two round shoulders. The space between the two outlines makes a broad square band.

中心をそろえて大小の正方形を描きます。それぞれの角を二本の短い直線で内側に入れ、肩を二つの円弧で丸くします。二つの輪郭の間を太い四角の帯にします。

Measured: An upright square makes one broad band. Each corner steps inward in the outer outline and in the opening. The little shoulders end in circular arcs. The square opening is about two thirds as wide as the outer square.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 2, fourth column from the right: 角入角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から四列目「角入角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

33 guides, 1 shapes, 40 steps.

## Folded-in square · 折り入り角 (`oriiri-kaku`)

A square with its corners on the outer circle has a straight notch near the middle of each side. The central white diamond touches four little square openings, one at each corner. All outlines are set out by full-length ruled lines.

外円の上に角を置いた正方形の各辺に、中央近くでまっすぐな切れ込みを入れます。中央の白い菱形は、四つの角で小さな四角い白に触れます。輪郭はどれも端から端まで引いた直線で作ります。

Measured: The outer square in the printed cell reaches about 154 pixels from the middle. Its four sides have one notch each, about 19 wide and 21 deep. A diamond opening reaches about 70 from the middle and a small square touches each corner of it. The drawing makes the notches 12 wide and 13 deep, with a central opening of radius 43 and four eyes of radius 11.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 4, second column from the right: 折入角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「折入角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

24 guides, 1 shapes, 30 steps.

## Inset-corner square · 隅入り角 (`sumiiri-kaku`)

A square frame stood on its corner whose four corners are each notched: two round lobes, 20 across their radius, with a point between them. The lobes' centres stand 76 from the middle, 18 to each side of the upright and level lines, so the outer circle touches all eight; the slanting sides are ruled at half a right angle to touch the lobes. The inner outline is the same with lobes 16 across, 48 from the middle.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形の枠で、四つの角にそれぞれ切れ込みを入れます。切れ込みのある角は、半径20の丸いふくらみが二つ並び、その間がとがります。ふくらみの中心は中心から76、縦横の中心線から左右に18の位置にあり、外円はふくらみ八つ全部に触れます。斜めの辺は45度に引き、ふくらみに接します。内側の輪郭は半径16、中心から48のふくらみで、同じ形です。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's すミ入角 (frame 45, left page, row 4, column 4 from the right), a square stood on its corner and drawn as two outlines, each corner two round lobes with a point between them, read as the positions of its corners and lobes in the frame's own pixels: the outer outline's lobes 31 across their radius with their tops 57 apart and the point between them 20 below the tops, so a lobe stands 74 from the middle and its top 144 out; the inner outline's lobes 25 across their radius, 47 apart and the point 16 below, the top 99 out; the band between the sides 38 across, and every side at half a right angle. The drawing takes the outer lobes' far points on the outer circle: lobes 20 across their radius and 18 to each side of the line through the middle, 76 from it; the inner lobes 16 across, 15 to each side, 48 from the middle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 45, left page, row 4, fourth column from the right: すミ入角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「すミ入角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

29 guides, 1 shapes, 38 steps.

## Square on its corner · 隅立ち角 (`sumitachi-kaku`)

A square frame stood on its corner: the outer corners on the outer circle, the inner corners on a circle 26 inside it, the black between them. The upright and level lines through the centre meet the two circles in the eight corners, and the eight sides are ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形の枠です。外側の角は外円の上、内側の角は外円より26内側の円の上に置き、その間を黒くします。中心を通る縦と横の直線が二つの円と交わる八つの点が角で、八つの辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's すみたち角 (frame 19, right page, row 1, column 3 from the right), a square stood on its corner and drawn as two outlines, read as the positions of its corners in the frame's own pixels: the outer diamond 300 across and 303 high, the inner one 219 across and 217 high, so the inner is 0.72 of the outer and the band between them 29 thick at right angles to its sides, 0.14 of the outer side. The drawing takes the outer corners on the outer circle and a band 26 of the 96 across the corners.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 1, third column from the right: すみたち角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「すみたち角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

12 guides, 1 shapes, 18 steps.

## Square with a round opening · 鉄砲角 (`teppo-kaku`)

A square stands on its corner around a concentric circular opening. The circle nearly reaches the middle of each ruled side and leaves the four corners broad.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形の中心を丸く抜きます。白い円を四辺の中央近くまで広げ、四つの角は広く黒く残します。

Measured: The outer square stands on its corner and is about 300 pixels across the opposite points. The central circle is about 191 across. The drawing gives the circle radius 62.5 and the square the full layout radius of 96 across each half diagonal.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 3, fourth column from the right: 鉄炮角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から四列目「鉄炮角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

8 guides, 1 shapes, 14 steps.

## Crossed square frames · 角違い (`kaku-chigai`)

Two square frames have their corners on the outer circle. One stands upright and the other on its corner. Each band is 12 wide. They pass over and under at alternate crossings, with thin gaps cut from the lower frame.

角を外円の上に置いた四角い枠を二つ重ねます。一方は辺を上下左右に向け、もう一方は角を上下左右に向けます。帯の幅は12です。交差するたびに上下を交代させ、下の枠には細いすき間を残します。

Measured: The upright frame in the printed cell spans about 220 pixels on a side. The other spans about 312 from point to point, which puts their outer corners on one circle. Each band is about 20 pixels thick. The drawing keeps the two squares on one outer circle, with a width of 12 and a narrow gap at every crossing.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, row 3, second column from the right: 角ちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「角ちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

35 guides, 2 shapes, 42 steps.

## Stacked squares · 重ね角 (`kasane-kaku`)

A square with upright and level sides, its corners on the outer circle, in front of a square stood on its corner whose tips reach 91 from the middle. The front square is solid, and the four tips of the back one show beyond the middle of its sides as small black triangles, each parted from it by a narrow white line. The sides are ruled full length.

辺を縦横にそろえた正方形を、角を外円の上に置いて手前に重ね、その後ろに、角を上下左右に向けた正方形を置きます。後ろの正方形の先は中心から91のところまで届きます。手前の正方形は黒く塗り、後ろの正方形は各辺の真ん中から出る四つの小さな黒い三角形になり、手前の正方形とは細い白線で分けます。辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's かさね角 (frame 19, right page, row 4, column 3 from the right), a square with level and upright sides laid over a square stood on its corner, drawn in outline with the lower square's lines stopping at the upper one, read as the positions of its lines in the frame's own pixels: the upper square 227 across its sides, the lower one's tips 152 out from the middle, so the tips stand out 0.34 of the upper square's half side beyond it. The drawing is solid: the front square with its corners on the outer circle, and the four tips of the square behind it, each parted from it by a white line of 2.4, the tips reaching 91.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 4, third column from the right: かさね角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「かさね角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

16 guides, 5 shapes, 30 steps.

## Parent and child squares · 子持ち角 (`komochi-kaku`)

Four concentric squares stand on their corners. The space between the outer two makes a broad band. The space between the inner two makes a second, narrower band inside it.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、中心をそろえて四つ描きます。外側の二つの間を太い帯にし、内側の二つの間を細い帯にします。

Measured: An outer square on its corner stands about 304 pixels high. Three smaller outlines inside it span about 178, 140 and 114 pixels. The drawing takes their corner radii as 96, 56, 44 and 36, keeping the outer band wide, the inner band narrow and a thin white space between them.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 2, third column from the right: 子持角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「子持角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 2 shapes, 26 steps.

## Knotted square · 結び角 (`musubi-kaku`)

A square frame and a broad cross-shaped frame interlace. The arms of the cross reach past the square. Both bands are 15 wide and alternate over and under at successive crossings. The opening in the middle is a cross.

正方形の枠と太い十字の枠を組み合わせます。十字の腕は正方形の外まで伸ばします。帯の幅はどちらも15で、交差するたびに上下を交代させます。中央には十字形の白が残ります。

Measured: The cross outline in the cell is about 308 pixels from end to end, the ends are about 104 wide, the square is about 207 across and both bands are about 25 thick. The drawing uses a cross 180 across, ends 64 wide, a square 122 across and bands 15 thick.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, row 4, second column from the right: むすび角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「むすび角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

43 guides, 2 shapes, 50 steps.

## Shadow square · 陰角 (`kage-kaku`)

Four squares stood on their corners, one inside another, each a thin line: the outer two about 11 apart, the inner two the same. The outer one's corners are on the outer circle; the upright and level lines through the centre meet every corner, and the sides are ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を四つ、順に内側へ重ね、どれも細い線一本で描きます。外の二つは11ほど間をあけ、内の二つも同じだけ間をあけます。いちばん外の角は外円の上にあり、中心を通る縦と横の直線がすべての角と交わり、辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's かげ角 (frame 19, right page, row 3, column 3 from the right), a square stood on its corner drawn as four thin outlines in two close pairs, read as the positions of its lines along the upright and level lines through the middle in the frame's own pixels: 147, 131, 83 and 65 out to their corners. A 陰 crest is the outline of a form and not the form filled, so ours keeps the four lines, each a band of the package's thread weight, the outer one's outer edge on the outer circle: 94.3, 83.5, 53 and 42 out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, right page, row 3, third column from the right: かげ角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「かげ角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

39 guides, 4 shapes, 54 steps.

## Eight nested square frames · 八つ入れ子角 (`yatsu-ireko-kaku`)

Four level square frames nest with four square frames on their corners. The latter sit at the top, bottom and either side, with a tip reaching the layout circle. The bands alternate over and under in each quarter.

辺を上下左右に向けた四つの正方形の枠と、角を上下左右に向けた四つの枠を組み合わせます。角を向けた枠は上下左右に置き、先を外円に届かせます。四方とも、帯は交差するたび上下を交代します。

Measured: The print has four level square frames in two rows and four turned squares reaching about 154 pixels from the middle. The level block spans about 226 pixels, with a narrow gap down and across it. The alternating bands are about 17 pixels thick. The drawing uses 66-unit upright squares, turned squares 96 across their corners and a band width of 10.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, row 1, second column from the right: 八入子角 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「八入子角」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

61 guides, 2 shapes, 68 steps.

## Four crossed squares in a thread ring · 糸輪に四つ角違い (`itowa-ni-yotsu-kaku-chigai`)

Rule four equal corner frames on one square grid. A central square band passes alternately over and under each corner frame. Turn the grid onto a corner inside a thread ring.

同じ大きさの四角い枠を、二つずつ二段に並べます。中央の四角い帯が、外側の枠と交差するたびに上下を交代します。並べた枠を隅立てにし、糸輪で囲みます。

Measured: Four corner frames stand in two rows of two in their own grid. A smaller square band meets each corner frame twice, crossing over and under in turn. The whole grid turns half a right angle inside a thread ring.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 16, right page, row 2, second column from the right: 糸ワニ四ッ角違 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「糸ワニ四ッ角違」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

44 guides, 3 shapes, 54 steps.

## Three bars in a cut-corner square · 隅切り角に三つ引両 (`sumikiri-kaku-ni-mitsu-hikiryo`)

A frame of eight sides, its corners on the outer circle, about a sixth of its width thick, and inside it three separate bars from top left to bottom right at half a right angle: a middle bar 31 across and a bar 16 across at each end, a white of about 29 across between the bars, and a narrow white line between the bars and the frame. The frame's inner outline is the outer one scaled by 0.65. Every side is ruled full length.

角が外円の上にある八角形の枠で、太さは幅の約6分の1です。その内側を、左上から右下へ斜め45度に、離れた三本の棒が入ります。真ん中の棒は幅31、両端の棒は幅16で、棒の間には幅29ほどの白があり、棒と枠の間には細い白線が一本あります。枠の内側の輪郭は外側の輪郭を0.65倍にしたもので、辺はどれも端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 角切ニ三ッ引 (frame 43, left page, row 3, column 4 from the right), an eight-sided frame drawn as two outlines with six lines running from top left to bottom right across the inside, read as the positions of those lines in the frame's own pixels, counted along a line at half a right angle to them from the middle: the two slanting sides of the inner outline (144 and 152 out, one each side) and four lines between them (109 and 38 out on one side, 38 and 109 on the other), so that three bars lie between pairs of lines (a bar 35 across at each end and one 76 across in the middle, the two ends touching the corners of the frame) and a white about 70 across lies between the bars. The outer outline is 287 across with its flat sides 145 long and the inner 188 across with flat sides 109, so the inner is 0.65 of the outer. The drawing puts the outer corners on the outer circle (the outer eight-sided frame 83.4 out to its sides, the inner 0.654 of it) and the bars 45 and 16 each side of the middle across the slant, and, where the book's single line parts a bar from the frame, a narrow white line of 2, so that the three bars stand apart from the frame and from each other.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, left page, row 3, fourth column from the right: 角切ニ三ッ引 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から四列目「角切ニ三ッ引」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

31 guides, 4 shapes, 42 steps.

## Basket eye · 籠目 (`kagome`)

Two equilateral triangles of the same size, one point up and one point down, drawn as lines of one weight.

同じ大きさの正三角形を二つ、一つは頂点を上に、一つは下に向けて重ね、同じ太さの線で描きます。

13 guides, 2 shapes, 21 steps.

## Basket eye in a circle · 丸に籠目 (`maru-ni-kagome`)

The basket eye inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its six points a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に籠目を収め、六つの角を、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Maru ni Kagome, read along the upright and level lines through the centre in its own units: the ring's outer radius 382 and its thickness 67 (0.088 of the diameter, the package's 0.087), the star's points 305 from the centre, 10 inside the ring, and each band 44 wide, 0.144 of the star's radius, so the inner triangles are 0.71 of the outer ones.

15 guides, 3 shapes, 27 steps.

## Three curled bands · 三つ環 (`mitsu-kan`)

Three open compass bands stand one third of a turn apart. The opening in each band turns outwards from the middle of the crest. Its two ends widen into rounded curls.

コンパスで描いた開いた帯を、三分の一回転ずつ回して三つ置きます。どの帯も開いた方を外へ向け、両端を丸く太くして内へ巻きます。

Measured: Three curved bands sit around a small triangular space. The upper band spans about 165 pixels, and its broad rounded ends curl inward. The open ends face away from the centre. The drawing gives the three bands equal angles and keeps a narrow gap between them.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 27, left page, row 2, first column from the right: 三ッくワん (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「三ッくワん」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 3 shapes, 22 steps.

## Five curled bands · 五つ環 (`itsutsu-kan`)

Five compass bands crown the outline. Their openings face a common white centre. A pair of rounded curls turns inwards at either end of each band.

コンパスで五つの帯を描いて外形を作ります。開いた方を共通の白い中心へ向け、各帯の両端を丸く巻き込みます。

Measured: Five curved bands make an almost circular outline. Each opens towards the large white middle and ends in two rounded hooks. The top band has a span of about 113 pixels and the whole crest about 286. The drawing places the five bends at equal fifths of a turn.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 27, left page, row 4, first column from the right: 五ッくワん (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から一列目「五ッくワん」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 5 shapes, 22 steps.

## Tortoise shell · 亀甲 (`kikko`)

A regular hexagon with one corner up: a thick line, and a thin line inside it.

角を上にした正六角形です。太い線の内側に、細い線をもう一本入れます。

26 guides, 2 shapes, 35 steps.

## Bishamon tortoise shell · 毘沙門亀甲 (`bishamon-kikko`)

A honeycomb of hexagons, one corner up, a central one left empty and three trefoils round it, one on top and one at each lower side. A trefoil is three hexagons round one shared corner. Each is a heavy band along its outline, a thin line a narrow white gap inside it, and a three-armed Y from the shared corner into the three hexagons. The trefoils share their outlines where they meet.

角を上にした六角形を蜂の巣のように並べ、真ん中の一つは空けて、そのまわりに三つ葉を三つ置きます。一つは上に、あとの二つは左右の下に置きます。三つ葉は、一つの角に集まる六角形三つです。どの三つ葉も、外の縁に沿う太い帯、細い白いすき間をはさんでその内側にある細い線、そして角から三つの六角形へ伸びる、三本の腕を持つY字でできています。三つ葉どうしが出会うところは、縁を共有します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, right page, first row, first column: 毘沙門きっこう (three trefoils of hexagons round an empty hexagon; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「毘沙門きっこう」（空いた六角形のまわりに三つ葉が三つ。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

157 guides, 15 shapes, 190 steps.

## Seven treasures · 七宝 (`shippo`)

A circle, and four arcs of a wider circle struck from outside it on the diagonals. Each cuts a lens from the circle, and the four lenses meet point to point at the top, the bottom and the two sides, where a small disc stands; the middle is left as a four-pointed star with hollow sides.

一つの円に、斜めの方向の外側から、より大きな円の弧を四つ引きます。弧はそれぞれ円からレンズ形を切り出し、四つのレンズ形は上下左右で先を合わせ、そこに小さな円を置きます。中央には、辺のくぼんだ四つ先の星形が白く残ります。

9 guides, 5 shapes, 20 steps.

## Three joined seven treasures · 三つ七宝繋ぎ輪違い (`mitsu-shippo-tsunagi-wachigai`)

Three circles of one compass width, one above two. The lower circles just touch. The top circle shares a lens with either neighbour. Equal circles form the remaining lenses and leave three four-pointed white openings.
