同じ幅のコンパスで、上に一つ、下に二つの円を描きます。下の二つは触れ合い、上の円は左右の円とレンズ形を一つずつ共有します。同じ大きさの円で残りのレンズ形を作り、四つの先がある白い部分を三つ残します。

Measured: The three circles in the print are each about 160 pixels across. Their centres stand one radius apart upwards and sideways, so the two low circles touch and the upper circle intersects both. The drawing uses radius 48, keeps their compass centres on that grid and draws each shared lens once.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, row 3, fourth column from the right: 三ッ七宝つなぎ輪違 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から四列目「三ッ七宝つなぎ輪違」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

7 guides, 10 shapes, 23 steps.

## Flower lozenge in seven treasures · 七宝花菱 (`shippo-hanabishi`)

Four equal lenses meet at the four corner discs. The opening holds a flower whose four round petals each have three lobes, a straight narrow stamen and a white gap against the next petal. A small white eye opens in the round centre.

四つの同じレンズ形を、上下左右に置いた四つの丸でつなぎます。中央の白に花を収め、丸い花びら四枚に、小さなふくらみを三つずつ、まっすぐで細いしべを一本ずつ入れます。となり合う花びらの間には白いすき間を残し、丸い中心には小さな白い目を抜きます。

Measured: The outer circle in the cell is about 275 pixels across and its four corner discs are about 39 across. The flower spans about 137 pixels, with four nearly equal scalloped petals around a central disc about 23 across. The drawing takes the frame radius as 84, the corner discs 12 in radius, the flower about 80 across and its central disc 14 across.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, row 3, third column from the right: 七宝花びし (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「七宝花びし」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

15 guides, 13 shapes, 37 steps.

## Snake's eye · 蛇の目 (`janome`)

A thick ring: a disc with a round hole in the middle, the hole four tenths of the disc across.

太い輪です。円の中央に丸い穴をあけ、穴の直径を円の10分の4にします。

2 guides, 1 shapes, 8 steps.

## Seven snake's eyes · 七つ蛇の目 (`nanatsu-janome`)

Six round eyes gather around a seventh, each with its round hole.

中央の一つを囲んで六つの蛇の目を並べ、すべてに丸い穴をあけます。

Measured: The central eye is slightly wider than the six around it. They have nearly round holes of about two fifths of their outer diameters. The layout balances the six outer eyes at equal turns.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 42, left page, first row, second column from the right: 七ッじやの目 (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「七ッじやの目」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

8 guides, 7 shapes, 20 steps.

## Plain cross · 十文字 (`jumonji`)

Two bars crossing square, each about a fifth of the cross's length across. Four lines along the arms' edges and four across their ends are ruled full length; the corners of the arm ends stand on the outer circle.

同じ太さの二本の棒を直角に交わらせます。棒の太さは全長の約5分の1です。腕の縁に沿う直線四本と、腕の先に沿う直線四本を端から端まで引き、腕の先の角は外円の上に置きます。

Measured: Three public pictures of the ringed cross, read along the level line through the centre and across an arm, in each picture's own units (the Wikimedia Commons pictures Japanese Crest maru ni jyuji, Maru ni Jūmonji - Hongō and Maru ni Jūmonji - Hongō Miyakonojō Shimazu): an arm is 0.212, 0.245 and 0.202 of the length from one arm's end to the opposite one's, the median 0.212. No unringed picture was found, so the cross is the ringed crest's.

9 guides, 1 shapes, 14 steps.

## Cross in a circle · 丸に十字 (`maru-ni-juji`)

A cross whose four arms reach the ring, the arms as thick as the ring, which is about a tenth of the diameter.

十字の四本の腕を輪まで伸ばし、腕の太さを輪と同じ（直径の約10分の1）にします。

12 guides, 2 shapes, 20 steps.

## Plain cross in a circle · 丸に十文字 (`maru-ni-jumonji`)

A cross inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its arms 32 across, the corners of their ends on a circle 0.9 of the ring's inner radius, so a white gap is left between each arm and the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、腕の太さ32の十字を置きます。腕の先の角は輪の内側の半径の0.9の円の上にあり、輪との間に白いすき間が残ります。

Measured: The same three pictures, read along the level line through the centre as parts of the ring's inner width: ring 0.081, 0.081 and 0.089 of the outer diameter (the package's 0.087), the cross's length 0.963, 0.837 and 0.876 of the inner width (median 0.876) and an arm 0.204, 0.206 and 0.177 of it (median 0.204), so a white gap is left between each arm's end and the ring, 0.015, 0.068 and 0.052 of the diameter. The third picture is the Miyakonojō Shimazu crest.

11 guides, 2 shapes, 20 steps.

## Left one-comma tomoe · 左一つ巴 (`hidari-hitotsu-tomoe`)

One comma fills the outer circle: its tail wraps all the way round its own head and thins to a point on the outer circle, at the upper right. The outer circle stays whole, and the white is one horn. Two circles touch the outer circle from inside at the tail's tip: the sweep, 0.75 of the radius, and the back, 0.70. The hook, 0.51, touches the sweep from inside at its lowest point, and the head, 0.53, touches the back from inside at the lower right. The horn's outer edge is the sweep, from the tip down to its lowest point, and then the hook, up the left. Its inner edge is the back and then the head. The hook and the head cross at the upper left, and that is the horn's other point.

巴一つで外円をいっぱいに使います。尾は頭のまわりをぐるりと回り、右上で細くなって、外円の上で尖ります。外円は欠けず、白い部分は角の形が一本だけです。円の大きさは、どれも外円の半径に対する割合で表します。まず、尾の先の点で外円に内側から接する円を二つ置きます。大きい円は0.75、小さい円は0.70です。次に、大きい円のいちばん下の点で内側から接するかぎの円（0.51）と、小さい円の右下で内側から接する頭の円（0.53）を置きます。白い角の外側の縁は、尾の先から大きい円に沿っていちばん下まで下り、そこからかぎの円に沿って左側を上がります。内側の縁は、小さい円に沿い、続けて頭の円に沿います。かぎの円と頭の円は左上で交わり、そこが角のもう一方の先になります。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's cell (frame 11, 左り一ツ巴), read as circles through the two edges of its white horn, in hundredths of the outer circle's radius, the centre at (0, 0), x to the right and y up. The edge beside the head is one circle to within 1: 56 on (32, 12). The tail's inner edge is two arcs, one running on from the other: 72 on (18, 17), which touches the outer circle at the tail's tip, about 43 degrees clockwise from the top, and then 47 on (13, -6). The horn's other point is at (-25, 26), and the horn is about 10 across at its widest, at the lower left. Beside the tip the book's two lines run as one, so the head's edge cannot be read there. The drawing takes whole numbers on grid points: the tip at (60, 80), 37 degrees from the top; the sweep 75 on (15, 20) and the hook 51 on (15, -4); the head 53 on (26, 9); and beside the tip the back, 70 on (18, 24), which stands 5 inside the sweep at its farthest, so that the white opens from nothing at the tip.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 11, right page, second row, first column counted from the right: 左り一ツ巴 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「左り一ツ巴」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

6 guides, 1 shapes, 15 steps.

## Right one-comma tomoe · 右一つ巴 (`migi-hitotsu-tomoe`)

The left one-comma tomoe turned the other way: the same circles, struck in place the other way round, so that the tail wraps round the head the other way and thins to a point on the outer circle at the upper left.

左一つ巴と同じ円を、左右に返して同じ位置に描いたものです。尾は頭のまわりを逆向きに回り、左上で細くなって、外円の上で尖ります。

Measured: None of its own: it is the left one-comma tomoe's circles struck the other way round, so its proportions are that crest's (the sweep 0.75, the back 0.70, the head 0.53 and the hook 0.51 of the radius; see the left crest). The 1892 crest book's cell (frame 11, 左り一ツ巴) was read turned left to right.

6 guides, 1 shapes, 15 steps.

## Left two-comma tomoe · 左二つ巴 (`hidari-futatsu-tomoe`)

On each of two spokes half a turn apart stand two circles that touch the outer circle from inside at the spoke's end: the head, 0.48 of the radius, and the swirl, 0.66. A comma is three arcs: the outer circle from the end of the other spoke round to its own, its head, and the swirl of the other spoke. The white is two horns, each the crescent between a swirl and a head, and each tail thins to a point where the circles part; the outer circle stays whole.

180度離れた二本の線の上に、線の端で外円に内側から接する円を二つずつ置きます。頭の円は半径の0.48、渦の円は0.66です。巴一つは三つの弧でできます。もう一方の線の端から自分の線の端までの外円、頭の円、そしてもう一方の線の渦の円です。白い部分は二本の角の形で、それぞれ渦の円と頭の円の間の三日月です。尾は二つの円が離れるところで細くなって尖り、外円は欠けません。

4 guides, 2 shapes, 12 steps.

## Right two-comma tomoe · 右二つ巴 (`migi-futatsu-tomoe`)

The left two-comma tomoe turned the other way: the same compass circles, struck in place the other way round, so that the heads lead counter-clockwise and the tails flow clockwise behind them.

左二つ巴と同じコンパスの円を、左右に返して同じ位置に描いたもので、頭が反時計回りに進み、尾がそのあとを時計回りに流れます。

Measured: None of its own: it is the left two-comma tomoe's circles (head 0.48 and swirl 0.66 of the radius) struck the other way round, which that crest took from between two public pictures. Read against both, turned left to right (Wikimedia Commons, Tomoe Variations second of four, and Japanese Crest Hidarifutatsudomoe 1): along the level line through the centre the white is 0.565 of the diameter across in ours, 0.452 in the first and 0.702 in the second.

4 guides, 2 shapes, 12 steps.

## Left three-comma tomoe · 左三つ巴 (`hidari-mitsu-tomoe`)

On each of three spokes a third of a turn apart stand two circles that touch the outer circle from inside at the spoke's end: the head, 0.45 of the radius, and the swirl, 0.66. A comma is three arcs: the outer circle from the end of the spoke behind to its own, its head, and the swirl of the spoke behind. The white is three horns, each the crescent between a swirl and its head, and each tail thins to a point on the outer circle, which stays whole.

120度ずつ離れた三本の線の上に、線の端で外円に内側から接する円を二つずつ置きます。頭の円は半径の0.45、渦の円は0.66です。巴一つは三つの弧でできます。後ろの線の端から自分の線の端までの外円、頭の円、そして後ろの線の渦の円です。白い部分は三本の角の形で、それぞれ渦の円と頭の円の間の三日月です。尾は外円の上で細くなって尖り、外円は欠けません。

6 guides, 3 shapes, 14 steps.

## Right three-comma tomoe · 右三つ巴 (`migi-mitsu-tomoe`)

The left three-comma tomoe turned the other way: the same compass circles, mirrored, so that the heads lead counter-clockwise and the tails flow clockwise behind them.

左三つ巴と同じコンパスの円を左右に返したもので、頭が反時計回りに進み、尾がそのあとを時計回りに流れます。

6 guides, 3 shapes, 14 steps.

## Left two-comma tomoe in a circle · 丸に左二つ巴 (`maru-ni-hidari-futatsu-tomoe`)

The two-comma tomoe laid out in the room inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white line between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、細い白線を残して二つ巴を割り付けます。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 11, right page, fourth row, first column counted from the right: 丸に二ツともゑ (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「丸に二ツともゑ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

6 guides, 3 shapes, 18 steps.

## Left three-comma tomoe in a circle · 丸に左三つ巴 (`maru-ni-hidari-mitsu-tomoe`)

The three-comma tomoe laid out in the room inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white line between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、細い白線を残して三つ巴を割り付けます。

8 guides, 4 shapes, 20 steps.

## Right three-comma tomoe in a circle · 丸に右三つ巴 (`maru-ni-migi-mitsu-tomoe`)

The right three-comma tomoe laid out in the room inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, a narrow white line between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、細い白線を残して右三つ巴を割り付けます。

Measured: None of its own: it is the right three-comma tomoe set inside the package's measured ring and white, as the left one is. Read against the Wikimedia Commons pictures Migi mitsudomoe (a right three-comma tomoe whose head is 153 over a radius of 344, 0.445, as in ours) and Tomoe Variations, third of four, turned left to right (a thin ring of 0.028 of the diameter, against the package's 0.087).

8 guides, 4 shapes, 20 steps.

## Itakura tomoe · 板倉巴 (`itakura-tomoe`)

Nine three-comma tomoe: one in the middle, and eight round it, each set out on its own centre in the same way, none turned. The eight stand on a ring of 69 about the centre at every eighth of a turn, from the top clockwise, each of radius 26 and a narrow white gap from the next; the middle one has radius 35.

三つ巴を九つ並べます。一つは真ん中、あとの八つはそのまわりです。どれもその場で、同じ向きの三つ巴を作図します。八つは、中心を同じくする半径69の円の上に、上から時計回りに八分の一周ごとに置き、半径はどれも26です。となりとの間には細い白いすき間を残します。真ん中の一つは半径35です。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 11, left page, second row, first column counted from the right: 板倉ともゑ (nine three-comma tomoe, one in the middle and eight round it; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「板倉ともゑ」（真ん中に一つ、まわりに八つの三つ巴。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

58 guides, 27 shapes, 98 steps.

## Flower lozenge · 花菱 (`hanabishi`)

Four petals about a round middle, the two at the sides longer than the two above and below, so that the flower fills a lozenge. Two lines through the centre part the petals, each a white line 2 wide. A petal is three round lobes: the lobes' circles stand beyond a circle of 58 about the centre, on which two petals meet in a notch. A white stamen lies along each petal from the middle, the space where two circles of one width cross. The middle is a disc of 10 with a white dot, in a white ring. The petal that points up and the one that points right are built in full, and the other two where they stand.

丸い芯のまわりに、花びらが四枚あります。左右の二枚は上下の二枚より長く、花全体が菱形におさまります。中心を通る幅2の白い線が二本、花びらを分けます。花びらの先は三つの丸い山で、山の円は、中心のまわりの半径58の円の外に置きます。となりの花びらとは、この円の上の切れ込みで出会います。花びらには、芯から白いしべが一本ずつ伸びます。しべは、同じ幅の二つの円が重なるところです。芯は半径10の円で、中に白い点があり、まわりを白い輪が囲みます。上の花びらと右の花びらを作図し、残りの二枚はその場で作図します。

Measured: The 花びし of the 1892 crest book (frame 8, left page, row 1, column 1 from the right), read on a grid as parts of half its width. The flower is 0.71 as high as it is wide. The notches between the petals stand 0.45 across and 0.36 up, so 0.58 from the centre, on lines through the centre. The petal that points up reaches 0.72, with side lobes that reach 0.45 across at 0.45 up; the petal that points to the side reaches 1.0, with side lobes that reach 0.34 up at 0.62 across. The ring in the middle is 0.12, and the stamens run out from it to 0.32 above and below and to 0.38 at the sides. The book draws it by hand, with the left petal longer than the right; the drawing makes the four quarters alike.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 8, left page, first row, first column counted from the right: 花びし (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から一列目「花びし」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

18 guides, 5 shapes, 37 steps.

## Four-way flower lozenge · 四方花菱 (`shiho-hanabishi`)

The flower lozenge with four petals of one size, a quarter turn apart, so that the flower fills a circle and not a lozenge. Two lines through the centre, one rising and one falling one in one, part the petals, each a white line 2 wide. A petal is three round lobes: a middle one of 25 with its tip on the outer circle, and a shoulder of 18 each side that runs into the line beside it; two petals meet in a notch on a circle of 64 about the centre. A white stamen lies along each petal from the middle, and the middle is the flower lozenge's own: a disc of 10 with a white dot in it and a white ring round it. The petal that points up is built in full, and the other three where they stand, clockwise.

花菱の花びら四枚を同じ大きさにして、四分の一回転ずつずらして置いた形です。花全体は菱形ではなく、円におさまります。中心を通る右上がりと右下がりの二本の線（幅2の白い線）が、花びらを分けます。花びらの先は三つの丸い山です。まん中の山は半径25で、先が外の円にとどきます。左右の山は半径18で、となりの白い線につながります。となりの花びらとは、中心のまわりの半径64の円の上の切れ込みで出会います。花びらには、芯から白いしべが一本ずつ伸びます。芯は花菱と同じで、半径10の円の中に白い点があり、まわりを白い輪が囲みます。上の花びらを作図し、残りの三枚は時計回りにその場で作図します。

Measured: The 四方花びし of the 1892 crest book (frame 8, left page, row 1, column 3 from the right), read on a grid as parts of the flower's half width, which is 0.97 of the layout circle on average over its four petals (the book's are 0.91 to 1.03, drawn by hand and a few degrees off square). The lines between the petals stand 45 degrees from upright and end in notches 0.57 to 0.67 from the centre, 0.64 here. A petal's middle lobe is 0.5 across with its tip at the full width; its shoulders are 0.35 across, their middles 0.33 to each side and 0.58 out. A stamen runs from the middle to 0.37. The middle and the white lines are the flower lozenge's own.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 8, left page, first row, third column counted from the right: 四方花びし (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から三列目「四方花びし」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

12 guides, 5 shapes, 27 steps.

## Flower lozenge in a hexagon · 亀甲に花菱 (`kikko-ni-hanabishi`)

A tortoise-shell hexagon, one corner up: a thick band, a narrow white line, a thin line, and inside it the flower lozenge, its side petals a narrow white line from the thin one.

角を上にした亀甲です。太い帯、細い白線、細い線の順に重ね、その内側に花菱を収めます。左右の花びらの先は、細い線から白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, right page, second row, second column counted from the right: 亀甲, a hexagon of one thick and two thin lines round a four-petalled flower (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「亀甲」（太い線一本と細い線二本の六角形に、四枚の花びらの花が入った形。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

44 guides, 7 shapes, 70 steps.

## Flower lozenge in three hexagons in a circle · 丸に三つ盛り亀甲に花菱 (`maru-ni-mitsumori-kikko-ni-hanabishi`)

The three hexagons with their flower lozenges inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, the corners of the hexagons a narrow white line from it.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、花菱の入った三つの亀甲を収め、亀甲の角を輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, right page, first row, second column counted from the right: 三ッきっこう, set inside a ring (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「三ッきっこう」（輪に入れた形）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

128 guides, 22 shapes, 178 steps.

## Flower lozenge in three hexagons · 三つ盛り亀甲に花菱 (`mitsumori-kikko-ni-hanabishi`)

Three tortoise-shell hexagons, one corner up, one above two, each a narrow white line from the next, each with a thick band, a narrow white line, a thin line and a flower lozenge: the top one first, then the lower right and the lower left.

角を上にした亀甲を三つ、品の字に並べます。隣どうしは細い白線一本分だけ離します。どの亀甲も、太い帯、細い白線、細い線の順に重ね、その内側に花菱を収めます。上、右下、左下の順に作図します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 41, right page, first row, second column counted from the right: 三ッきっこう (three hexagons, one above two, with a flower in each; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「三ッきっこう」（品の字に並んだ三つの亀甲に、それぞれ花が入った形。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

127 guides, 21 shapes, 174 steps.

## Four flower lozenges · 四つ花菱 (`yotsu-hanabishi`)

Four flower lozenges laid in a lozenge: one above, one at the right, one below and one at the left, the two at the sides a little wider apart than the two above and below. Each is the flower lozenge, built on its own centre where it stands, from the top clockwise.

花菱を四つ、菱形に並べます。左右の二つは、上下の二つより少し離して置きます。上から時計回りに、それぞれの中心でその場で花菱を作図します。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 9, right page, fourth row, first column counted from the right: 四ッ花ひし (four flower lozenges in a lozenge; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「四ッ花ひし」（菱形に並んだ四つの花菱。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

52 guides, 20 shapes, 89 steps.

## Sword flower lozenge · 剣花菱 (`ken-hanabishi`)

Four scalloped petals fill a broad lozenge, with a dotted round heart and a short white stamen in each petal. Four short sword points stand diagonally between them. The upright petals are shorter than the side petals.

先が三つにふくらむ花びら四枚を、横に広い菱形に並べます。中央の丸には点を入れ、各花びらに短い白いしべを入れます。花びらの間に剣先を斜めに四つ置きます。上下の花びらは左右より短くします。

Measured: Frame 8: the upright petals reach about 80 in a side reach of 100, and the heart is about 22 across. The upper right sword point is near (60, 53) in that frame. Each petal has one tip lobe and two shoulders struck from circles.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 8, left page, third row, first column from the right: 剣花びし (左頁、三段目、右から一列目「剣花びし」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

30 guides, 9 shapes, 55 steps.

## Sword flower lozenge in a circle · 丸に剣花菱 (`maru-ni-ken-hanabishi`)

The sword flower lozenge sits in a thin ring. The two broad side petals leave a narrow space to the inside of the ring.

剣花菱を細輪の中に収めます。左右の幅広い花びらと輪の内側の間に細いすき間を空けます。

Measured: Frame 8: the labelled ringed cell surrounds four scalloped petals and four diagonal sword points with one thin band. The side petals reach about five sixths of the ring's outer radius.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 8, left page, first row, second column from the right: 丸にけん花菱 (左頁、一段目、右から二列目「丸にけん花菱」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

32 guides, 10 shapes, 61 steps.

## Two bars · 二つ引両 (`futatsu-hikiryo`)

Two level bars, each 0.30 of the crest's width high, the white between them 0.24. Two upright and four level lines are ruled full length; the outer corners stand on the outer circle.

横棒を二本並べます。棒の太さは全体の幅の0.30、間の白は0.24です。縦の直線二本と横の直線四本を端から端まで引き、外側の角は外円の上に置きます。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest soroi Futatu Hiki, read along the upright line through the centre and across the bars in its own pixels (688 across): the bars 167 high each, the white between them 131, the whole 556 across and 465 high, so a bar is 0.300 of the width and the white 0.236, and the four outer corners stand 362 from the centre.

7 guides, 2 shapes, 14 steps.

## One bar in a circle · 丸に一つ引両 (`maru-ni-hitotsu-hikiryo`)

One level bar across the middle of a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, the bar twice as thick as the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪に、真ん中を通る横棒を一本渡します。棒の太さは輪の2倍です。

4 guides, 2 shapes, 12 steps.

## Three bars · 三つ引両 (`mitsu-hikiryo`)

Three level bars, the middle one 0.26 of the width high and the outer two 0.27, a white of 0.018 between them. Two upright and six level lines are ruled full length; the outer corners stand on the outer circle.

横棒を三本並べます。真ん中の棒の太さは幅の0.26、外の二本は0.27で、間に0.018の細い白を残します。縦の直線二本と横の直線六本を端から端まで引き、外側の角は外円の上に置きます。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest mitu Hikiryou, read along the upright line through the centre in its own pixels (688 across): the bars 150, 145 and 150 high and the two whites 10 each, the whole 556 across and 465 high, so the middle bar is 0.261 of the width, an outer one 0.270 and a white 0.018, and the four outer corners stand 362 from the centre.

9 guides, 3 shapes, 18 steps.

## Two bars in a circle · 丸に二つ引両 (`ni-hikiryo`)

Two level bars across a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, each bar a little thicker than the white between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪に、横棒を二本渡します。棒は、間の白より少し太くします。

6 guides, 3 shapes, 16 steps.

## Three separate bars in a circle · 丸に離れ三つ引両 (`maru-ni-hanare-mitsu-hikiryo`)

Three level bars, the middle one 35 high and the others 32.5, 17.5 of white between them, standing clear of a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick: each bar's ends are arcs of one circle 5.7 inside the ring. Six level lines are ruled full length.

水平な棒を三本、間に17.5の白をはさんで並べます。真ん中の棒は高さ35、上下の棒は32.5です。直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に収め、棒の両端は、輪の5.7内側にある一つの円の弧で、輪には触れません。水平の直線六本を端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 丸ニはなれ三ッ引 (frame 43, left page, row 3, column 3 from the right: the cell stands to the left of its label, which is on its right), three bars inside a ring drawn as two outlines, read as the positions of its lines in the frame's own pixels: the ring's outer outline 312 across (156 out) and its inner one 265 across (132 out), so the ring is 0.075 of the diameter thick, between the package's thin ring (0.033) and its medium one (0.087) and nearer the medium; three bars of 53, 56 and 53 high with a white of 29 between them, centred on the ring; the bars' ends 8 to 10 short of the ring's inner outline at the sides, and the top and bottom bars' outer edges 20 short of it. The drawing uses the package's medium ring, and bars between level lines at 17.5, 35 and 67.5 from the middle, each bar ending on a circle of 73.5 about the centre, 5.7 inside the ring, so that every bar stands clear of it.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, left page, row 3, third column from the right: 丸ニはなれ三ッ引 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、三段目、右から三列目「丸ニはなれ三ッ引」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 4 shapes, 22 steps.

## Three bars in a circle · 丸に三つ引両 (`maru-ni-mitsu-hikiryo`)

Three level bars across a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, each bar a little thinner than the white between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪に、横棒を三本渡します。棒は、間の白より少し細くします。

8 guides, 4 shapes, 20 steps.

## Two upright bars in a circle · 丸に竪二つ引両 (`maru-ni-tate-futatsu-hikiryo`)

Two upright bars across a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, each bar as wide as the white between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪に、縦棒を二本渡します。棒の幅は、二本の間の白と同じです。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Maru ni tate futatsu hikiryo, read along the level line through the centre as parts of the ring's diameter: ring 0.084, each bar 0.167 wide with the same of white between, the bars' centres 0.167 either side of the middle.

6 guides, 3 shapes, 16 steps.

## Niwa crossed bars · 丹羽違い棒 (`niwa-chigai-bo`)

Two bars crossed in an X, each a fifth as wide as it is long, at 38 degrees above and below the level line, the corners of their ends on the outer circle. Every edge is ruled full length, and the crest is the space where the bars lie.

二本の棒をX字に交差させます。棒の太さは長さの約5分の1で、水平から上下に38度ずつ傾け、両端の角は外円の上に置きます。辺はどれも端から端まで引き、棒の重なった形が家紋になります。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 丹羽ちがひ棒 (frame 43, left page, row 4, column 4 from the right), two bars crossed, each with square ends, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: each bar 62 across and 322 and 308 long from end to end, one falling to the right at 41 degrees from the level line and the other rising at 35, so the outer corners stand 160 from the middle. The drawing makes the two bars alike and mirrored, each 37 across and 188.4 long, at 38 degrees, the ends' corners on the outer circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, left page, row 4, fourth column from the right: 丹羽ちがひ棒 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「丹羽ちがひ棒」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 1 shapes, 16 steps.

## Three upright bars in a circle · 丸に竪三つ引両 (`maru-ni-tate-mitsu-hikiryo`)

Three upright bars across a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, each bar a little narrower than the white between them.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪に、縦棒を三本渡します。棒は、間の白より少し細くします。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Maru ni tate mitu Hiki, as numbers in its own units: bars 80 wide, their centres 164 apart, the ring's outer radius 343: of the ring's radius, bars 0.233 wide and 0.478 apart. Its ring is thicker (0.102 of the diameter) than the package's 0.087, which is kept.

8 guides, 4 shapes, 20 steps.

## Single lozenge · 一つ菱 (`hitotsu-bishi`)

One lozenge wider than it is high. Its side corners are where the level line through the centre meets the outer circle, and its top and bottom corners are where the upright line meets a circle 0.69 of the radius; each side is a line ruled full length.

横の長い菱形を一つ置きます。左右の角は、中心を通る横の線が外円と交わる点、上下の角は、縦の線が半径の0.69の円と交わる点です。辺はどれも端から端まで引いた直線です。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Kamon Maru ni Hitotsubishi, read along the level and upright lines through the centre as parts of the ring's diameter: the lozenge 0.798 across and 0.551 high, so 0.69; its ring 0.090. No unringed picture was found, so the lozenge is the ringed crest's.

8 guides, 1 shapes, 14 steps.

## Pine-bark lozenge · 松皮菱 (`matsukawa-bishi`)

Three lozenges stood one on another, the middle one the largest, so that the two sides step in where they meet. The middle lozenge's corners are 1 to the left and right of the centre and 0.57 above and below it; the other two have their centres 0.43 above and below the centre, and their corners 0.54 to the left and right and 0.35 above and below their own centres.

三つの菱形を縦に重ね、真ん中を最も大きくして、重なるところで両側が内へ切れ込むようにします。中央の菱の角は、中心から左右に1、上下に0.57の距離にあります。上の菱と下の菱は、中心から0.43上と下に中心を置き、その角はそれぞれの中心から左右に0.54、上下に0.35の距離にあります。

17 guides, 1 shapes, 24 steps.

## Single lozenge in a circle · 丸に一つ菱 (`maru-ni-hitotsu-bishi`)

The single lozenge laid out inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its side corners a narrow white from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に、左右の角が輪に触れないよう細い白を残して、一つ菱を割り付けます。

Measured: The same picture as the single lozenge: ring 0.088 to 0.092 of the diameter, white between ring and lozenge 0.012, lozenge 0.798 across and 0.551 high.

10 guides, 2 shapes, 20 steps.

## Two lozenges · 二つ菱 (`futatsu-bishi`)

Two lozenges of one size, one a little below the other so that the lower one lies over the upper: the lower is whole, and the upper is left with a narrow white line between it and the edge of the lower. The lozenges are 90 wide to each side and 52 high, their centres 50 apart, so the tips stand inside the layout circle.

同じ大きさの菱を二つ、一つを少し下にずらして重ねます。下の菱は上の菱の手前にあってそのまま残り、上の菱は、下の菱の縁との間に細い白線を残します。菱は中心から左右に90、上下に52で、二つの中心は50離れ、先は外の円の内側にあります。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, left page, second row, first column counted from the right: 二ッひし (two lozenges, the lower over the upper; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から一列目「二ッひし」（上の菱に下の菱が重なった二つの菱。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 2 shapes, 18 steps.

## Three-storey lozenge · 三階菱 (`sangai-bishi`)

Three lozenges stood one on another on the upright line through the centre, the top one the smallest, so that the two sides step out at each storey. Their side corners stand 0.41, 0.64 and 0.97 of the radius out from the upright line, and their side corners stand 0.56, 0.19 above and 0.24 below the centre, in that order. The lowest lozenge's side corners stand on the outer circle, and each lozenge's sides are lines ruled full length.

三つの菱形を、中心を通る縦の線の上に、いちばん上を最も小さくして重ねます。上から下へ、段ごとに両側が外へ広がります。横の角は、上の菱から順に、中心を通る縦の線から左右に、半径の0.41、0.64、0.97の距離にあります。その高さは、中心から上へ0.56、上へ0.19、下へ0.24の位置です。いちばん下の菱の横の角は外円の上に置き、どの菱も辺は端から端まで引いた直線です。

Measured: Two public pictures: the Wikimedia Commons pictures Japanese crest Sanngai Hisi ni itutu Kuginuki (the three storeys read off row by row, the five nail-pullers below them left out) and Japanese crest sanngai bisi. In both the storeys' half-widths are 0.42, 0.66 and 1 of the lowest's (the two agree to 0.003), their side corners 0.15, 0.38 and 0.65 of the height down, and the height 0.83 and 0.82 of the width.

14 guides, 1 shapes, 19 steps.

## Stacked three-storey lozenge · 重ね三階菱 (`kasane-sangai-bishi`)

Three whole lozenges, one over another on the upright line through the centre, each larger than the one above and lying under it, with a narrow white between neighbours. Their side corners stand 0.57, 0.77 and 0.96 of the radius out, and the white is the lozenge above struck a little larger about its own centre, so that its sides run parallel to the lozenge's at that distance. Every side is a line ruled full length.

三つの菱形を、中心を通る縦の線の上に、一つずつ下に重ねて置きます。下の菱ほど大きく、上の菱の下に隠れ、となり合う菱の間に細い白を残します。横の角は、上の菱から順に、中心を通る縦の線から左右に、半径の0.57、0.77、0.96の距離にあります。白は、上の菱を、その中心はそのままに少し大きく広げた形で、辺はもとの菱の辺と平行に、一定の間隔を置いて走ります。辺はどれも端から端まで引いた直線です。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest kasane Sanngai Hisi, read row by row in its own pixels (the crest 808 across): the three lozenges' side corners 240, 324 and 404 out at 285, 449 and 615 down, their top corners at 138, 253 and 368 (the lowest one's bottom corner then 861, as far below the middle as the top is above it), their sides all at the same slope (1.65 across to 1), and the white 15 across the level, 7.8 at right angles to the sides.

22 guides, 3 shapes, 31 steps.

## Takeda lozenges · 武田菱 (`takeda-bishi`)

A lozenge three wide to two high, cut into four like lozenges by a narrow white cross that runs parallel to its sides.

横3に対して縦2の菱形を、辺と平行な細い白い十字で、同じ形の四つの菱に分けます。

16 guides, 4 shapes, 28 steps.

## Split lozenge · 割菱 (`wari-bishi`)

A lozenge four wide to three high, cut into four like lozenges by a narrow white cross that runs along its sides. Its side corners stand on the outer circle, and its top and bottom corners on a circle three quarters of it. Ten lines are ruled full length: the two through the centre, the four sides, and the two edges of each white band.

横4に対して縦3の菱形を、辺と平行な細い白い十字で、同じ形の四つの菱に分けます。左右の角は外円の上、上下の角は外円の4分の3の円の上にあります。直線は十本、端から端まで引きます。中心を通る縦と横の線、四つの辺、二本の白い帯それぞれの両側の線です。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest wari Hisi, read in its own units: the four lozenges 577 across and 437 high, so 0.757 high to wide (ours 0.75, four to three), and the white between them 16 across the upright line, 0.073 of the half-height (ours 0.08). The 1892 crest book draws the same four lozenges in a ring and names them 九こ武田ひし (frame 43, right page, row 4, column 2 from the right): 0.78 high to wide in its hand. The ringed 丸に割菱 (`maru-ni-wari-bishi`) already has this lozenge; this is it with no ring, at the full layout circle.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, right page, fourth row, second column counted from the right: 九こ武田ひし, the same four lozenges in a ring (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「九こ武田ひし」、輪の中の同じ四つの菱（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-25.

12 guides, 4 shapes, 24 steps.

## Split lozenge in a circle · 丸に割菱 (`maru-ni-wari-bishi`)

A lozenge four wide to three high, cut into four like lozenges by a narrow white cross that runs along its sides, laid out inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick.

横4に対して縦3の菱形を、辺と平行な細い白い十字で、同じ形の四つの菱に分けます。これを、直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に割り付けます。

14 guides, 5 shapes, 30 steps.

## Five lozenges · 五つ菱 (`itsutsu-bishi`)

Five lozenges of one size: one in the middle, and one above, at the right, below and at the left, each a narrow white line from the next. The middle one lies over the other four, which are left with a narrow white line between them and its edges. The lozenges are 44 wide to each side and 30 high.

同じ大きさの菱を五つ置きます。一つは真ん中、あとは上、右、下、左です。となり合う菱どうしは細い白線一本分だけ離します。真ん中の菱が手前にあり、ほかの四つは、真ん中の菱の縁との間に細い白線を残します。菱は中心から左右に44、上下に30です。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, right page, first row, fourth column counted from the right: 五ッひし (five lozenges, the middle one over the four round it; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「五ッひし」（真ん中の菱がまわりの四つの上に重なった五つの菱。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

23 guides, 5 shapes, 36 steps.

## Five stones · 五つ石 (`itsutsu-ishi`)

Five squares of one size, each with its corners up, down, left and right, joined corner to corner: one in the middle, and one above, at the right, below and at the left, each touching the middle one at a corner. The squares are 32 to each side and 32 up and down, so the two side corners stand on the layout circle.

同じ大きさの、角を上下左右に向けた正方形を五つ、角と角でつなぎます。一つは真ん中、あとは上、右、下、左で、どれも角で真ん中の正方形に触れます。正方形は中心から左右に32、上下に32で、左右の二つの角は外の円の上にあります。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 7, right page, first row, third column counted from the right: 五ッ石 (five squares with their corners up, down, left and right, joined corner to corner; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「五ッ石」（角と角でつながった五つの正方形。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 5 shapes, 22 steps.

## Linked lozenges · 繋ぎ菱 (`tsunagi-bishi`)

Five lozenges of one size joined tip to tip: one in the middle, and one above, at the right, below and at the left, each touching the middle one at a tip. The lozenges are 32 wide to each side and 22 high, so the two side tips stand on the layout circle.

同じ大きさの菱を五つ、先と先でつなぎます。一つは真ん中、あとは上、右、下、左で、どれも先で真ん中の菱に触れます。菱は中心から左右に32、上下に22で、左右の二つの先は外の円の上にあります。

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 43, right page, fourth row, fourth column counted from the right: つなぎひし (five lozenges joined tip to tip; measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から四列目「つなぎひし」（先と先でつながった五つの菱。寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

9 guides, 5 shapes, 22 steps.

## Left manji · 左万字 (`hidari-manji`)

A square of nine equal cells, three by three, with four narrow white slits: from each side a slit one cell long runs in beside the middle cell, all four turning the same way, so the top arm bends left. A cell is 0.31 of the width and a slit 0.03. Twelve lines are ruled full length; the outer corners stand on the outer circle.

同じ大きさのマス目を縦横に三つずつ並べた正方形に、細い白いすき間を四つ入れます。すき間は各辺からマス目一つ分の長さで、中央のマス目の横まで入ります。四つとも同じ向きにそろえるので、いちばん上の腕は左に曲がります。マス目は正方形の幅の0.31、すき間は0.03です。直線十二本を端から端まで引き、外側の角は外円の上に置きます。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Maru ni Hidari Mannji, read along the level line through the centre and the rows above it in its own pixels (688 across): the square 326 across, every cell 102 and every slit 10 (0.314 and 0.031 of the width), and read against the ringed manji of the 1892 crest book (frame 30, right page, first row, fourth column: 丸にまんじ), where the square is 0.555 of the ring's outer width, the picture's being 0.586. No unringed picture was found, so the manji is the ringed crest's, its corners on the outer circle.

13 guides, 1 shapes, 18 steps.

## Loose manji · 万字崩し (`manji-kuzushi`)

A square on its corner has four narrow slits, one entering each side. Every slit branches once, with its shorter branch parallel to the side it entered. The four patterns turn the same way.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形の四つの辺に、細いすき間を一つずつ入れます。どのすき間も途中で一度分かれ、短い方は入ってきた辺に平行に伸びます。四つとも同じ向きに回します。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: Each side of the rotated square in the print spans about 225 pixels. A white slit about 6 pixels wide enters about one fifth of a side from its midpoint, reaches towards the middle and sends a branch along the side of the square. The drawing uses a slit 3.8 wide and preserves the branching on all four sides.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, row 2, second column from the right: まんじくづし (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から二列目「まんじくづし」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

29 guides, 1 shapes, 34 steps.

## Round manji · 万字丸 (`manji-maru`)

Four white openings cut into a round disc. Each has a compass arc from a common inner circle and a straight-sided channel reaching towards the centre. The four openings turn a quarter turn apart.

丸い地に四つの形を白く抜きます。それぞれの外側は内円の弧を使い、中心へ向かう部分の辺は直線で引きます。四つの白抜きを4分の1回転ずつ離して置きます。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: The disk in the book is about 299 pixels across. The four isolated cutouts follow a second circle of radius about 131, then turn down along straight sides about 32 pixels apart. The drawing uses radius 96 outside and 84 for the round edges of the cutouts, with the inward white channels 20 wide.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, row 1, second column from the right: まんじ丸 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から二列目「まんじ丸」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

22 guides, 1 shapes, 28 steps.

## Corner-up manji in a circle · 丸に隅立て万字 (`maru-ni-sumitate-manji`)

A square stands on its corner inside a thin ring. One narrow slit runs in from each of its four sides beside the middle, with the same turn at every side. A narrow gap separates the four tips from the ring.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を細い輪に収めます。四つの辺から中心のそばまで細いすき間を一つずつ入れ、どれも同じ向きにそろえます。四つの先と輪の間には細い白いすき間を残します。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: The ring in the cell spans about 294 pixels and is about 16 thick. The motif measures about 253 pixels from one tip to the opposite one. The sides are turned 45 degrees, and the four narrow white slits are cut a little to one side of the midpoint of each edge. The drawing uses the thin ring and places the four tips on a circle of radius 82.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, row 4, first column from the right: 丸ニ角立まんじ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「丸ニ角立まんじ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

14 guides, 2 shapes, 22 steps.

## Right manji · 右万字 (`migi-manji`)

The left manji turned the other way: the same cells and slits, mirrored, so the top arm bends right. Every line is ruled in place.

左万字を左右反転した形です。マス目もすき間も同じで、いちばん上の腕が右に曲がります。直線はどれもその場で引きます。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: The same picture as the left manji, turned the other way: the same cells and slits, mirrored about the upright line through the centre.

13 guides, 1 shapes, 18 steps.

## Left manji in a circle · 丸に左万字 (`maru-ni-hidari-manji`)

The left manji inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, its corners a narrow white from the ring, struck in place.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に左万字を置きます。角は輪から細い白線一本分だけ離し、その場で描きます。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Japanese Crest Maru ni Hidari Mannji, read along the level line through the centre in its own pixels (688 across): the ring 556 across and 40 thick (0.072 of the diameter; the package's ring is 0.087, which is kept), the square 326 across, so its corners stand 7.5 inside the ring's inner edge, every cell 102 and every slit 10. The 1892 crest book's 丸にまんじ (frame 30, right page, first row, fourth column) draws the same square in a ring.

14 guides, 2 shapes, 22 steps.

## Four joined manji · 四つ組み合い万字 (`yotsu-kumiai-manji`)

Four equal, broad arms meet at the centre. Narrow slots cut their edges into repeating square bends. The central opening follows four ruled bends, one each quarter turn.

同じ大きさの太い腕を四つ、中央でつなぎます。縁に細い切れ込みを入れ、直角に曲がる形をくり返します。中央の白抜きは4分の1回転ずつ直角に曲がる直線の形です。

Note: The manji (卍) is a Buddhist sign used as a Japanese family crest for centuries; it is unrelated to the Nazi swastika. (卍（まんじ）は仏教のしるしで、古くから日本の家紋に使われてきました。ナチスのハーケンクロイツとは関係がありません。)

Measured: The cell has four broad arms reaching about 154 pixels from the middle and about 120 across. The outer slits are about 8 pixels across. The drawing makes the arms straight and equal and retains both the outer slits and the four bends of the central white opening.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, row 3, second column from the right: 四ッ組合まんじ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「四ッ組合まんじ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

49 guides, 1 shapes, 54 steps.

## Four eyes · 四つ目結 (`yotsume-yui`)

Four squares stood on their corners in a square, a narrow white cross between them, and a small square eye in the middle of each.

角で立てた正方形を四つ、細い白い十字で分けて正方形に並べ、それぞれの中央に小さな四角い目をあけます。

23 guides, 4 shapes, 34 steps.

## Three eyes · 三つ目 (`mitsume`)

Three squares stood on their corners at the corners of a right-angled triangle, the right angle at the top, a narrow white line between neighbours, each with a small square eye. The circle through the three outer corners is the layout circle; the upright and level lines through its centre meet the squares' corners, and the squares' sides are ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を三つ、直角三角形の頂点に置きます。直角は上で、となり合う正方形の間には細い白線を残し、どの正方形にも中央に小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。外側の三つの角を通る円が外円です。中心を通る縦と横の直線が正方形の角と交わり、正方形の辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 三ッ目 (frame 20, right page, row 1, column 4 from the right), three squares stood on their corners at the corners of a right-angled triangle, each with a small square eye, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: a square 147 across its corners, the centres of the left and right squares 162 apart and the top one 80 above their middle, so the circle through the three outer corners is 154.5 about the middle of the long side, a gap of 10.6 between neighbouring squares (0.10 of a side) and an eye 27 across its corners (0.18 of the square's). The drawing takes the layout circle as that circle: the centres 50 from its middle and the corners 46 beyond them, a gap of 5.7 (0.09 of a side) and an eye 8 from its middle to a corner.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 1, fourth column from the right: 三ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から四列目「三ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 3 shapes, 28 steps.

## Lozenge four eyes · 菱四つ目 (`hishi-yotsume`)

Four lozenges wider than they are high, 90 across and 68 up, one above, one below, one to the left and one to the right of the middle, a narrow white line between neighbours, each with a small lozenge eye. The side corners of the left and right ones are on the outer circle; the upright and level lines through the centre meet every corner, and the sides are ruled full length.

幅90、高さ68の横長の菱形を、中心の上、下、左、右に四つ置き、となり合う菱形の間は細い白線一本分あけ、どれも中央に小さな菱形の目を白く抜きます。左右の菱形の横の角は外円の上にあり、中心を通る縦と横の直線がすべての角と交わり、辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's ひし四ッ目 (frame 20, right page, row 1, column 1 from the right), four lozenges wider than high, one above, one below, one to the left and one to the right of the middle, each with a small lozenge eye, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: a lozenge 144 across and 109 high (72 and 55 out), the centres of the left and right ones 82 from the middle and of the upper and lower ones 63, so the far corners stand 153 from the middle across and 118 up, the gap between neighbours is 16 up the page (0.15 of a lozenge's height) and an eye is 39 across and 26 high (0.27 and 0.24 of the lozenge). The drawing takes the side corners on the outer circle: lozenges 45 out across and 34 up, centres 51 across and 39 up, an eye 12 across and 8 up.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 1, first column from the right: ひし四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「ひし四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

35 guides, 4 shapes, 46 steps.

## Four nested eyes · 掛け四つ目 (`kake-yotsume`)

Four eyes stand on the upright and level axes. Each has two nested square bands with their corners upright, a broad white space between the bands and a small square opening at the centre.

縦横の中心線に四つの目を置きます。それぞれ角を上下左右に向けた正方形の帯を二重にして、帯の間を広く白く取り、真ん中に小さな四角い穴を残します。

Measured: Each of the four eyes contains two nested square bands. The largest eyes span about 135 pixels between their corners, and their centres lie about 80 pixels from the centre of the crest. The smallest white openings are about 24 pixels wide across the corners. The drawing evens out the printed edges and keeps the four eyes apart.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, left page, row 2, fourth column from the right: かけ四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「かけ四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

35 guides, 4 shapes, 46 steps.

## Knotted four eyes · 結び四つ目 (`musubi-yotsume`)

Four squares stood on their corners, with centres 56 from the middle on the upright and level axes. A broad diamond band runs between them, with its corners 21 and 50 from the middle. The ruled boundaries divide their crossings into alternating black and white spaces.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を四つ、縦横の中心線の上に、中心が全体の中心から56離れるように置きます。その間を太い菱形の帯で結び、内側と外側の角は中心から21と50の位置に置きます。交わるところは、定規で引いた線で囲まれた部分を、黒と白に交互に分けます。

Measured: The four squares in the cell reach about 150 pixels from the middle, their centres about 90 out, and the small openings about 38 across their corners. The near and far edges of the connecting diamond reach about 33 and 80 from the middle. The drawing uses centres 56 out, openings 24 across, and connecting edges 21 and 50 out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 4, second column from the right: むすび四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から二列目「むすび四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

27 guides, 1 shapes, 32 steps.

## Twisted four eyes · 捻じ四つ目 (`neji-yotsume`)

Place four square eyes on their corners, turning a quarter of a turn between them. Each square covers one corner of the eye beside it. The ruled outlines leave a small open notch at each crossing.

角を上下左右に向けた目を四つ置き、四分の一回転ずつ回します。一つの目が隣の目の角に重なります。交差するところには、直線で囲んだ小さなくぼみを残します。

Measured: Four squares on point overlap at their inner corners. Each has a small square opening. The next square crosses the opening's inner edge, leaving its outer part visible. The crossing order repeats around the centre.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 2, first column from the right: 捻ぢ四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「捻ぢ四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

41 guides, 4 shapes, 52 steps.

## Linked four eyes · 繋ぎ四つ目 (`tsunagi-yotsume`)

Four squares stood on their corners, two above two, each touching the next at a corner, so that a small diamond of white is left in the middle; each has a small square eye. The eight outer corners stand on the layout circle, and the sides of the squares are ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、上に二つ、下に二つ並べ、となり合う正方形は角で触れ合わせます。中心には小さな菱形の白が残ります。どの正方形にも中央に小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。外側の八つの角は外円の上にあり、正方形の辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's つなぎ四ッ目 (frame 19, left page, row 4, column 4 from the right), four squares stood on their corners, two above two, each corner touching the next one's, each with a small square eye, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: a square 159 across its corners, the centres 76 right and left and 73 above and below the middle, and the eight outer corners 170 from the middle, with an eye 39 across its corners (0.25 of the square's). The drawing makes the squares touch exactly, so the eight outer corners stand on the layout circle: a square 43 out to its corners, the centres 43 from the middle each way, an eye 10.5 out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, left page, row 4, fourth column from the right: つなぎ四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「つなぎ四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

23 guides, 4 shapes, 34 steps.

## Sideways four eyes · 横四つ目 (`yoko-yotsume`)

Four squares stood on their corners, one above, one below, one to the left and one to the right of the middle, a gap between neighbours a third of a side wide, each with a small square eye. The circle through the four outer corners is the layout circle; the upright and level lines through its centre meet the corners, and the squares' sides are ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を四つ、中心の上、下、左、右に置きます。となり合う正方形の間は一辺の3分の1ほどあけ、どの正方形にも中央に小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。外側の四つの角を通る円が外円です。中心を通る縦と横の直線が角と交わり、正方形の辺は端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 横四ッ目 (frame 19, left page, row 2, column 3 from the right), four squares stood on their corners at the top, bottom, left and right, each with a small square eye, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: the centres 89 above, below, left and right of the middle of the crest, a square 134 across its corners (67 out), a gap of 32 between neighbouring squares (0.34 of a side) and an eye 46 across its corners (0.34 of the square's). The drawing takes the circle through the four outer corners as the layout circle: the centres 55 from its middle and the corners 41 beyond them, an eye 14 from its middle to a corner.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, left page, row 2, third column from the right: 横四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から三列目「横四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

19 guides, 4 shapes, 30 steps.

## Broken four eyes · 四つ目崩し (`yotsume-kuzushi`)

Two ruled triangular pieces form a quarter of the design. Each has a small white triangular cut at its edge. The four quarters face the same direction around the middle so that the pointed pieces form a pinwheel.

直線で引いた三角形を二つ使って4分の1を作り、縁に小さな三角の白を入れます。4分の1ごとに同じ向きで回して、先のとがった形を風車のように並べます。

Measured: The cell has four pairs of ruled triangular points turning around a common centre. The farthest points are about 153 pixels from the centre. Each pair has a small triangular opening along the edge of each piece. The drawing gives the four pairs exactly one quarter turn between them.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 4, first column from the right: 四ッ目くづし (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「四ッ目くづし」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

31 guides, 4 shapes, 42 steps.

## Five eyes · 五つ目 (`itsutsume`)

One square on its corner overlaps four more on the upright and level axes. Each has a small square eye. The front square cuts across the four back openings, with a narrow white gap along the overlap.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を一つ、縦横の中心線に沿って置いた四つの正方形に重ねます。一つずつ小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。手前の正方形は奥の四つの目に重なり、重なる縁に沿って細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The four outside squares reach about 155 pixels from the middle. Their centres are about 81 out, the centre square's corners about 79 out, and the five small openings about 34 across their corners. The drawing places the four centres 51 units out, the centre square's tips 49 out and the openings 20 across. A narrow gap follows the centre square's edge.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 4, third column from the right: 五ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から三列目「五ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

31 guides, 5 shapes, 44 steps.

## Six eyes in a circle · 丸に六つ目 (`maru-ni-mutsume`)

Six squares stood on their corners in two rows of three, their tips meeting at 48-unit intervals. Each square has a 12-unit opening measured across its corners. The whole grid sits inside a thin ring.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、横に三つずつ二段に並べ、48の間隔で角を触れ合わせます。一つずつ角から角まで12の目を白く抜き、全体を細い輪に収めます。

Measured: The 1892 cell has a ring about 305 pixels across and 14 thick. The two rows of three squares span about 236 by 147 pixels, and each opening spans about 19 pixels. The drawing squares up the rows at 144 by 96 units, with centres 48 apart, openings 12 across the corners and a ring of the package's thin weight.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 1, third column from the right: 丸ニ六ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「丸ニ六ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

28 guides, 7 shapes, 46 steps.

## Nine eyes · 九つ目 (`kokonotsume`)

A square stood on its corner, its corners on the layout circle, cut into three by three equal squares by two ruled lines each way, a narrow white line where they meet, and a small square eye in each. The upright and level lines through the centre meet its corners, and every line is ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、角を外円の上に置いて、縦横三つずつの同じ大きさの正方形に分けます。分け目は直線を二本ずつ引いて細い白線にし、どの正方形にも小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。中心を通る縦と横の直線が角と交わり、直線はどれも端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 九ッ目 (frame 20, right page, row 2, column 3 from the right), a diamond cut into three by three squares by shared lines, each square with a small square eye, read as the positions of its corners and lines in the frame's own pixels: the whole 163 across and 160 high out from the middle (so a square is 75 on a side), an eye 25 across its corners (0.24 of the square's side across its own corners). The drawing takes the diamond's corners on the layout circle, two lines each way at a third of the way across, a white line of 2 for each line the book shares between squares, and an eye 7.5 out to its corners.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 2, third column from the right: 九ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から三列目「九ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

27 guides, 9 shapes, 48 steps.

## Linked nine eyes · 繋ぎ九つ目 (`tsunagi-kokonotsume`)

Nine squares stood on their corners in three rows of three, their tips meeting. The eight furthest tips lie on the layout circle. Every square has a small square opening, and four broad diamonds of white lie between the squares.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、横に三つずつ三段に並べ、角を触れ合わせます。いちばん外側の八つの角は外円の上に置きます。一つずつ小さな四角い目を白く抜き、正方形の間には大きな菱形の白が四つ残ります。

Measured: The cell spans about 286 by 270 pixels. Adjacent centres are about 95 pixels apart horizontally and 90 vertically. The small white openings are about 24 pixels across their corners. The drawing regularises both intervals to about 53 units, puts the furthest corners on the outer circle and leaves openings 14 units across.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 3, third column from the right: つなぎ九ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から三列目「つなぎ九ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

35 guides, 9 shapes, 56 steps.

## Sixteen eyes · 十六目 (`juroku-me`)

A square stood on its corner, its corners on the layout circle, cut into four by four equal squares by three ruled lines each way, a narrow white line between neighbours, and a tiny square eye in each. The upright and level lines through the centre meet its corners, and every line is ruled full length.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、角を外円の上に置いて、縦横四つずつの同じ大きさの正方形に分けます。分け目は直線を三本ずつ引いて細い白線にし、どの正方形にもごく小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。中心を通る縦と横の直線が角と交わり、直線はどれも端から端まで引きます。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 十六目 (frame 20, right page, row 3, column 2 from the right), sixteen squares stood on their corners four by four, a narrow gap between neighbours, each with a tiny square eye, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: the whole 152 out to its corners from the middle, a square 54 from the next one's middle (36 out to its own corners) so that the gap is a twelfth of the pitch, and an eye 9 across (0.17 of the pitch). The drawing takes the diamond's corners on the layout circle, three lines each way a quarter of the way across, a white line of 2.4 between neighbours, and an eye 4.2 out to its corners.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 20, right page, row 3, second column from the right: 十六目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「十六目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

35 guides, 16 shapes, 70 steps.

## Four eyes on their corners in a circle · 丸に隅立て四つ目 (`maru-ni-sumitate-yotsume`)

The four eyes, stood on their corners, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, their outer corners a narrow white line from the ring.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に、角で立てた四つ目を収め、外側の角を、輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Wada Maru ni Sumitate Yotsume, read along the upright and level lines through the centre as parts of the ring's diameter: the four eyes stand on their corners, the outer corners a narrow white line inside the ring. Its ring and the white inside it are the package's measured ones. The picture's title gives the house, Wada.

25 guides, 5 shapes, 40 steps.

## Four eyes in a circle · 丸に四つ目 (`maru-ni-yotsume`)

Four squares stood on their corners, one above, one below, one to the left and one to the right of the middle, a narrow white line between neighbours, each with a small square eye, inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, their outer corners a narrow white line from it.

角を上下左右に向けた正方形を、中心の上、下、左、右に四つ置き、となり合う正方形の間は細い白線一本分あけ、どれも中央に小さな四角い目を白く抜きます。直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の内側に収め、外側の角を輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 丸ニ四ッ目 (frame 19, left page, row 4, column 3 from the right), four squares stood on their corners, each with a small square eye, inside a ring drawn as two outlines, read as the positions of their corners in the frame's own pixels: the outer outline of the ring 156 across its radius, the squares' centres 68 above, below, left and right of the middle, a square 61 out to its corners, a gap of 11 between neighbours (0.12 of a side) and an eye 31 across its corners (0.26 of the square's). The drawing puts them in the package's medium ring and its white, scaled by 0.95 to fit: centres 40 out, squares 35.5 out to their corners, an eye 9 out.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 19, left page, row 4, third column from the right: 丸ニ四ッ目 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から三列目「丸ニ四ッ目」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

18 guides, 5 shapes, 32 steps.

## Flat four eyes · 平四つ目 (`hira-yotsume`)

Four squares set two by two with their sides upright and level, a square hole in the middle of each. Across the crest it is seven parts: a square is three, its hole one, and the white between neighbours one. Sixteen lines are ruled full length, eight upright and eight level, at 0.5, 1.5, 2.5 and 3.5 parts either side of the centre, and the outer corners stand on the outer circle.

四つの正方形を、辺を縦横にそろえて二つずつ並べ、それぞれの真ん中に四角い穴をあけます。全体の幅を7とすると、正方形の一辺は3、穴の一辺は1、となり合う正方形の間の白は1です。中心から左右・上下に0.5、1.5、2.5、3.5の位置に、縦の直線と横の直線を八本ずつ、端から端まで引き、いちばん外の角は外円の上に置きます。

Measured: The Wikimedia Commons picture Nanatsuwari-hira-yotsume, as numbers in its own units: each square 4150 across, each hole 1380, the white between squares 1382; that is 3, 1 and 1 in seven parts, and the same in ours to a thousandth of the width.

17 guides, 4 shapes, 28 steps.

## Thread ring · 糸輪 (`itowa`)

Two circles about the centre, the outer the layout circle and the inner struck 2.4 inside it, an eightieth of the diameter: a line, not a band.

同じ中心に円を二つ描きます。外側は割付の円、内側はそこから2.4内側の円で、幅は直径の80分の1です。帯ではなく、線のような輪です。

Measured: No picture of a thread ring alone was found, so the ring is the package's own thread weight, 2.4 of the 192 of the layout circle (an eightieth of the diameter), the thinnest of its rings: a line and not a band.

2 guides, 1 shapes, 8 steps.

## Thin ring · 細輪 (`hosowa`)

Two circles about the centre, the outer the layout circle and the inner struck 6.4 inside it, a thirtieth of the diameter.

同じ中心に円を二つ描きます。外側は割付の円、内側はそこから6.4内側の円で、幅は直径の30分の1です。

Measured: The 1892 crest book's 細丸 (frame 18, right page, first row, third column: 細丸に二つ雁金), whose ring is drawn as two lines 8.5 apart on a ring 285.5 across, 0.030 of its diameter; the package's thin ring is a thirtieth (6.4 of the 192).

2 guides, 1 shapes, 8 steps.

## Medium ring · 中輪 (`chuwa`)

Two circles about the centre, the outer the layout circle and the inner struck 16.8 inside it, about a tenth of the diameter.

同じ中心に円を二つ描きます。外側は割付の円、内側はそこから16.8内側の円で、幅は直径の約10分の1です。

Measured: The package's measured medium ring, 0.087 of the diameter at the median of fifteen ringed crests (16.8 of the 192), as docs/provenance.md says under Rings. The 1892 crest book's 中丸 (frame 18, right page, third row, third column: 中丸に三つ雁金) draws its ring as two lines 14.5 apart on a ring 300.5 across, 0.048 of its diameter: narrower than the black ring of the crests as they are drawn today, and 1.6 times its 細丸.

2 guides, 1 shapes, 8 steps.

## Black disc · 石持ち (`kokumochi`)

The layout circle, struck about the centre and filled in black.

割付の円を中心に描き、その中を黒く塗ります。

Measured: A plain disc has one proportion, its size: it is the layout circle itself, 96 either side of the centre, as every unringed crest of the package reaches. No picture sets a different size.

1 guides, 1 shapes, 6 steps.

## Linked rings · 輪違い (`wachigai`)

Two equal rings whose centres are a little over a third of the diameter apart; each passes over the other at one crossing and under it at the other.

同じ大きさの輪を二つ、中心を直径の3分の1強だけ離して重ね、二か所の交点で、一方は上、もう一方は下を通します。

10 guides, 2 shapes, 21 steps.

## Linked-ring cross · 輪違い十 (`wachigai-ju`)

Four bands reach the enclosing thin ring at both ends. One bows up, one down and one towards each side. The layout leans 7 degrees clockwise and leaves a square of white with curved sides in the middle. Each band passes under the next, with a thin gap where they cross.

四本の弧の帯の両端を外側の細い輪まで届かせます。帯は上下左右に一つずつふくらみます。全体を時計回りに7度傾け、中央に辺の曲がった四角い白を残します。どの帯も次の帯の下を通り、交差には細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The printed circular outline is about 304 pixels wide. The two almost-level bands bow away from the horizontal axis by about 57 pixels at the middle and are about 20 pixels thick. The uprights show the same construction. The compass centres are regularised to 111 from the middle, with a 7-degree lean measured from the top and bottom joins. Each band has radius 148 and width 12.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, row 2, fourth column from the right: わちがひ十 (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「わちがひ十」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

6 guides, 5 shapes, 19 steps.

## Linked rings in a circle · 丸に輪違い (`maru-ni-wachigai`)

The linked rings inside a ring about a tenth of the diameter thick, the two rings a narrow white line from it at the sides.

直径の約10分の1の太さの輪の中に輪違いを収め、二つの輪の左右の端を、外の輪から細い白線一本分だけ離します。

Measured: The ringed linked rings of the 1892 crest book (frame 15, 丸に輪ちがひ), read along the level line through the centre: the two rings stand side by side on it and reach a narrow white line from the ring at each side; of the room inside the ring, their centres are 0.36 either side of the middle, their outer edges 0.64 across the radius and their bands 0.13 wide. The book draws them in outline with a thin ring; the ring here is the package's measured one.

Drawn from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, right page, second row, first column counted from the right: 丸に輪ちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、二段目、右から一列目「丸に輪ちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-24.

12 guides, 3 shapes, 27 steps.

## Three linked rings in a thick circle · 太輪に三つ輪違い (`futowa-ni-mitsu-wachigai`)

A thick ring encloses three bands of equal width. Their centres are 23 from the middle, one above and two below. Each passes over its next neighbour at one crossing and under it at the other, with narrow white gaps on both sides of the upper band.

太い輪の中に、同じ太さの輪を三つ収めます。それぞれの中心は全体の中心から23離し、上に一つ、下に二つ置きます。となり合う輪は一方の交差で上、もう一方で下を通ります。上を通る輪の両側に細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The outer ring is about 300 pixels across and 42 thick. The inner three rings are about 121 across with bands roughly 11 thick. Their centres are about 36 from the middle. The drawing uses the package's thick ring and inner rings of radius 39, width 7, with centres 23 from the middle. Each takes the over-under order from the printed intersections.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, right page, row 4, first column from the right: 太ワニ三ッワちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、四段目、右から一列目「太ワニ三ッワちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

8 guides, 4 shapes, 21 steps.

## Three-way split linked rings · 三つ割り輪違い (`mitsu-wari-wachigai`)

Three bowed bands turn about the middle inside a thin ring. Each lies between two circles about a centre 91 from the middle, reaches the ring at both ends and crosses once over and once under its neighbours. Thin white gaps follow the edges at the crossings.

細い輪の中に弧の帯を三つ回して収めます。どの帯も全体の中心から91離れた点を中心とする二つの円で挟み、両端を輪まで届かせます。ほかの二つとは一度上、一度下で交わり、帯の縁に沿って細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The printed ring spans about 294 pixels. One band's curve runs from the two sides of the ring, with a sag of about 70 on a chord about 255 wide. Each band is about 20 pixels thick where it crosses another. The drawing places its compass centres 91 units from the middle and takes the 12-unit bands between radii 98 and 110.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, right page, row 1, first column from the right: 三ッワりわちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、一段目、右から一列目「三ッワりわちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

6 guides, 4 shapes, 19 steps.

## Knotted linked rings · 結び輪違い (`musubi-wachigai`)

One band makes four rounded lobes. A broad diagonal arc joins each lobe to the next, touching the two lobe circles. Each edge of the band follows the same circles at radii 10 apart. A thin white gap parts the lower band from the upper band at each crossing.

一本の帯で丸いふくらみを四つ作ります。斜めの大きな弧がとなり合う二つの円に接し、ふくらみどうしをつなぎます。帯の両側の弧は中心をそろえ、半径を10ずつ離します。交差するところでは、上と下の帯の間に細い白いすき間を残します。

Measured: The four lobes reach about 150 pixels from the middle. A lobe has a round crown about 145 pixels across and a band about 17 pixels wide. The diagonal bridges follow a markedly wider arc. The drawing sets the lobe centres 50 from the middle with radius 46, then joins consecutive lobes with tangent bridge arcs of radius 101. The band's other edge is 10 inside both arcs.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, row 4, fourth column from the right: むすびわちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から四列目「むすびわちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

11 guides, 4 shapes, 24 steps.

## Flower ring · 花輪違い (`hana-wachigai`)

Four compass lenses fill the circle and leave a curved diamond between them. A four-petalled flower sits in the opening, with a short white stamen in each petal and a small round heart.

円の中に弧で囲んだ形を四つ置き、間に曲線の菱形の白を残します。その中に花びらを四枚置き、それぞれに短い白いしべを入れ、小さな丸を中心に置きます。

Measured: Four lens-shaped panels meet at the upright and level tips of a circle. The open shape between them curves inwards. Within it is a four-petalled flower about 150 pixels across, a little under half the outside diameter. Its small round heart and four spokes are on the axes.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, row 1, fourth column from the right: 花わちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、一段目、右から四列目「花わちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

14 guides, 9 shapes, 31 steps.

## Flower lozenge ring · 菱輪違い (`hishi-wachigai`)

Four ruled sides make the lozenge. Each side and an inward compass arc make one panel. In the open curved lozenge a flower of four petals surrounds a round heart.

直線四本で菱の外側を作ります。各辺と内側に曲がる弧で、一つずつ形を作ります。曲線の菱形の白の中に、丸い中心を囲んで花びらを四枚置きます。

Measured: The outer lozenge is about 290 pixels wide and 224 high. Its four straight sides have one concave arc on the inside. A four-petalled flower about 120 pixels across stands between those arcs. In the drawing the outer points are kept level and the straight sides are ruled in pairs.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 14, left page, row 4, third column from the right: ひしワちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (左頁、四段目、右から三列目「ひしワちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 9 shapes, 35 steps.

## Three linked flower rings · 三つ花輪違い (`mitsu-hana-wachigai`)

Three circles stand one above two. Four curved panels surround the flower in each circle. The black panels alternate with white where they overlap, and all three flowers stand upright.

円を上に一つ、下に二つ置きます。それぞれ花のまわりを弧で囲んだ四つの形に分けます。形が重なるところでは黒と白を交互にし、三つの花はいずれも上に向けます。

Measured: Three equal circles overlap, one above and two below. Each circle is about 170 pixels across, and the two lower centres are about 167 pixels apart. A four-petalled flower stands in the middle of each circle, inside a diamond edged with four arcs. The drawing keeps the three flowers apart.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 15, right page, row 3, first column from the right: 三ッ花わちがひ (measured, and drawn with compass and ruler) (右頁、三段目、右から一列目「三ッ花わちがひ」（寸法を測り、コンパスと定規で描いたもの）); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

68 guides, 16 shapes, 100 steps.

## Character five in ring-drum form · 五の字輪鼓 (`go-no-ji-ringo`)

Two rounded horizontal caps sit above and below two crossed bands. The bands meet the caps on four short uprights, leaving a pointed opening above and below the join.

両端を丸くした横棒二本の間に、帯二本を交差させます。四本の短い縦線で帯と横棒をつなぎ、交差の上下に先のとがった空きを残します。

Measured: The printed outline spans about 242 pixels and rises about 269. A horizontal cap is about 40 pixels thick and the left and right uprights are about 165 pixels apart. The drawing uses a span of 135.2, height 149.2, caps 23.2 thick and upright outside edges 92 apart. The two horizontal caps reflect across the centre, and the ruling regularises the two crossing bands.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 13, left page, second row, fourth column from the right: 五の字りんこ (左頁、二段目、右から四列目「五の字りんこ」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

17 guides, 1 shapes, 23 steps.

## Character hon in a circle · 丸に本の字 (`maru-ni-hon-no-ji`)

The centre stroke of 本 is upright, with a curved head and a hooked foot. The long bar crosses above two slanting strokes. The short lower bar joins the upright.

「本」の縦画は、頭を曲線にして下端をはねさせます。長い横画の下から斜めの二画を広げ、短い下の横画を縦画につなげます。

Measured: The ring is about 305 pixels wide. The upper bar is about 220 pixels across and the printed brush character is about 246 pixels tall. The drawing uses about 137 by 153 and the package medium ring. The asymmetric brush feet, rising long bar and lower hook are ruled and bounded by circular arcs.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 30, right page, first row, third column from the right: 丸ニ本の字 (右頁、一段目、右から三列目「丸ニ本の字」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

30 guides, 2 shapes, 56 steps.

## Character mountain in a thin circle · 細輪に山の字 (`hosowa-ni-yama-no-ji`)

The centre point of 山 rises above two curved shoulders. Below the three points, two broad arcs form a bowl with a rounded opening. A narrow ring encloses the character.

「山」の中央の先が二つの丸い肩より高く立ちます。三つの先の下は大きな弧で丸く囲み、内側に丸い空きを残します。細い輪で字を囲みます。

Measured: The crest book's ring is about 319 pixels across. The outer shoulders span about 270 pixels and the opening in the bowl about 94 pixels. The drawing uses 164 and 58 on a ring of diameter 192. The centre point is straight-sided above the broad neck and the sides reflect about the upright axis. The ring follows the printed qualifier 細ワ.

Measured from 伊呂波分新撰紋帳 (Iroha-wake shinsen monchō (a new crest book in syllabary order), ed. Inoue Katsugorō, Kunshidō, 1892), [NDL 780550](https://dl.ndl.go.jp/pid/780550), frame 29, right page, third row, second column from the right: 細ワニ山の字 (右頁、三段目、右から二列目「細ワニ山の字」); public domain (保護期間満了); read 2026-11-26.

16 guides, 2 shapes, 31 steps.
